# <span class="deva-large">क</span> <span class="iast-large">ka</span>

<div class="dict-page-header">
  <span class="badge-count">1514 entrées</span>
  <a href="../" class="back-link">← Index du dictionnaire</a>
</div>

---

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--ka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰क</span> <i>-ka</i> forme des agt. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ifc. après s. de parenté en <i>ṛ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> diminutif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_lp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कॢप्</span> <i>kḷp</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(kalpate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kḷpta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> être arrangé, réussir, convenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> servir de, participer à, contribuer à, devenir ⟨dat.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être adapté à, s'accorder avec ⟨i.⟩; être bon pour ⟨dat. loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aider à, procurer; disposer, préparer, prescrire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> produire, créer; déclarer comme, considérer comme ⟨2 acc.⟩ — ca. <i>(kalpayati)</i> ca. md. <i>(kalpayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kalpita</i><i>)</i> faire; ordonner, nommer (à une fonction).<br> <i>idaṃ</i> <i>me</i> <i>iṣṭasiddhaye</i> <i>kalpeta</i> ceci me convient.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_lpta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कॢप्त</span> <i>kḷpta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_lp" class="skt-link">kḷp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_lpta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_lpta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_lptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kḷptā</i> arrangé, préparé; prêt; en ordre de marche; complet, fini; exact, parfait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à portée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> produit, créé; inventé; fixé, décidé, prescrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृष्</span> <i>kṛṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(kṛṣati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(karṣati)</i> pft. <i>(cakaṣa)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(acīkṛṣat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>kṛṣṭvā</i>, <i>-kṛṣya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> traîner, entraîner, tirer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> labourer — ca. <i>(karṣayati)</i> extraire, arracher; tourmenter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृष्ण</span> <i>kṛṣṇa</i> a. <a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛṣṇā</i> noir, bleu-noir — <a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> couleur noire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kṛṣṇa «le Noir», divinité multiforme; à l'époque védique, <a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> représentant les peuples dravidiens, il est de couleur noire; 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, il serait né à <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a> du prince <a href="#entry-Uvasudeva" class="skt-link">Vasudeva</a> de la lignée <a href="#entry-yaadava" class="skt-link">yādava</a> et de <a href="#entry-Udevakii" class="skt-link">Devakī</a>; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] le cousin de <a href="#entry-Udevakii" class="skt-link">Devakī</a>, le cruel <a href="#entry-Uka_msa" class="skt-link">Kaṃsa</a> roi de <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a>, sachant qu'un fils de celle ci le tuerait, fit tuer ses 6 premiers enfants; le 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩, <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a>, s'échappa, et le 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩, <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, fut élevé en secret par le roi des bergers <a href="#entry-Unanda" class="skt-link">Nanda</a> et sa femme <a href="#entry-Uyazodaa" class="skt-link">Yaśodā</a>; [BhP.] bébé espiègle, il volait le beurre de <a href="#entry-Uyazodaa" class="skt-link">Yaśodā</a> et était d'une force herculéenne; enfant, il vainquit les démons <a href="#entry-Ut_r_naavarta" class="skt-link">Tṛṇāvarta</a>, <a href="#entry-Uagha" class="skt-link">Agha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uari_s_ta" class="skt-link">Ariṣṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukaaliya" class="skt-link">Kāliya</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukezii" class="skt-link">Keśī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubaka" class="skt-link">Baka</a> et <a href="#entry-Uzaka_ta" class="skt-link">Śakaṭa</a>; il sauva de l'inondation provoquée par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> le village de <a href="#entry-Ugovardhana" class="skt-link">Govardhana</a> en élevant sa colline au-dessus de sa tête; il courtisa des milliers de bergères [<a href="#entry-gopii" class="skt-link">gopī</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uv_rndaavana" class="skt-link">Vṛndāvana</a>, parmi lesquelles ses favorites étaient <a href="#entry-Uraadhaa" class="skt-link">Rādhā</a> et <a href="#entry-Urukmi_nii" class="skt-link">Rukmiṇī</a>; il détrôna et tua le roi <a href="#entry-Uka_msa" class="skt-link">Kaṃsa</a>; son maître fut le sage <a href="#entry-Usaandiipani" class="skt-link">Sāndīpani</a>; devenu roi de <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a>, il épousa <a href="#entry-Urukmi_nii" class="skt-link">Rukmiṇī</a> en l'enlevant au mauvais <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a>; il est père de <a href="#entry-Upradyumna" class="skt-link">Pradyumna</a>; il combattit <a href="#entry-Ujaambavaan" class="skt-link">Jāmbavān</a> auquel il prit le joyau <a href="#entry-Usyamantaka" class="skt-link">Syamantaka</a>; il épousa <a href="#entry-Ujaambavatii" class="skt-link">Jāmbavatī</a> qui conçut de lui <a href="#entry-Usaamba" class="skt-link">Sāmba</a>; il épousa <a href="#entry-Usatyabhaamaa" class="skt-link">Satyabhāmā</a> dont il eut 10 fils, et qui l'aida à vaincre l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Unaraka" class="skt-link">Naraka</a> et son allié <a href="#entry-Umura" class="skt-link">Mura</a>; il donna sa sœur <a href="#entry-Usubhadraa" class="skt-link">Subhadrā</a> en mariage à <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] il participa avec <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> à la bataille du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mahābhārata</a> au côté des <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>; au début de la bataille, cocher d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>, il l'exhorta à accomplir son devoir [<a href="#entry-karmayoga" class="skt-link">karmayoga</a>] en lui révélant sa nature divine dans la <a href="#entry-Ubhagavadgiitaa" class="skt-link">Bhagavadgītā</a>; mais il ne prit pas part lui-même à la guerre, assurant par sa neutralité l'équité du combat; à la mort de son frère <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a>, dernier survivant des <a href="#entry-yaadavaas" class="skt-link">yādavās</a>, il méditait sur la tristesse de l'existence, lorsqu'il fut tué par la flèche du chasseur <a href="#entry-Ujaraa_h" class="skt-link">Jarāḥ</a> qui l'avait pris pour un daim, et qui l'atteint au talon, seul endroit vulnérable de son corps; de cette blessure sortit à sa mort un <a href="#entry-zaalagraama" class="skt-link">śālagrāma</a>; on célèbre son anniversaire lors de la fête <a href="#entry-janmaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">janmāṣṭamī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uacyuta" class="skt-link">Acyuta</a>, <a href="#entry-Uadhok_saja" class="skt-link">Adhokṣaja</a>, <a href="#entry-Uarisuudana" class="skt-link">Arisūdana</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugiridhara" class="skt-link">Giridhara</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugopa" class="skt-link">Gopa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugopaala" class="skt-link">Gopāla</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugopiinaatha" class="skt-link">Gopīnātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugopendra" class="skt-link">Gopendra</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugovinda" class="skt-link">Govinda</a>, <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Upaarthasaarathi" class="skt-link">Pārthasārathi</a>, <a href="#entry-Umadhudvi_t" class="skt-link">Madhudviṭ</a>, <a href="#entry-Umadhusuudana" class="skt-link">Madhusūdana</a>, <a href="#entry-Umaadhava" class="skt-link">Mādhava</a>, <a href="#entry-Umitravindaa" class="skt-link">Mitravindā</a>, <a href="#entry-Umuraari" class="skt-link">Murāri</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaasudeva" class="skt-link">Vāsudeva</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvi_t_thala" class="skt-link">Viṭṭhala</a>, <a href="#entry-Uh_r_siikeza" class="skt-link">Hṛṣīkeśa</a>, <a href="#entry-v_r_s_ni" class="skt-link">vṛṣṇi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>kṛṣṇau</i> myth. <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>, figurant Dieu et l'Homme ensemble <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du roi <a href="#entry-Uraa_s_trakuu_ta" class="skt-link">Rāṣṭrakūṭa</a> Kṛṣṇa_3 (10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il succéda à son père Amogavarṣa_3 à sa mort en 939; il envahit le pays <a href="#entry-Ucola" class="skt-link">Cola</a> en 949, et conquit <a href="#entry-Ukaa_ncii" class="skt-link">Kāñcī</a>; son demi-frère Khottiga lui succéda en 967; il encouragea la culture sanskrite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a> <a href="#entry-Ubhaaratii" class="skt-link">Bhāratī</a> Kṛṣṇa_4 Tīrthajī <a href="#entry-Umahaaraaja" class="skt-link">Mahārāja</a>, <a href="#entry-zafkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">śaṃkarācārya</a> du <a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a> de <a href="#entry-Ugovardhana_3" class="skt-link">Govardhana_3</a> à <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a> (1884–1960); il est l'auteur d'une méthode de calcul mental appelée «mathématiques védiques» — <a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> obscurité; noirceur — <a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> poivre noir; fer; taches noires de la Lune, etc. — <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛṣṇā</i> myth. np. de Kṛṣṇā, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udraupadii" class="skt-link">Draupadī</a> «la Noire» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. c'est l'une des 7 langues d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> [<a href="#entry-saptajihva" class="skt-link">saptajihva</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de la rivière Kṛṣṇā_1, qui traverse le pays <a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a> — <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛṣṇī</i> nuit.<br> <i>kṛṣṇastu</i> <i>bhagavān</i> <i>svayam</i> [BhP.] <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> est Dieu en personne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_naa_s_tamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇāṣṭamī</i> [<a href="#entry-a_s_tamii" class="skt-link">aṣṭamī</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. anniversaire de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-k_r_s_najanmaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇajanmāṣṭamī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine sombre d'un mois quelconque.<br> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_naajina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇājina</i> [<a href="#entry-ajina" class="skt-link">ajina</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naajina" class="skt-link">n.</a> peau d'antilope noire (qui sert de vêtement et de siège à un ascète) — <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naajina" class="skt-link">m.</a> couvert d'une peau d'antilope noire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_naayas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇāyas</i> [<a href="#entry-ayas" class="skt-link">ayas</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naayas" class="skt-link">n.</a> fer; syn. <a href="#entry-zyaamaayas" class="skt-link">śyāmāyas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇadeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de l'empereur Kṛṣṇadeva <a href="#entry-Uraaya" class="skt-link">Rāya</a>, qui régna sur <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a> de 1509 à 1529, succédant à son frère aîné <a href="#entry-Uviira_1" class="skt-link">Vīra_1</a> <a href="#entry-Unarasi_mha_6" class="skt-link">Narasiṃha_6</a>; son règne est l'apogée de cet empire du <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_ta" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭa</a>; il vainquit le Sultan de Bahmani à Bīdar, et le réinstalla comme vassal, en se donnant le titre d'Établisseur du roi des Musulmans; il s'allia avec les portugais contre le Sultan de Bijapur; il défit le Sultan de Golconde, et reprit le Telangana aux rois <a href="#entry-Ugajapati" class="skt-link">Gajapati</a>; il prit la citadelle d'<a href="#entry-Uudayagiri" class="skt-link">Udayagiri</a> en 1513 après un siège de 18 mois; il prit le fort de <a href="#entry-Uraayacuuru" class="skt-link">Rāyacūru</a> (mod. Raichur, Karnataka), conquit son doab en 1520, et rasa la forteresse de Gulburga; on dit que tous les matins après son massage, il pratiquait les haltères, l'épée et la lutte, puis montait son cheval avant de prendre son bain; il encouragea les arts à sa court, not. la littérature sanskrite, kannada et telugu; il patronna not. une cour de 8 poètes [<a href="#entry-a_s_tadiggaja" class="skt-link">aṣṭadiggaja</a>]; <a href="#entry-Uvyaasatiirtha" class="skt-link">Vyāsatīrtha</a> était son <a href="#entry-raajaguru" class="skt-link">rājaguru</a>; il avait les titres de Kāvyanāṭakamarmajña «Expert en poésie et art dramatique», Kavitāprāvīṇyaphaṇīśa «<a href="#entry-Upata_njali" class="skt-link">Patañjali</a> de virtuosité poétique» et Sakalakalābhoja «Expert en tous les arts à l'instar du Roi <a href="#entry-Ubhoja_1" class="skt-link">Bhoja_1</a>»; il composa le drame <a href="#entry-Ujaambavatiipari_naya" class="skt-link">Jāmbavatīpariṇaya</a> en sanskrit et le poème Āmuktamālyada en télugu; il fit don du village de Mattur à une communauté védique, qui a gardé le sanskrit comme langue vernaculaire jusqu'à ce jour; il construisit un nouveau <a href="#entry-gopura" class="skt-link">gopura</a> au temple de <a href="#entry-Uviruupaak_sa" class="skt-link">Virūpākṣa</a>; en 1513 il érigea le temple de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_nasvaamii" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇasvāmī</a>, pour servir de sanctuaire à l'image de <a href="#entry-Ubaalak_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Bālakṛṣṇa</a> qu'il avait rapportée d'<a href="#entry-Uudayagiri" class="skt-link">Udayagiri</a>; en 1516 il construisit le Rājagopura du temple <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a> <a href="#entry-Ukaalahastiizvara" class="skt-link">Kālahastīśvara</a>; son frère <a href="#entry-Uacyutadeva" class="skt-link">Acyutadeva</a> <a href="#entry-Uraaya" class="skt-link">Rāya</a> lui succéda.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nagiitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇagītā</i> [<a href="#entry-giitaa" class="skt-link">gītā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nagiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. texte où <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> enseigne à <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>; se dit notamment de la triade <a href="#entry-Ubhagavadgiitaa" class="skt-link">Bhagavadgītā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uanugiitaa" class="skt-link">Anugītā</a> et <a href="#entry-Uuttaragiitaa" class="skt-link">Uttaragītā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_najanmaa_s_tamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇajanmāṣṭamī</i> [<a href="#entry-janmaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">janmāṣṭamī</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_najanmaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. jour anniversaire de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine sombre du mois <a href="#entry-zraava_na_2" class="skt-link">śrāvaṇa_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nakaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇakānta</i> hist. np. du logicien Kṛṣṇakānta Vidyāvāgiśa (18⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩-19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); on lui doit la Kṛṣṇakāntīṭīkā, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Uzabdazaktiprakaazikaa" class="skt-link">Śabdaśaktiprakāśikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृष्णल</span> <i>kṛṣṇala</i> [<a href="#entry-k_r_s_na" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇa</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nala" class="skt-link">n.</a> baie noire de l'Abrus Precatorius employée comme mesure de poids.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_naliilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇalīlā</i> [<a href="#entry-liilaa" class="skt-link">līlā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naliilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. jeu ou ébat érotique de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_naliilaazuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇalīlāśuka</i> [<a href="#entry-zuka" class="skt-link">śuka</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naliilaazuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du poète Kṛṣṇalīlāśuka «Perroquet de jeu de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>», qui vivait au <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a> au 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.<br> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naliilaa" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇalīlā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nama_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇamaṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nama_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nama_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du temple Kṛṣṇamaṭha ou <a href="#entry-Uzriik_r_s_nama_tha" class="skt-link">Śrīkṛṣṇamaṭha</a>, fondé par <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a> à <a href="#entry-Uu_dupii" class="skt-link">Uḍupī</a>, au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_namitraacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇamitrācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-mitra" class="skt-link">mitra</a>-<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_namitraacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe Kṛṣṇamitrācārya (début 19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); cf. <a href="#entry-Udurbalaacaarya" class="skt-link">Durbalācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_namizra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇamiśra</i> [<a href="#entry-mizra" class="skt-link">miśra</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_namizra" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kṛṣṇamiśra, auteur du drame allégorique <a href="#entry-Uprabodhacandrodaya" class="skt-link">Prabodhacandrodaya</a> (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_napak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇapakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-pak_sa" class="skt-link">pakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_napak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. «quinzaine sombre», période (défavorable) entre une pleine lune [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] et une nouvelle lune [<a href="#entry-amaavaasyaa" class="skt-link">amāvāsyā</a>]; opp. <a href="#entry-zuklapak_sa" class="skt-link">śuklapakṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_napratipad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇapratipad</i> [<a href="#entry-pratipad_2" class="skt-link">pratipad_2</a>] astr. jour [<a href="#entry-tithi" class="skt-link">tithi</a>] suivant une pleine lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_naraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_naraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Kṛṣṇarāja <a href="#entry-Ukalacuri" class="skt-link">Kalacuri</a> de <a href="#entry-Umaahi_smatii" class="skt-link">Māhiṣmatī</a>; il régna vers (550–575) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du roi Kṛṣṇarāja_1 Wodeyar III, <a href="#entry-Umahaaraaja" class="skt-link">Mahārāja</a> de Mysore (1794–1868); il était lettré et patronnait les arts; il commandita le <a href="#entry-Uzriitattvanidhi" class="skt-link">Śrītattvanidhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nasarpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇasarpa</i> [<a href="#entry-sarpa" class="skt-link">sarpa</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nasarpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. [«serpent noir»] zoo. <i>Coluber Naga</i>, cobra très venimeux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nasarpavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇasarpavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nasarpavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nasarpavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nasarpavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛṣṇasarpavatī</i> (venimeux, mortel) comme le cobra.<br> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nasarpa" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇasarpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nasvaamin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇasvāmin</i> [<a href="#entry-svaamin" class="skt-link">svāmin</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nasvaamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du temple de Kṛṣṇasvāmī à <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a>; il fut érigé par <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_nadeva" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇadeva</a> <a href="#entry-Uraaya" class="skt-link">Rāya</a> en 1513, pour servir de sanctuaire à l'image de <a href="#entry-Ubaalak_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Bālakṛṣṇa</a> qu'il avait rapportée d'<a href="#entry-Uudayagiri" class="skt-link">Udayagiri</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> noirceur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. phase décroissante de la Lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_natva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_natva" class="skt-link">n.</a> noirceur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. la nature de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_navartman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇavartman</i> [<a href="#entry-vartman" class="skt-link">vartman</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_navartman" class="skt-link">m.</a> feu [«qui noircit son passage»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_nayajurveda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṇayajurveda</i> [<a href="#entry-yajurveda" class="skt-link">yajurveda</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nayajurveda" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Kṛṣṇayajurveda ou <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> noir; cf. <a href="#entry-Utaittiriiya" class="skt-link">Taittirīya</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukaa_thaka" class="skt-link">Kāṭhaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृष्ट</span> <i>kṛṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_r_s" class="skt-link">kṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛṣṭā</i> traîné, entraîné, tiré; arraché <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cultivé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_s_taja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣṭaja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_r_s_taja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_taja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_tajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛṣṭajā</i> cultivé (plante).<br> <a href="#entry-k_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">kṛṣṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृष</span> <i>kṛṣa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_r_s" class="skt-link">kṛṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> charrue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृषक</span> <i>kṛṣaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_r_s" class="skt-link">kṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_r_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_r_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛṣikā</i> qui laboure, qui cultive — <a href="#entry-k_r_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> laboureur; fermier, cultivateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> charrue; bœuf — <a href="#entry-k_r_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛṣikā</i> labourage, cultivation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_si">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृषि</span> <i>kṛṣi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_r_s" class="skt-link">kṛṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_si" class="skt-link">f.</a> agriculture, labourage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kṛṣī</i> champ.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_siibala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣībala</i> [<a href="#entry-bala" class="skt-link">bala</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_siibala" class="skt-link">m.</a> laboureur, paysan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kṛṣīvala</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-k_r_si" class="skt-link">kṛṣi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_siparaazara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛṣiparāśara</i> [<a href="#entry-paraazara" class="skt-link">parāśara</a>] <a href="#entry-k_r_siparaazara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Kṛṣiparāśara, manuel d'agriculture attribué à <a href="#entry-Uparaazara" class="skt-link">Parāśara</a>; aussi appelé Kṛṣisaṃgraha, Kṛṣipaddhati et Kṛṣitantra.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृ</span> <i>kṛ_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(kṛṇoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(kṛṇute)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(karoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(kurute)</i> imp. <i>(kuru)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>kariṣyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(cakāra)</i> pft. md. <i>(cakre)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] <i>(akārṣīt)</i> aor. md. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <i>(akṛta)</i> cond. <i>(akariṣyat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṛta</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(kurvat)</i> ppr. ps. <i>(kriyamāṇa)</i> ppft. <i>(cakṛvas)</i> inf. <i>(kartum)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>kārya</i>, <i>karaṇīya</i>, <i>kartavya</i>, <i>kṛtya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>kṛtvā</i>, <i>-kṛtya</i>, <i>kāram</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>adhi</i>, <i>anu</i>, <i>apa</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>tiras</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>puras</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>bahis</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> faire; accomplir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> produire, créer, préparer, fabriquer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rendre ⟨acc.⟩ tel à ⟨acc.⟩ — ps. <i>(kriyate)</i> être fait, être accompli, être créé — ca. <i>(kārayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kārita</i><i>)</i> faire faire, faire exécuter — dés. <i>(cikīrṣati)</i> dés. md. <i>(cikīrṣate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>cikīrṣita</i><i>)</i> vouloir faire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>creare</i>; fr. créer.<br> <i>mā</i> <i>kārṣīḥ</i> inj. ne fais pas.<br> <i>mā</i> <i>kṛthāḥ</i> inj. ne fais pas.<br> <i>kālaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> mourir [«avoir accompli son temps»].<br> <i>ciraṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> traîner, être en retard; être lent.<br> <i>daṇḍaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> infliger un châtiment, punir.<br> <i>pūjaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> honorer.<br> <i>manasā</i> <i>kṛ</i> penser, considérer.<br> <i>rājyaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> régner.<br> <i>vaśīkṛ</i> soumettre à sa volonté.<br> <i>sakhyaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> lier amitié.<br> <i>padaṃ</i> <i>kurute</i> il fait un pas; il pose le pied.<br> <i>yathecchaṃ</i> <i>kuru</i> fais comme il te plaît.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_r_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृ</span> <i>kṛ_2</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(akārīt)</i> int. <i>(carkarti)</i> mentionner, louer, célébrer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rcchra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृच्छ्र</span> <i>kṛcchra</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rz" class="skt-link">kṛś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rcchra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rcchra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rcchraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛcchrā</i> difficile, fâcheux, dangereux; mauvais (maladie) — <a href="#entry-k_rcchra" class="skt-link">n.</a> difficulté, besoin, danger — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(kṛcchrāyate)</i> éprouver des difficultés <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rendre difficile.<br> <i>kṛcchreṇa</i> var. <i>kṛcchrāt</i> adv. avec difficulté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rcchraadavaapta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛcchrādavāpta</i> [<a href="#entry-avaapta" class="skt-link">avāpta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rcchraadavaapta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rcchraadavaapta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rcchraadavaaptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛcchrādavāptā</i> obtenu avec peine.<br> <a href="#entry-k_rcchraat" class="skt-link">kṛcchrāt</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rcchraat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृच्छ्रात्</span> <i>kṛcchrāt</i> [abl. <a href="#entry-k_rcchra" class="skt-link">kṛcchra</a>] iic. ⟨ppp.⟩ avec difficulté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rdanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛdanta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rdanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. dérivé primaire.<br> <i>bhūte</i> <i>kṛdanta</i> gram. participe passé passif; il s'accorde avec l'actant unique d'un verbe intransitif [<a href="#entry-akarmaka" class="skt-link">akarmaka</a>] et avec le patient d'un verbe transitif [<a href="#entry-sakarmaka" class="skt-link">sakarmaka</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rdantaruupamaalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛdantarūpamālā</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a>-<a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">mālā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rdantaruupamaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. liste des formes déclinées de dérivés primaires.<br> <a href="#entry-k_rdanta" class="skt-link">kṛdanta</a> <br> <a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rka_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृकण</span> <i>kṛkaṇa</i> <a href="#entry-k_rka_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. perdrix; faisan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. d'une localité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rmi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृमि</span> <i>kṛmi</i> [<a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rmi" class="skt-link">m.</a> insecte; ver, vermine, asticot; araignée, pou; ver à soie; fourmi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> carmin (produit par la cochenille) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>vermi</i>; fr. vermine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rmija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛmija</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rmija" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rmija" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rmijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛmijā</i> produit par un insecte; not. soie — <a href="#entry-k_rmijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛmijā</i> teinture carmin (produite par la cochenille) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. κερμεσ; lat. <i>carminium</i>; fr. carmin; ang. crimson; arabe qirmiz.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rmiparis_rpta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛmiparisṛpta</i> [<a href="#entry-paris_rpta" class="skt-link">parisṛpta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rmiparis_rpta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rmiparis_rpta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rmiparis_rpta" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛmiparisṛpta</i> couvert de vermine grouillante.<br> <a href="#entry-k_rmi" class="skt-link">kṛmi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृप्</span> <i>kṛp</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. md. <i>(kṛpate)</i> véd. se plaindre, se lamenter; avoir de la peine — ca. <i>(kṛpayati)</i> plaindre, avoir pitié de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>crepo</i>.<br> <i>kṛpayā</i> adr. s'il vous plaît, je vous en prie; pitié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृप</span> <i>kṛpa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_rp" class="skt-link">kṛp</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Kṛpa «Pitié», issu du sage <a href="#entry-Uzaradvaan" class="skt-link">Śaradvān</a>; il fut trouvé avec sa sœur dans une touffe de roseau [<a href="#entry-zarastamba" class="skt-link">śarastamba</a>] par le roi <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāṃtanu</a> qui les recueillit par compassion [<a href="#entry-k_rpaa" class="skt-link">kṛpā</a>]; plus tard il fit partie du conseil de <a href="#entry-Uhastinaapura" class="skt-link">Hastināpura</a>, et comme expert d'archerie fut maître d'armes des <a href="#entry-Ukauravaas" class="skt-link">Kauravās</a>; aussi appelé Kṛpācārya; c'est l'un des 7 immortels [<a href="#entry-cira_njiivin" class="skt-link">ciraṃjīvin</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du brâhmane <a href="#entry-Uk_rpa" class="skt-link">Kṛpa</a> <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Uzaradvaan" class="skt-link">Śaradvān</a> (et donc grand-père du précédent) — <a href="#entry-k_rpii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛpī</i> myth. np. de Kṛpī «Compassion», jumelle de <a href="#entry-Uk_rpa" class="skt-link">Kṛpa</a>; elle épousa <a href="#entry-Udro_na" class="skt-link">Droṇa</a>, dont elle conçut <a href="#entry-Uazvatthaamaa" class="skt-link">Aśvatthāmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rpa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृपण</span> <i>kṛpaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_rp" class="skt-link">kṛp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rpa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rpa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rpa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛpaṇā</i> malheureux, misérable; pauvre, faible, lamentable — <a href="#entry-k_rpa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> misère.<br> <i>kṛpaṇam</i> adv. d'une façon lamentable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rpaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृपा</span> <i>kṛpā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_rp" class="skt-link">kṛp</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rpaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> compassion, pitié, tendresse.<br> <i>kṛpayā</i> adr. s'il vous plaît.<br> <i>guru</i> <i>kṛpā</i> <i>hi</i> <i>kevalam</i> Le maître n'est que compassion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कॄ</span> <i>kṝ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(kirati)</i> pft. <i>(cakāra)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kīrṇa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ati</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> lancer, jeter, répandre, arroser; disperser, éparpiller; joncher, couvrir de ⟨i.⟩; remplir — ps. <i>(kīryate)</i> être répandu, être dispersé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rt_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्</span> <i>kṛt_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(kṛntati)</i> pft. <i>(cakarta)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(akartīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṛtta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> couper, diviser, découper <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> retrancher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (au fig.) détruire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>culter</i>; fr. couteau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rt_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्</span> <i>kṛt_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a fait, qui a accompli, qui a exécuté; auteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. suffixe [<a href="#entry-pratyaya" class="skt-link">pratyaya</a>] primaire (forme des substantifs, participes et indéclinables à partir de la racine); opp. <a href="#entry-taddhita" class="skt-link">taddhita</a>.<br> <i>sukṛt</i> qui a bien agi.<br> <i>karmakṛt</i> qui a fait le travail.<br> <i>pāpakṛt</i> qui a commis un péché.<br> <i>mantrakṛt</i> à qui un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> a été révélé.<br> <i>puṇyakṛt</i> qui a fait une bonne action.<br> <i>karmakṛt</i> qui a fait le travail.<br> <i>śāstrakṛt</i> qui a fait des traités.<br> <i>bhāṣyakṛt</i> qui a fait un commentaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत</span> <i>kṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtā</i> fait, accompli; bien fait, parfait — iic. qui a fait; accompli en — ifc. qui est l'objet de, devenu, rendu tel à — <a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait, action, acte; bonne action, bienfait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> coup de dé (maximum) de 4 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ âge [<a href="#entry-yuga" class="skt-link">yuga</a>] du monde, âge d'or.<br> <i>kṛtam</i> adv. assez de ⟨i.⟩, que cesse, ne pas ...<br> <i>kṛtaṃ</i> <i>saṃdehena</i> assez d'hésitation.<br> <i>kṛtametādṛśena</i> <i>asaṃgatena</i> <i>pralāpena</i> arrête de parler de cette manière absurde.<br> <i>kṛte</i> var. <i>kṛtena</i> ind. pour; pour le compte de; à cause de ⟨g. iic.⟩.<br> <i>mama</i> <i>kṛte</i> pour mon compte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtaak_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtākṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_rta" class="skt-link">akṛta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtaak_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaak_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaak_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtākṛtā</i> fait et défait; à moitié fait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> optionnel, indifférent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-astra" class="skt-link">astra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtaastra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaastraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtāstrā</i> adroit à l'arc; exercé au maniement des armes.<br> <a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">kṛta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtaa_njali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtāñjali</i> [<a href="#entry-a_njali" class="skt-link">añjali</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtaa_njali" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaa_njali" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaa_njali" class="skt-link">f.</a> en posture d'<a href="#entry-a_njali" class="skt-link">añjali</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtaghna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtaghna</i> [<a href="#entry-ghna" class="skt-link">ghna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtaghna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaghna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaghnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtaghnā</i> [«qui détruit ce qui a été fait»] qui oublie le bienfait, ingrat; opp. <a href="#entry-k_rtaj_na" class="skt-link">kṛtajña</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtajña</i> [<a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaj_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaj_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtajñā</i> [«qui sait ce qui a été fait»] reconnaissant; opp. <a href="#entry-k_rtaghna" class="skt-link">kṛtaghna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtak_rtya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtakṛtya</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rtya" class="skt-link">kṛtya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtak_rtya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtak_rtya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtak_rtyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtakṛtyā</i> qui a accompli sa tâche, satisfait — <a href="#entry-k_rtak_rtya" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [«atteinte du but»] sérénité; libération; sentiment d'avoir accompli tout ce qui devait être accompli <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ce qui est fait et ce qui reste à faire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृतक</span> <i>kṛtaka</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">kṛta</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtakā</i> fabriqué, artificiel, factice; non-naturel, adoptif.<br> <i>kṛtakakalahaṃ</i> <i>kṛtvā</i> ayant simulé une querelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtakarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtakarman</i> [<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtakarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtakarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtakarma_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtakarmaṇī</i> qui a accompli les rites; qui a fait son devoir — <a href="#entry-k_rtakarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> acte accompli.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtakrudha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtakrudha</i> [<a href="#entry-krudhaa" class="skt-link">krudhā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtakrudha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtakrudha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtakrudhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtakrudhā</i> en colère, fâché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtamaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtamāla</i> [<a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">mālā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rtamaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. antilope tachetée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Cassia fistula</i>, arbre aux feuilles composées, aux jolies fleurs jaunes en grappes pendantes, utilisé en médecine; syn. <a href="#entry-aruja" class="skt-link">aruja</a>, <a href="#entry-aaragvadha" class="skt-link">āragvadha</a>, <a href="#entry-ja_tharanud" class="skt-link">jaṭharanud</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtaprayatna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtaprayatna</i> [<a href="#entry-prayatna" class="skt-link">prayatna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtaprayatna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaprayatna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtaprayatnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtaprayatnā</i> à qui l'on a consacré des efforts.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtavarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtavarman</i> [<a href="#entry-varman" class="skt-link">varman</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rtavarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Kṛtavarmā «Protecteur du Bien», prince [<a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a>] ami de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> qui s'engagea à la guerre du côté de <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a>; il sera le seul survivant du camp des <a href="#entry-Ukauravaas" class="skt-link">Kauravās</a>, avec les brâhmanes <a href="#entry-Uk_rpa" class="skt-link">Kṛpa</a> et <a href="#entry-Uazvatthaamaa" class="skt-link">Aśvatthāmā</a>; 36 ans plus tard, il se battit avec <a href="#entry-Uyuyudhaana" class="skt-link">Yuyudhāna</a> dans la bataille d'ivrognes fratricide qui mit fin à la lignée <a href="#entry-yaadava" class="skt-link">yādava</a>, réalisant la malédiction de <a href="#entry-Ugaandhaarii" class="skt-link">Gāndhārī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhoja" class="skt-link">Bhoja</a>, <a href="#entry-Uakruura" class="skt-link">Akrūra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृतवत्</span> <i>kṛtavat</i> [ppa. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtavatī</i> qui a fait; ayant fait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prakrit kitava.<br> <i>sa</i> <i>tat</i> <i>kṛtavān</i> il a fait ça.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtaviirya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtavīrya</i> [<a href="#entry-viirya" class="skt-link">vīrya</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rtaviirya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du prince Kṛtavīrya «le Vigoureux», roi des <a href="#entry-Uhaihayaas" class="skt-link">Haihayās</a>; pour son éducation il donna une fortune à son <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>, rendant riches les <a href="#entry-Ubhaargavaas" class="skt-link">Bhārgavās</a>; il est père de <a href="#entry-Ukaartaviirya" class="skt-link">Kārtavīrya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtayuga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtayuga</i> [<a href="#entry-yuga" class="skt-link">yuga</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rtayuga" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ âge du monde, âge d'or; syn. <a href="#entry-satyayuga" class="skt-link">satyayuga</a>; son roi est <a href="#entry-Unarasi_mha" class="skt-link">Narasiṃha</a>; seuls les <a href="#entry-braahma_naas" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇās</a> peuvent y pratiquer l'ascèse [<a href="#entry-tapasya" class="skt-link">tapasya</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृति</span> <i>kṛti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> fabrication, composition; action, activité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> création, œuvre; œuvre littéraire; composition musicale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. paroles dévotionnelles adressées à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> magie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. mètre véd. de 2 vers de 12 syllabes et un vers de 8 syllabes; aussi de 4 vers de 20 syllabes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre vingt <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. carré d'un nombre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] np. de Kṛti «Magie», épouse de <a href="#entry-Usa_mhraada" class="skt-link">Saṃhrāda</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Upa_ncajana" class="skt-link">Pañcajana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtimatī</i> qui crée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृतिन्</span> <i>kṛtin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtinī</i> qui agit; actif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> expert, habile, doué, savant (en ⟨loc. iic.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vertueux, pur, pieux; obéissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a accompli un but ou obtenu un objet; satisfait — <a href="#entry-k_rtin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kṛtī «Accompli», fils de <a href="#entry-Ucyavana" class="skt-link">Cyavana</a>, père d'<a href="#entry-Uuparicara" class="skt-link">Uparicara</a>-<a href="#entry-Uvasu_2" class="skt-link">Vasu_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtiraata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtirāta</i> [<a href="#entry-raata" class="skt-link">rāta</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rtiraata" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhP.] np. du roi Kṛtirāta <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a> «Donné par magie», fils de <a href="#entry-Upratiipaka" class="skt-link">Pratīpaka</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Umahaaromaa" class="skt-link">Mahāromā</a>; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Ukiirtiraata" class="skt-link">Kīrtirāta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-k_rti" class="skt-link">kṛti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtnu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्नु</span> <i>kṛtnu</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rt_1" class="skt-link">kṛt_1</a><a href="#entry--nu" class="skt-link">-nu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtnu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtnu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtnu" class="skt-link">f.</a> capable, habile, qui a le savoir-faire — <a href="#entry-k_rtnu" class="skt-link">m.</a> artisan habile, bon ouvrier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtrima">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्रिम</span> <i>kṛtrima</i> [<a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtrima" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtrima" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtrimaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtrimā</i> artificiel, factice, faux; accessoire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adoptif (enfant).<br> <i>saṃskṛtaṃ</i> <i>kṛtrime</i> <i>lakṣaṇopete</i> [<a href="#entry-Uamarako_sa" class="skt-link">Amarakoṣa</a>] Le sanskrit est marqué du signe de l'artificiel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtsna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्स्न</span> <i>kṛtsna</i> a. <a href="#entry-k_rtsna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtsna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtsnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtsnā</i> complet, entier; tout.<br> <i>kṛtsnena</i> partiellement; opp. <a href="#entry-ekadezena" class="skt-link">ekadeśena</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtsnakaaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtsnakāraka</i> [<a href="#entry-kaaraka" class="skt-link">kāraka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtsnakaaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtsnakaaraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtsnakaarakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtsnakārakā</i> que quiconque est capable de faire.<br> <a href="#entry-k_rtsna" class="skt-link">kṛtsna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtsnataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्स्नता</span> <i>kṛtsnatā</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rtsna" class="skt-link">kṛtsna</a><a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rtsnataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> totalité; complétude.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्त</span> <i>kṛtta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_rt_1" class="skt-link">kṛt_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rttaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛttā</i> coupé, retranché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्ति</span> <i>kṛtti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_rt_1" class="skt-link">kṛt_1</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> action de couper, de fendre, de trancher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> écorce; écorce de bouleau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> peau; peau de bête; peau d'antilope <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>cortex</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rttika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛttika</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rtti" class="skt-link">kṛtti</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rttika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rttika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rttikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛttikā</i> coupant — <a href="#entry-k_rttikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛttikā</i> pl. myth. np. des Kṛttikās «les Coupantes» ou groupe des 6 Pléiades; elles furent les nourrices de <a href="#entry-Ukaarttikeya" class="skt-link">Kārttikeya</a>; elles forment avec <a href="#entry-Uarundhatii" class="skt-link">Arundhatī</a> les épouses des 7 sages [<a href="#entry-saptar_si" class="skt-link">saptarṣi</a>]; <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> apparut à chacune sous l'apparence de son époux pour abuser d'elles; seule <a href="#entry-Uarundhatii" class="skt-link">Arundhatī</a> décela la supercherie et resta fidèle, évitant son bannissement loin de la Grande Ourse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. premier astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] les symbolisant cosmiquement, corr. aux 6 étoiles des Pléiades, et dont le symbole est un couteau; <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> y préside.<br> <a href="#entry-k_rtti" class="skt-link">kṛtti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्वा</span> <i>kṛtvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-k_rt_1" class="skt-link">kṛt_1</a>] ind. ayant fait.<br> <i>iti</i> <i>kṛtvā</i> adv. de ce fait, ceci étant; ceci fait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtvas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्वस्</span> <i>kṛtvas</i> ifc. adv. fois.<br> <i>daśa</i> <i>kṛtvas</i> 10 fois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृत्य</span> <i>kṛtya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtyā</i> devant être fait, devant être accompli; juste, convenable — <a href="#entry-k_rtya" class="skt-link">n.</a> affaire, occupation, ouvrage; intérêt; tâche, devoir, responsabilité; le bien (opp. <a href="#entry-ak_rtya" class="skt-link">akṛtya</a> le mal) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> effet, résultat; motif, but, dessein, objet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ce qui concerne ⟨g.⟩ (à propos de ⟨i.⟩) — <a href="#entry-k_rtya" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. suffixe du pfp. (<i>-tavya</i>, <i>-anīya</i>, <i>-ya</i>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rtyavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛtyavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rtyavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtyavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rtyavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛtyavatī</i> qui a une affaire, occupé.<br> <a href="#entry-k_rtya" class="skt-link">kṛtya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृश्</span> <i>kṛś</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(kṛśyati)</i> maigrir, s'affaiblir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. décroître (lune) — ca. <i>(karśayati)</i> faire maigrir, faire dépérir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृश</span> <i>kṛśa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_rz" class="skt-link">kṛś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rzaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛśā</i> mince, maigre, émacié; faible, chétif; menu, insignifiant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rzaanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृशानु</span> <i>kṛśānu</i> <a href="#entry-k_rzaanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> feu de l'éclair; myth. un nom d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> le Feu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kṛśānu «Qui bande l'arc», centaure [<a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>] sagittaire qui tira à l'arc contre le faucon [<a href="#entry-zyena" class="skt-link">śyena</a>] en lequel s'était transformé <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, pour tenter de l'empêcher de donner le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> divin à <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a> l'homme; il est armé de l'éclair [<a href="#entry-vidyut_2" class="skt-link">vidyut_2</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rzaanuretas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛśānuretas</i> [<a href="#entry-retas" class="skt-link">retas</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rzaanuretas" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kṛśānuretas, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «au sperme de feu».<br> <a href="#entry-k_rzaanu" class="skt-link">kṛśānu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rzaazva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛśāśva</i> [<a href="#entry-azva" class="skt-link">aśva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rzaazva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rzaazva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rzaazvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛśāśvā</i> qui a des chevaux maigres — <a href="#entry-k_rzaazva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du géniteur [<a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a>] Kṛśāśva; il épousa <a href="#entry-Ujayaa" class="skt-link">Jayā</a> et <a href="#entry-Usuprabhaa" class="skt-link">Suprabhā</a> dont il eut 50 fils de chacune; chacun d'eux était une arme de grand pouvoir; <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a> acquit ces armes par ses austérités, et les transmit à <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> et <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rzataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛśatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rzataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> minceur, maigreur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rzatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛśatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-k_rzatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> minceur, maigreur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_rzodara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṛśodara</i> [<a href="#entry-udara" class="skt-link">udara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_rzodara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rzodara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_rzodaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṛśodarā</i> qui a la taille fine.<br> <a href="#entry-k_rza" class="skt-link">kṛśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्ष</span> <i>kṣa</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-k_si" class="skt-link">kṣi</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-k_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> champ.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षण</span> <i>kṣaṇa</i> <a href="#entry-k_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> seconde, instant, moment; loisir, occasion; mesure de temps (approx. 4/5 seconde).<br> <i>kṣaṇam</i> adv. un instant.<br> <i>kṣaṇena</i> var. <i>kṣaṇāt</i> adv. à l'instant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sa_nadaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣaṇadā</i> [<a href="#entry-daa" class="skt-link">dā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sa_nadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> nuit [«qui donne le loisir»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sa_nadaacara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣaṇadācara</i> [<a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">cara</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sa_nadaacara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du vampire [<a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>] Kṣaṇadācara «Noctambule».<br> <a href="#entry-k_sa_nadaa" class="skt-link">kṣaṇadā</a> <br> <a href="#entry-k_sa_na" class="skt-link">kṣaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sa_nika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षणिक</span> <i>kṣaṇika</i> [<a href="#entry-k_sa_na" class="skt-link">kṣaṇa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sa_nika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sa_nika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sa_nikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣaṇikā</i> fugace, momentané; qui ne dure qu'un instant, éphémère; instantané <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a des loisirs, qui profite d'une occasion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sa_nikavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣaṇikavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sa_nikavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. doctrine de la nature transitoire de toutes choses.<br> <a href="#entry-k_sa_nika" class="skt-link">kṣaṇika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षाम</span> <i>kṣāma</i> a. <a href="#entry-k_saama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣāmā</i> brûlé; émacié, maigre; affaibli <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faible; infirme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षान्त</span> <i>kṣānta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_sam" class="skt-link">kṣam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_saanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣāntā</i> patient <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> supportable, pardonné — <a href="#entry-k_saanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> patience.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षान्ति</span> <i>kṣānti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_sam" class="skt-link">kṣam</a>] <a href="#entry-k_saanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> patience, indulgence; constance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. la tolérance, une des 6 perfections [<a href="#entry-paaramitaa" class="skt-link">pāramitā</a>] d'un accompli [<a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षार</span> <i>kṣāra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_sar" class="skt-link">kṣar</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_saara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣārā</i> caustique, corrosif; âcre, amer, salé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षात्र</span> <i>kṣātra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-k_satra" class="skt-link">kṣatra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_saatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saatrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣātrī</i> propre au guerrier [<a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a>] — <a href="#entry-k_saatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> état de chef, privilège du prince, dignité de guerrier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saatratejas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣātratejas</i> [<a href="#entry-tejas" class="skt-link">tejas</a>] <a href="#entry-k_saatratejas" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. effulgence des princes; cf. <a href="#entry-U_rciika" class="skt-link">Ṛcīka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-k_saatra" class="skt-link">kṣātra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षद्</span> <i>kṣad</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(kṣadate)</i> découper (not. la viande), disséquer; diviser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> distribuer la nourriture; manger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षल्</span> <i>kṣal</i> ca. <i>(kṣālayati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i><i>)</i> laver <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ondoyer, purifier par l'eau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षम्</span> <i>kṣam</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(kṣamate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣānta</i>, <i>kṣamita</i><i>)</i> être patient, endurer, supporter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> permettre; pardonner, être indulgent envers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pardonner qqc. ⟨acc.⟩ à qqn. ⟨g. dat⟩ — ca. <i>(kṣamayati)</i> ca. md. <i>(kṣāmayate)</i> demander pardon à qqn. ⟨acc.⟩ pour ⟨acc.⟩.<br> <i>kṣamatām</i> var. <i>kṣamyatām</i> adr. excusez moi.<br> <i>kṣamasva</i> <i>me</i> <i>tat</i> adr. excusez moi pour cela.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षम</span> <i>kṣama</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_sam" class="skt-link">kṣam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_samaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣamā</i> patient, capable d'endurer; capable de ⟨loc. inf.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> supportable, tolérable — f. cf. <a href="#entry-k_samaa" class="skt-link">kṣamā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_samaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षमा</span> <i>kṣamā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-k_sama" class="skt-link">kṣama</a>] <a href="#entry-k_samaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> patience; indulgence, pardon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la Terre.<br> <i>kṣamāṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> être indulgent.<br> <i>kṣamāṃ</i> <i>karotu</i> adr. excusez moi.<br> <i>kṣamāṃ</i> <i>prārthaye</i> adr. excusez moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_samaada_mza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣamādaṃśa</i> [<a href="#entry-da_mza" class="skt-link">daṃśa</a>] <a href="#entry-k_samaada_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Moringa oleifera</i>, arbre raifort; il est cultivé comme plante médicinale pour combattre le diabète; ses feuilles et fruits sont riches en nutrients et utilisés en cuisine.<br> <a href="#entry-k_samaa" class="skt-link">kṣamā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_samataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣamatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_samataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> capacité, aptitude.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_samatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣamatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-k_samatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> capacité, aptitude (à ⟨iic. loc.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_samavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣamavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_samavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_samavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_samavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣamavatī</i> sachant ce qui est approprié ou adéquat.<br> <a href="#entry-k_sama" class="skt-link">kṣama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_samita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षमित</span> <i>kṣamita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_sam" class="skt-link">kṣam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_samita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_samita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_samitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣamitā</i> pardonné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_san">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षन्</span> <i>kṣan</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(kṣaṇoti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣata</i><i>)</i> frapper, blesser — pr. md. <i>(kṣaṇute)</i> se blesser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षप्</span> <i>kṣap</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kṣapati)</i> pr. md. <i>(kṣapate)</i> être abstinent; jeûner; faire pénitence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sapa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षपण</span> <i>kṣapaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_sap" class="skt-link">kṣap</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sapa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> abstinence — <a href="#entry-k_sapa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> moine mendiant bd. ou jn.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sapa_naka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣapaṇaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sapa_naka" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. moine mendiant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Kṣapaṇaka, l'un des neuf joyaux [<a href="#entry-navaratna" class="skt-link">navaratna</a>] de la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-k_sapa_na" class="skt-link">kṣapaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sapaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षपा</span> <i>kṣapā</i> <a href="#entry-k_sapaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> nuit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sapaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣapāha</i> [<a href="#entry-aha" class="skt-link">aha</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sapaaha" class="skt-link">n.</a> un jour et une nuit (24 h).<br> <a href="#entry-k_sapaa" class="skt-link">kṣapā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sapita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षपित</span> <i>kṣapita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-k_sii" class="skt-link">kṣī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sapita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sapita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sapitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣapitā</i> détruit; ruiné; diminué; supprimé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sapitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षपितव्य</span> <i>kṣapitavya</i> [ca. pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-k_sii" class="skt-link">kṣī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sapitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sapitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sapitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣapitavyā</i> à finir, à supprimer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षर्</span> <i>kṣar</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kṣarati)</i> pft. <i>(cakṣāra)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣarita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> couler, s'écouler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'échapper, se perdre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> répandre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> expulser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षर</span> <i>kṣara</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_sar" class="skt-link">kṣar</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣarā</i> qui s'écoule, périssable — <a href="#entry-k_sara" class="skt-link">n.</a> corps.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षत</span> <i>kṣata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_san" class="skt-link">kṣan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣatā</i> frappé, blessé, brisé; anéanti — <a href="#entry-k_sata" class="skt-link">n.</a> blessure, contusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_satra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षत्र</span> <i>kṣatra</i> [<a href="#entry-k_si" class="skt-link">kṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-k_satra" class="skt-link">n.</a> pouvoir temporel, domination, noblesse, souveraineté; autorité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. principe dominant de la noblesse d'épée; classe [<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>] noble des guerriers; un de ses membres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_satradharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣatradharma</i> [<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] <a href="#entry-k_satradharma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_satradharma" class="skt-link">n.</a> devoir de la caste noble; éthique royale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aptitude guerrière; courage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_satrapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣatrapa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_2" class="skt-link">pa_2</a>] <a href="#entry-k_satrapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> gouverneur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. satrape.<br> <a href="#entry-k_satra" class="skt-link">kṣatra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_satriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षत्रिय</span> <i>kṣatriya</i> [<a href="#entry-k_satra" class="skt-link">kṣatra</a><a href="#entry--iya" class="skt-link">-iya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_satriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣatriyā</i> qui gouverne — <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> souveraineté; noblesse d'épée — <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_satriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣatriyā</i> soc. membre de la 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ classe [<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>], guerrier, prince, noble <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kṣatriyās</i> soc. la classe des princes — <a href="#entry-k_satriyii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣatriyī</i> épouse d'un <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>khattiya</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_satt_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षत्तृ</span> <i>kṣattṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_sad" class="skt-link">kṣad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_satt_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_satt_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sattrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣattrī</i> véd. qui découpe ou distribue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. huissier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षौर</span> <i>kṣaura</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-k_sura" class="skt-link">kṣura</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_saura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sauraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣaurā</i> effectué avec un rasoir — <a href="#entry-k_saura" class="skt-link">n.</a> rasage; not. fait de se raser le crâne — <a href="#entry-k_saurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣaurī</i> rasoir.<br> <i>kṣauraṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> raser.<br> <i>kṣauraṃ</i> <i>kār</i> se faire raser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saurakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣaurakaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-k_saurakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> rasage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saurakarma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣaurakarma</i> [<a href="#entry-karma" class="skt-link">karma</a>] <a href="#entry-k_saurakarma" class="skt-link">n.</a> rasage.<br> <a href="#entry-k_saura" class="skt-link">kṣaura</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षव</span> <i>kṣava</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_su" class="skt-link">kṣu</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sava" class="skt-link">m.</a> éternuement; toux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_savathu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षवथु</span> <i>kṣavathu</i> [<a href="#entry-k_su" class="skt-link">kṣu</a><a href="#entry--athu" class="skt-link">-athu</a>] <a href="#entry-k_savathu" class="skt-link">m.</a> éternuement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saya_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षय</span> <i>kṣaya_1</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_si" class="skt-link">kṣi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_saya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_saya_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣayā</i> qui habite, qui réside — <a href="#entry-k_saya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> habitation, séjour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_saya_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
