    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षय</span> <i>kṣaya_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_sii" class="skt-link">kṣī</a>] <a href="#entry-k_saya_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> ruine, perte, destruction; infortune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> décroissance; opp. <a href="#entry-v_rddhi" class="skt-link">vṛddhi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fin, maladie mortelle; not. phtisie, tuberculose <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. mois lunaire orphelin, encadrant un mois solaire court ne comportant pas de nouvelle lune; un tel mois est toujours précédé et suivi dans les trois mois d'un mois intercalaire [<a href="#entry-adhika" class="skt-link">adhika</a>], ce qui permet de le nommer; l'école de l'Est supprime le deuxième mois intercalaire, celle du Nord-Ouest supprime le premier; l'école du Sud les garde tous deux, mais nomme doublement le mois orphelin qui est dit double [<a href="#entry-yugma" class="skt-link">yugma</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. quantité négative; opposé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Kṣaya «en fin de vie», personnifiant l'an 60 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>].<br> <i>kṣayaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> diminuer; aller à sa fin, périr.<br> <i>kṣayaṃ</i> <i>i</i> id.<br> <i>kṣayaṃ</i> <i>yā</i> id.<br> <i>kṣayaṃ</i> <i>nī</i> détruire.<br> <i>kulakṣaya</i> ruine d'une famille.<br> <i>lokakṣaya</i> fin du monde.<br> <i>jīvitakṣaye</i> adv. à la fin de la vie.<br> <i>dinakṣaye</i> adv. à la fin du jour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षयिन्</span> <i>kṣayin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_sii" class="skt-link">kṣī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sayi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣayiṇī</i> périssable; malade; qui s'éteint (lumière).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sayya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षय्य</span> <i>kṣayya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-k_sii" class="skt-link">kṣī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sayya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sayya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sayyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣayyā</i> qui peut être détruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sema">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षेम</span> <i>kṣema</i> [<a href="#entry-k_si" class="skt-link">kṣi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sema" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sema" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_semaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣemā</i> habitable; sûr, confortable, tranquille — <a href="#entry-k_sema" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sema" class="skt-link">n.</a> paix, tranquillité; sécurité, bien-être, bonheur, prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [SP.] np. de Kṣema «Prospérité», fils de <a href="#entry-Usiddhi_2" class="skt-link">Siddhi_2</a> et <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. fils de <a href="#entry-Utitik_saa" class="skt-link">Titikṣā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_semaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣemarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-k_semaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kṣemarāja, philosophe du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> du 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, élève d'<a href="#entry-Uabhinavagupta" class="skt-link">Abhinavagupta</a>; il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Upratyabhij_naah_rdaya" class="skt-link">Pratyabhijñāhṛdaya</a>, de l'exégèse <a href="#entry-Uvimarzinii" class="skt-link">Vimarśinī</a> du <a href="#entry-Uzivasuutra" class="skt-link">Śivasūtra</a>, du commentaire <a href="#entry-Uspandanir_naya" class="skt-link">Spandanirṇaya</a> des <a href="#entry-Uspandakaarikaas" class="skt-link">Spandakārikās</a>, et d'un commentaire du <a href="#entry-Ustavacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Stavacintāmaṇi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_semayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣemayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-k_semayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>kṣemayogau</i> la quiétude et l'action; le repos et l'effort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_semendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣemendra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-k_semendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kṣemendra, poète du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> du 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur de nombreuses œuvres, not. de manuels de composition poétique comme l'<a href="#entry-Uaucityavicaaracarcaa" class="skt-link">Aucityavicāracarcā</a>, le <a href="#entry-Ukavika_n_thaabhara_na" class="skt-link">Kavikaṇṭhābharaṇa</a> et le <a href="#entry-Usuv_rttatilaka" class="skt-link">Suvṛttatilaka</a>; sa <a href="#entry-Ub_rhatkathaama_njarii" class="skt-link">Bṛhatkathāmañjarī</a> est une versification de la <a href="#entry-Ub_rhatkathaa" class="skt-link">Bṛhatkathā</a>; il est aussi l'auteur d'ouvrages satiriques comme la <a href="#entry-Usamayamaat_rkaa" class="skt-link">Samayamātṛkā</a>; on lui doit aussi la <a href="#entry-Uraamaya_nama_njarii" class="skt-link">Rāmayaṇamañjarī</a> et la <a href="#entry-Ubarathakathaama_njarii" class="skt-link">Barathakathāmañjarī</a>, versions resp. du <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a> et du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a><br> <a href="#entry-k_sema" class="skt-link">kṣema</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_semiizvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षेमीश्वर</span> <i>kṣemīśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-k_sema" class="skt-link">kṣema</a>-<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-k_semiizvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kṣemīśvara, auteur dramatique du 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩-10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui doit les drames <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dakauzika_1" class="skt-link">Caṇḍakauśika_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Unai_sadhaananda" class="skt-link">Naiṣadhānanda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sepa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षेप</span> <i>kṣepa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sepa" class="skt-link">m.</a> jet, tir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> offense, injure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sepa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षेपण</span> <i>kṣepaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sepa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> action de jeter, de déplacer, de propulser — <a href="#entry-k_sepa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣepaṇī</i> rame; aviron.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_setra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षेत्र</span> <i>kṣetra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-k_si" class="skt-link">kṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-k_setra" class="skt-link">n.</a> propriété, domaine; champ, plaine, campagne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> endroit, emplacement, lieu; figure géométrique; lieu de pélerinage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> épouse; corps <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>khetra</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_setraj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣetrajña</i> [<a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_setraj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_setraj_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_setraj_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣetrajñā</i> [«qui connaît son domaine»] habile, expert, adroit — <a href="#entry-k_setraj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> âme, conscience <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. conscience spirituelle animant le corps et l'esprit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_setriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣetriya</i> [<a href="#entry--iya" class="skt-link">-iya</a>] <a href="#entry-k_setriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme adultère.<br> <a href="#entry-k_setra" class="skt-link">kṣetra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_seya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षेय</span> <i>kṣeya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-k_sii" class="skt-link">kṣī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_seya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_seya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_seyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣeyā</i> qui doit être détruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_si">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षि</span> <i>kṣi</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(kṣeti)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> véd. posséder, gouverner; être maître de ⟨g.⟩ — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kṣayati)</i> id. — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(kṣiyati)</i> habiter, demeurer, résider <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être tranquille; être caché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षी</span> <i>kṣī</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(kṣiṇoti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(kṣiṇāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣīṇa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> détruire, endommager, diminuer; frapper, tuer — ps. <i>(kṣīyate)</i> périr; passer (dit du temps) — ca. <i>(kṣapayati)</i> détruire, ruiner, achever.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sii_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षीण</span> <i>kṣīṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_sii" class="skt-link">kṣī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sii_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sii_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sii_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣīṇā</i> diminué, faible; usé, épuisé; détruit, ruiné; pauvre, misérable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sii_nataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīṇatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sii_nataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> épuisement; diminution, usure, corruption.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sii_nav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīṇavṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sii_nav_rtti" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sii_nav_rtti" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sii_nav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> sans emploi; qui n'a plus de moyen de subsistance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sii_navat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīṇavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sii_navat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sii_navat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sii_navatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣīṇavatī</i> usé, épuisé; corrompu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sii_nazakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīṇaśakti</i> [<a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sii_nazakti" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sii_nazakti" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sii_nazakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> épuisé, qui n'a plus de force.<br> <a href="#entry-k_sii_na" class="skt-link">kṣīṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siiba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षीब</span> <i>kṣība</i> a. <a href="#entry-k_siiba" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_siiba" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_siibaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣībā</i> ivre, intoxiqué — <a href="#entry-k_siiba" class="skt-link">m.</a> ivrogne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षीर</span> <i>kṣīra</i> [<a href="#entry-k_sar" class="skt-link">kṣar</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siira" class="skt-link">n.</a> lait; eau, suc, sève — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(kṣīrasyati)</i> désirer du lait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>khīr</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siiramahaar_nava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīramahārṇava</i> [<a href="#entry-mahaar_nava" class="skt-link">mahārṇava</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siiramahaar_nava" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-k_siiroda" class="skt-link">kṣīroda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siiraraama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīrarāma</i> [<a href="#entry-raama" class="skt-link">rāma</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siiraraama" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Kṣīrarāma, grand lieu de pélerinage shivaïte à Palakollu en Andhra, à l'ouest du delta de la <a href="#entry-Ugodaavarii" class="skt-link">Godāvarī</a>; c'est un <a href="#entry-pa_ncaraamak_setra" class="skt-link">pañcarāmakṣetra</a>, on dit que le <a href="#entry-zivalifga" class="skt-link">śivaliṅga</a> <a href="#entry-Uk_siiraraamezvara" class="skt-link">Kṣīrarāmeśvara</a> y fut installé par <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siiraraamezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīrarāmeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siiraraamezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Kṣīrarāmeśvara, <a href="#entry-zivalifga" class="skt-link">śivaliṅga</a> installé par <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> à <a href="#entry-Uk_siiraraama" class="skt-link">Kṣīrarāma</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-k_siiraraama" class="skt-link">kṣīrarāma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siirasagara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīrasagara</i> [<a href="#entry-sagara" class="skt-link">sagara</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siirasagara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. océan de lait primordial; syn. <a href="#entry-k_siirodadhi" class="skt-link">kṣīrodadhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siirasvaamin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīrasvāmin</i> [<a href="#entry-svaamin" class="skt-link">svāmin</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siirasvaamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du grammairien Kṣīrasvāmī (8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il vécut au <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> à la cour de <a href="#entry-Ujayaapii_da" class="skt-link">Jayāpīḍa</a>, à qui il enseigna la grammaire du sanskrit; on lui doit l'<a href="#entry-Uamarako_sodghaa_tana" class="skt-link">Amarakoṣodghāṭana</a>, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Unaamalifgaanuzaasana" class="skt-link">Nāmaliṅgānuśāsana</a>, et la <a href="#entry-Uk_siiratarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">Kṣīrataraṃgiṇī</a>, commentaire du <a href="#entry-paa_niniiya" class="skt-link">pāṇinīya</a> <a href="#entry-Udhaatupaa_tha" class="skt-link">Dhātupāṭha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siiratarafgi_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīrataraṃgiṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-tarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">taraṃgiṇī</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siiratara_mgi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Kṣīrataraṃgiṇī, lexique de racines [<a href="#entry-Udhaatuv_rtti" class="skt-link">Dhātuvṛtti</a>] dû à <a href="#entry-Uk_siirasvaamii" class="skt-link">Kṣīrasvāmī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siirin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षीरिन्</span> <i>kṣīrin</i> [<a href="#entry-k_siira" class="skt-link">kṣīra</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_siirin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_siirin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_siiri_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣīriṇī</i> qui donne du lait; succulent (plante).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siiroda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīroda</i> [<a href="#entry-uda" class="skt-link">uda</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siiroda" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. océan de lait primordial <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. du chambellan Kṣirodha, envoyé par <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha" class="skt-link">Citraratha</a> à <a href="#entry-Umahaazvetaa" class="skt-link">Mahāśvetā</a> pour consoler <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>; il ramena <a href="#entry-Utaralikaa" class="skt-link">Taralikā</a> à cette dernière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siirodadhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīrodadhi</i> [<a href="#entry-udadhi" class="skt-link">udadhi</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siirodadhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. océan de lait primordial; syn. <a href="#entry-k_siiroda" class="skt-link">kṣīroda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-k_siira" class="skt-link">kṣīra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siirodaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīrodaka</i> [<a href="#entry-udaka" class="skt-link">udaka</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siirodaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-k_siiroda" class="skt-link">kṣīroda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siirodakazaayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīrodakaśāyin</i> [<a href="#entry-zaayin" class="skt-link">śāyin</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siirodakazaayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kṣīrodakaśāyī épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> cosmique, reposant sur les eaux primordiales.<br> <a href="#entry-k_siirodaka" class="skt-link">kṣīrodaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siirodamathana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣīrodamathana</i> [<a href="#entry-mathana" class="skt-link">mathana</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. barattage de l'océan de lait; les dieux [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] et les démons [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] s'allièrent pour baratter la mer de lait primordiale, avec le mont <a href="#entry-Umandara" class="skt-link">Mandara</a> comme baratte, le dragon [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>] <a href="#entry-Uvaasuki" class="skt-link">Vāsuki</a> comme corde, et <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukuurma" class="skt-link">Kūrma</a> comme support; tous les trésors de la Terre en sortirent, notamment <a href="#entry-Usurabhii" class="skt-link">Surabhī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaaru_nii" class="skt-link">Vāruṇī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>, l'arbre <a href="#entry-paarijaata" class="skt-link">pārijāta</a>, <a href="#entry-Uuccai_hzravaa" class="skt-link">Uccaiḥśravā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uairaavata" class="skt-link">Airāvata</a>, <a href="#entry-Usudarzana" class="skt-link">Sudarśana</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukaustubha" class="skt-link">Kaustubha</a>, et enfin <a href="#entry-Udhanvantari" class="skt-link">Dhanvantari</a> portant l'urne [<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a>] d'ambroisie [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>] que les dieux et les démons se disputèrent; <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> transformé en <a href="#entry-Umohinii" class="skt-link">Mohinī</a> les subjugua par sa magie [<a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">māyā</a>] et servit les 33 dieux, <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a> le premier; le démon <a href="#entry-Uraahuketuu" class="skt-link">Rāhuketū</a> en vola et commença à boire, mais <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> le décapita en une tête éternelle <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a>, et un corps éphémère <a href="#entry-Uketu" class="skt-link">Ketu</a>; il en sortit aussi des maux, tels que <a href="#entry-Ujye_s_thaa" class="skt-link">Jyeṣṭhā</a> la mauvaise sœur aînée de <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>, et le poison <a href="#entry-halaahala" class="skt-link">halāhala</a> qui détruit tout, et qui fut avalé par <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> dont la gorge en bleuit [<a href="#entry-Uniilaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">Nīlakaṇṭha</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-k_siiroda" class="skt-link">kṣīroda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sip">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षिप्</span> <i>kṣip</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(kṣipati)</i> pft. <i>(cikṣepa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣipta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>kṣiptvā</i>, <i>-kṣipya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>adhi</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> jeter, lancer; propulser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> précipiter dans ⟨2 acc.⟩; atteindre (avec un trait) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jeter au loin; écarter, se débarrasser de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rejeter la faute sur ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mépriser; insulter; frapper; détruire — ca. <i>(kṣepayati)</i> faire lancer; envoyer; faire descendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षिप</span> <i>kṣipa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sipa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sipaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣipā</i> ifc. qui lance — <a href="#entry-k_sipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lanceur — <a href="#entry-k_sipaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣipā</i> lancement; jet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sipaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षिपक</span> <i>kṣipaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sipaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> archer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sipnu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षिप्नु</span> <i>kṣipnu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sipnu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sipnu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sipnu" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«qui lance des obstacles sur le chemin»] qui obstrue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui méprise; qui insulte; syn. <a href="#entry-niraakari_s_nu" class="skt-link">nirākariṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sipra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षिप्र</span> <i>kṣipra</i> [<a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sipra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sipra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sipraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣiprā</i> rapide — <a href="#entry-k_sipraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣiprā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Kṣiprā «Rapide», coulant à <a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Usipraa" class="skt-link">Siprā</a>.<br> <i>kṣipram</i> adv. rapidement, vite, aussitôt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sipta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षिप्त</span> <i>kṣipta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sipta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sipta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_siptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣiptā</i> jeté, lancé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siptayoni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣiptayoni</i> [<a href="#entry-yoni" class="skt-link">yoni</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_siptayoni" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_siptayoni" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_siptayoni" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. de mauvaise naissance; se dit d'une personne pour laquelle un brâhmane ne peut officier comme prêtre [<a href="#entry-_rtvij" class="skt-link">ṛtvij</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-k_sipta" class="skt-link">kṣipta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_siti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षिति</span> <i>kṣiti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_si" class="skt-link">kṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-k_siti" class="skt-link">f.</a> habitation, séjour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> terre, sol; la Terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sitibhuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣitibhuj</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuj_3" class="skt-link">bhuj_3</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sitibhuj" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«qui jouit de la terre»].<br> <a href="#entry-k_siti" class="skt-link">kṣiti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sitigarbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣitigarbha</i> [<a href="#entry-garbha" class="skt-link">garbha</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sitigarbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de l'accompli [<a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a>] Kṣitigarbha «Germe de la Terre»; on le représente tenant le bâton [<a href="#entry-khakkhara" class="skt-link">khakkhara</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sitipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣitipa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_2" class="skt-link">pa_2</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sitipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«qui protège la terre»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_smaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्ष्मा</span> <i>kṣmā</i> <a href="#entry-k_smaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> Terre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. χθων; fr. chtonien, autochtone.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sobha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षोभ</span> <i>kṣobha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_subh" class="skt-link">kṣubh</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sobha" class="skt-link">m.</a> tremblement, agitation; trouble, inquiétude, émotion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fort courant d'eau, torrent, houle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sobhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षोभ्य</span> <i>kṣobhya</i> [ca. pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-k_subh" class="skt-link">kṣubh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sobhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sobhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sobhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣobhyā</i> agité, troublé, inquiet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षु</span> <i>kṣu</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(kṣauti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣuta</i><i>)</i> éternuer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tousser — dés. <i>(cukṣūṣati)</i> avoir envie d'éternuer ou de tousser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_su_n_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुण्ण</span> <i>kṣuṇṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_sud" class="skt-link">kṣud</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_su_n_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_su_n_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_su_n_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣuṇṇā</i> écrasé, broyé, pulvérisé; cassé, troué; piétiné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> violé (vœu).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_subdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुब्ध</span> <i>kṣubdha</i> var. <i>kṣubhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_subh" class="skt-link">kṣubh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_subdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_subdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_subdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣubdhā</i> secoué, agité; expulsé (not. roi) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perturbé; inquiet, excité — <a href="#entry-k_subdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> bâton de baratte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_subh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुभ्</span> <i>kṣubh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(kṣubhyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣubdha</i>, <i>kṣubhita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> trembler, être agité — ca. <i>(kṣobhayati)</i> secouer, agiter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> all. schieben; ang. to shake.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_subhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुभित</span> <i>kṣubhita</i> var. <i>kṣubdha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_subh" class="skt-link">kṣubh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_subhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_subhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_subhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣubhitā</i> secoué, agité; lancé, mis en mouvement; expulsé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perturbé; inquiet, excité; en colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sud">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुद्</span> <i>kṣud</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kṣodati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣuṇṇa</i><i>)</i> écraser, broyer; piétiner, fouler aux pieds.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sudh_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुध्</span> <i>kṣudh_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(kṣudhyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣudhita</i><i>)</i> avoir faim.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sudh_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुध्</span> <i>kṣudh_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_sudh_1" class="skt-link">kṣudh_1</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sudh_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> faim.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sudhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुधा</span> <i>kṣudhā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_sudh_1" class="skt-link">kṣudh_1</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sudhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> faim.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sudhaakula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣudhākula</i> [<a href="#entry-aakula" class="skt-link">ākula</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sudhaakula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sudhaakula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sudhaakulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣudhākulā</i> agité par la faim.<br> <a href="#entry-k_sudh_2" class="skt-link">kṣudh_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sudhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुधित</span> <i>kṣudhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_sudh_1" class="skt-link">kṣudh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sudhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sudhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sudhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣudhitā</i> affamé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sudra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुद्र</span> <i>kṣudra</i> [<a href="#entry-k_sud" class="skt-link">kṣud</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sudra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sudra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣudrā</i> petit, insignifiant; bas, vil, mauvais — <a href="#entry-k_sudra" class="skt-link">n.</a> petite chose, bassesse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kṣudrāṇi</i> phil. [ChU.] les êtres inférieurs qui tournent dans la ronde du <a href="#entry-sa_msaara" class="skt-link">saṃsāra</a>, au contraire des réalisés qui s'en sont libérés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sudrapanasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣudrapanasa</i> [<a href="#entry-panasa" class="skt-link">panasa</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sudrapanasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Artocarpus communis</i>, arbre à pain, une moracée; grand arbre à l'écorce gris-jaune, à usage médical — <a href="#entry-k_sudrapanasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> son fruit, comestible.<br> <a href="#entry-k_sudra" class="skt-link">kṣudra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुर</span> <i>kṣura</i> <a href="#entry-k_sura" class="skt-link">m.</a> rasoir, couteau affilé, cimeterre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>khura</i>] sabot de vache ou de cheval — <a href="#entry-k_surii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣurī</i> couteau, poignard, dague.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sut">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षुत्</span> <i>kṣut</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-k_sudh_2" class="skt-link">kṣudh_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sutpipaasaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣutpipāsā</i> [<a href="#entry-pipaasaa" class="skt-link">pipāsā</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sutpipaasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> faim et soif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sutpipaasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kṣutpipāsita</i> [<a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-k_sutpipaasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sutpipaasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-k_sutpipaasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣutpipāsitā</i> qui a faim et soif.<br> <a href="#entry-k_sutpipaasaa" class="skt-link">kṣutpipāsā</a> <br> <a href="#entry-k_sut" class="skt-link">kṣut</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_sve_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्ष्वेड</span> <i>kṣveḍa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_svi_d" class="skt-link">kṣviḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-k_sve_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> sifflement, bruit — <a href="#entry-k_sve_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kṣveḍā</i> rugissement; cri de bataille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-k_svi_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्ष्विड्</span> <i>kṣviḍ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kṣveḍati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kṣveḍita</i><i>)</i> ca. <i>(kṣveḍayati)</i> émettre un son inarticulé; bourdonner, murmurer, grogner, rugir, siffler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_daara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कडार</span> <i>kaḍāra</i> a. <a href="#entry-ka_daara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_daara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_daaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaḍārā</i> basané — <a href="#entry-ka_daara" class="skt-link">m.</a> serviteur, esclave.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_damba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कडम्ब</span> <i>kaḍamba</i> cf. <a href="#entry-kadamba" class="skt-link">kadamba</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_dambezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaḍambeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_dambezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du temple de Kaḍambeśvara à Raṭṭihalli au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>, construit par les sectateurs <a href="#entry-Ukaalaamukha" class="skt-link">Kālāmukha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_damba" class="skt-link">kaḍamba</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कंस</span> <i>kaṃsa</i> <a href="#entry-ka_msa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_msa" class="skt-link">n.</a> gobelet, coupe (en métal) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (collectif) ustensiles (en métal) — <a href="#entry-ka_msa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du cruel Kaṃsa, fils d'un démon aux traits d'<a href="#entry-Uugrasena" class="skt-link">Ugrasena</a>, roi de <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a>; il épousa <a href="#entry-Uasti" class="skt-link">Asti</a> et <a href="#entry-Upraapti" class="skt-link">Prāpti</a>; il incarnait l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Ukaalanemi" class="skt-link">Kālanemi</a>, et <a href="#entry-Unaarada" class="skt-link">Nārada</a> lui avait prophétisé qu'il serait tué par un fils de sa cousine <a href="#entry-Udevakii" class="skt-link">Devakī</a>; il emprisonna <a href="#entry-Udevakii" class="skt-link">Devakī</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvasudeva" class="skt-link">Vasudeva</a> il fit tuer ses 6 premiers enfants, mais le 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩, <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a>, s'échappa [<a href="#entry-Usafkar_sa_na" class="skt-link">Saṃkarṣaṇa</a>] en étant transféré par Magie [<a href="#entry-Uyogamaayaa" class="skt-link">Yogamāyā</a>] dans le ventre de <a href="#entry-Urohi_nii" class="skt-link">Rohiṇī</a>; le 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩, <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, incarnant <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, fut enlevé par <a href="#entry-Uvasudeva" class="skt-link">Vasudeva</a> par miracle, et élevé en secret par le roi des bergers <a href="#entry-Unanda" class="skt-link">Nanda</a> et sa femme <a href="#entry-Uyazodaa" class="skt-link">Yaśodā</a>; plus tard <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> le détrôna et le tua; cf. <a href="#entry-Uasti" class="skt-link">Asti</a> — <a href="#entry-ka_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṃsā</i> myth. [BhP.] np. de Kaṃsā, sœur de <a href="#entry-Uka_msa" class="skt-link">Kaṃsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्ड्</span> <i>kaṇḍ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(kaṇḍayati)</i> vanner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_dana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्डन</span> <i>kaṇḍana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ka_n_d" class="skt-link">kaṇḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_n_dana" class="skt-link">n.</a> vannage; fait de broyer — <a href="#entry-ka_n_danii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇḍanī</i> mortier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_du">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्डु</span> <i>kaṇḍu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ka_n_d" class="skt-link">kaṇḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_n_du" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_du" class="skt-link">f.</a> démangeaison — ifc. désir violent de — <a href="#entry-ka_n_duu" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇḍū</i> démangeaison, prurit — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(kaṇḍūyati)</i> (se) gratter — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(kaṇḍūyate)</i> démanger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_duuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇḍūti</i> [<a href="#entry--ti" class="skt-link">-ti</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_n_duuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> démangeaison <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (au fig.) désir sexuel (pour une femme).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_duuyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇḍūyā</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_n_duuyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> démangeaison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_dvaadi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇḍvādi</i> [<a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">ādi</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_n_dvaadi" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. [<a href="#entry-Ukaumudii" class="skt-link">Kaumudī</a>] «qui commence par <i>kaṇḍū</i>», qualifie la classe [<a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>] des verbes nominaux.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_n_du" class="skt-link">kaṇḍu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्ट</span> <i>kaṇṭa</i> <a href="#entry-ka_n_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> épine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्टक</span> <i>kaṇṭaka</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_ta" class="skt-link">kaṇṭa</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_n_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> épine, écharde; pointe, aiguille; aiguillon, arête <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rebelle, bandit, ennemi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_takaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_n_takaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_takaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_takaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭakārī</i> épineux — <a href="#entry-ka_n_takaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭakārī</i> natu. bot. <i>Solanum xanthocarpum</i>, petite solanée épineuse aux fleurs violettes et aux fruits jaunes; cette plante médicinale, employée contre la toux, est l'une des 5 petites racines [<a href="#entry-hrasvapa_ncamuula" class="skt-link">hrasvapañcamūla</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-ka_n_ta" class="skt-link">kaṇṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_taki">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्टकि</span> <i>kaṇṭaki</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-ka_n_takin" class="skt-link">kaṇṭakin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_takik_siirin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭakikṣīrin</i> [<a href="#entry-k_siirin" class="skt-link">kṣīrin</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_n_takik_siirin" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. les plantes épineuses et les plantes grasses.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_n_taki" class="skt-link">kaṇṭaki</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_takin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्टकिन्</span> <i>kaṇṭakin</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_taka" class="skt-link">kaṇṭaka</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_n_takin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_takin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_takinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭakinī</i> pourvu d'épines, piquant — <a href="#entry-ka_n_takin" class="skt-link">m.</a> plante épineuse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_takita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭakita</i> [<a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_n_takita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_takita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_takitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭakitā</i> épineux, piquant; plein d'épines.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_n_taka" class="skt-link">kaṇṭaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्ठ</span> <i>kaṇṭha</i> <a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> cou, gorge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_thaabhara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭhābharaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-aabhara_na" class="skt-link">ābharaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_n_thaabhara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> collier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_thabhuu_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭhabhūṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">bhūṣaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_n_thabhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«ornement de la gorge»] collier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se dit d'un poème apprécié.<br> <i>satām</i> <i>kanṭhabhūṣaṇam</i> (poème) ornant la gorge des érudits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_thadaghna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭhadaghna</i> [<a href="#entry-daghna" class="skt-link">daghna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_n_thadaghna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thadaghna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thadaghnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭhadaghnā</i> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thadaghnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭhadaghnī</i> qui a (de l'eau) jusqu'au cou.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_thastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭhastha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_n_thastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thasthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭhasthā</i> [«dans la gorge»] se dit d'un savoir mémorisé, jugé supérieur à un savoir lu dans un livre [<a href="#entry-granthastha" class="skt-link">granthastha</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-mukhastha" class="skt-link">mukhastha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_thataalavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭhatālavya</i> [<a href="#entry-taalavya" class="skt-link">tālavya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_n_thataalavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thataalavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thataalavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭhatālavyā</i> phon. gutturalo-palatal (se dit des voyelles <i>e</i>, <i>ai</i>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_thekaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭhekāla</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_2" class="skt-link">kāla_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_n_thekaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thekaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thekaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭhekālā</i> qui a la gorge noire; syn. <a href="#entry-kaalaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kālakaṇṭha</a> — <a href="#entry-ka_n_thekaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_tho_s_thya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇṭhoṣṭhya</i> [<a href="#entry-o_s_thya" class="skt-link">oṣṭhya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_n_tho_s_thya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_tho_s_thya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_tho_s_thyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭhoṣṭhyā</i> phon. gutturalo-labial (se dit des voyelles <i>o</i>, <i>au</i>).<br> <a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_n_thya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्ठ्य</span> <i>kaṇṭhya</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_n_thya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_n_thyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṇṭhyā</i> relatif à la gorge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. [«prononcé dans la gorge»] guttural — <a href="#entry-ka_n_thya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phonème vélaire ou guttural (postpalatal) (<i>k</i>, <i>kh</i>, <i>g</i>, <i>gh</i>, <i>ṅ</i>) — ifc. qui a ⟨iic.⟩ dans la gorge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण</span> <i>kaṇa</i> <a href="#entry-ka_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> grain, graine; goutte; grain de poussière; atome.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_naabhak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇābhakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-bhak_sa" class="skt-link">bhakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_naabhak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. np. de Kaṇabhakṣa, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uka_naada" class="skt-link">Kaṇāda</a> «qui se nourrit d'atomes».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_naabhuk">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇābhuk</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuj_3" class="skt-link">bhuj_3</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_naabhuk" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. np. de Kaṇabhuk, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uka_naada" class="skt-link">Kaṇāda</a> «qui régit les atomes».<br> <a href="#entry-ka_na" class="skt-link">kaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_naada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇāda</i> [<a href="#entry-ada" class="skt-link">ada</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_naada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. np. de Kaṇāda «qui se nourrit d'atomes», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa_1" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa_1</a>, fondateur du système discriminatif [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>]; on lui attribue le <a href="#entry-Uvaize_sikasuutra" class="skt-link">Vaiśeṣikasūtra</a>; il vécut vers le 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant.; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Uka_nabhuk" class="skt-link">Kaṇabhuk</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uka_nabhak_sa" class="skt-link">Kaṇabhakṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_naadarahasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇādarahasya</i> [<a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">rahasya</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_naadarahasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kaṇādarahasya «Doctrine secrète de <a href="#entry-Uka_naada" class="skt-link">Kaṇāda</a>», manuel de <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>-<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_2" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara_2</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_naada" class="skt-link">kaṇāda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_nva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्व</span> <i>kaṇva</i> <a href="#entry-ka_nva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Kaṇva, fils de <a href="#entry-Umedhaatithi" class="skt-link">Medhātithi</a>, auteur d'hymnes védiques, vivant sur les bords de la <a href="#entry-Umaalinii" class="skt-link">Mālinī</a>; il est époux de <a href="#entry-Ugautamii" class="skt-link">Gautamī</a> et père adoptif de <a href="#entry-Uzakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Śakuntalā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kaṇva_1 <a href="#entry-Ukaazyapa" class="skt-link">Kāśyapa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de la dynastie <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a>, fondée par <a href="#entry-Uvaasudeva_2" class="skt-link">Vāsudeva_2</a> vers 71 ant.; elle succéda à la dynastie <a href="#entry-Uzufga" class="skt-link">Śuṅga</a> — <a href="#entry-ka_nva" class="skt-link">n.</a> péché — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(kaṇvāyate)</i> commettre un péché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_nvatama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṇvatama</i> [<a href="#entry--tama" class="skt-link">-tama</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_nvatama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a> en personne; un véritable <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_nva" class="skt-link">kaṇva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कष्</span> <i>kaṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kaṣati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>kaṣiṣyati</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kaṣita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ni</i><i>)</i> frotter, gratter, râper <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blesser; détruire — pr. md. <i>(kaṣate)</i> se gratter; démanger — ca. <i>(kāṣayati)</i> blesser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कष्ट</span> <i>kaṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ka_s" class="skt-link">kaṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṣṭā</i> mauvais, méchant; sévère, rigoureux; douloureux; dangereux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inaccessible — <a href="#entry-ka_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> mal, malheur; infortune, misère; difficulté; fatigue, stress — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(kaṣṭāyate)</i> s'efforcer de commettre le mal.<br> <i>kaṣṭam</i> hélas! malheur! misère!<br> <i>kanyāpitṛtvaṃ</i> <i>khalu</i> <i>nāma</i> <i>kaṣṭam</i> [PT.] Être père d'une fille est certes une calamité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_s_taafka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṣṭāṅka</i> [<a href="#entry-afka" class="skt-link">aṅka</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_s_taafka" class="skt-link">m.</a> degré de difficulté.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_s_ta" class="skt-link">kaṣṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कष</span> <i>kaṣa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ka_s" class="skt-link">kaṣ</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-ka_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṣā</i> qui gratte, qui frotte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_saaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कषाय</span> <i>kaṣāya</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_s" class="skt-link">kaṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_saaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_saaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_saayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṣāyā</i> astringent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> orange; rouge — <a href="#entry-ka_saaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_saaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] l'astringent, un type de goût [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> couleur safran (not. des vêtements monastiques) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> décoction herbale, infusion; potion; onguent, baume; gomme, résine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> impureté; défaut; péché.<br> <i>kaṣāyīkṛ</i> rougir, teindre en rouge.<br> <i>kaṣāyīkṛtalocana</i> a. m. n. <a href="#entry-ka_saayiik_rtalocanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṣāyīkṛtalocanā</i> qui a les yeux rougis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_saayita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कषायित</span> <i>kaṣāyita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-ka_s" class="skt-link">kaṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_saayita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_saayita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_saayitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṣāyitā</i> rougi; coloré, teint.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कषित</span> <i>kaṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ka_s" class="skt-link">kaṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṣitā</i> gratté, frotté; blessé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कट</span> <i>kaṭa</i> [pkt. <a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">kṛta</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hanche, croupe; flanc, côté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tempes de l'éléphant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natte, paille, tresse; chaume <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natte de repos, tapis; paillasse, matelas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_taak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṭākṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sa" class="skt-link">akṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_taak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> regard de côté.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_ta" class="skt-link">kaṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_taha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कटह</span> <i>kaṭaha</i> <a href="#entry-ka_taha" class="skt-link">m.</a> poêle, wok; chaudron, cocotte (semi-sphérique, avec des anses) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (objet renflé) carapace de tortue, bosse des tempes de l'éléphant, tumulus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> van.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṭaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> paille; natte — <a href="#entry-ka_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_taka" class="skt-link">n.</a> corde, bracelet, chaîne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vallée; versant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de Kaṭaka, capitale du <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a> (mod. Cuttack); sa divinité tutélaire est <a href="#entry-Uka_taka" class="skt-link">Kaṭaka</a> <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dii" class="skt-link">Caṇḍī</a> — <a href="#entry-ka_tikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṭikā</i> natte; paillasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_tapayaadi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṭapayādi</i> [<a href="#entry-_ta" class="skt-link">ṭa</a>-<a href="#entry-pa" class="skt-link">pa</a>-<a href="#entry-ya" class="skt-link">ya</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_tapayaadi" class="skt-link">m.</a> math. codage des chiffres par des phonèmes, attribué à <a href="#entry-Uvararuci_1" class="skt-link">Vararuci_1</a>; la série <i>kādinava</i> ‘<i>ka</i>’ ... ‘<i>ña</i>’ représente 1,2, ..., 9,0, la série <i>ṭādinava</i> ‘<i>ṭa</i>’ ... ‘<i>na</i>’ aussi, la série <i>pādipañca</i> ‘<i>pa</i>’ ... ‘<i>ma</i>’ les chiffres 1 à 5, la série <i>yādyaṣṭa</i> ‘<i>ya</i>’ ... ‘<i>ha</i>’ les chiffres 1 à 8; les voyelles codent 0; par ce codage toute syllabe code un chiffre, seule la dernière consonne compte; les syllabes successives d'un mot comptent les puissances de 10; ainsi <a href="#entry-jaya" class="skt-link">jaya</a> donne 8+1×10=18, le nombre de livres du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> et de sections de la <a href="#entry-Ugiitaa" class="skt-link">Gītā</a>; ce codage est aussi utilisé en musique carnatique pour déterminer l'échelle d'un mode musical [<a href="#entry-raaga" class="skt-link">rāga</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-ka_2" class="skt-link">ka_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_tapruu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṭaprū</i> [<a href="#entry-pruu" class="skt-link">prū</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_tapruu" class="skt-link">m.</a> ver <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> joueur, parieur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_tapuutanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṭapūtanā</i> [<a href="#entry-puutanaa" class="skt-link">pūtanā</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_tapuutanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de la démonesse Kaṭapūtanā, qui saisit de fièvre les jeunes enfants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कठ</span> <i>kaṭha</i> <a href="#entry-ka_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. nom du pays <a href="#entry-ka_tha" class="skt-link">kaṭha</a> (au nord du Pañjab) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. le peuple des <i>kaṭhās</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du sage Kaṭha, fondateur d'une école du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a>; il était élève de <a href="#entry-Uvaizampaayana" class="skt-link">Vaiśampāyana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_thina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कठिन</span> <i>kaṭhina</i> a. <a href="#entry-ka_thina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_thina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_thinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṭhinā</i> dur, rigide; opp. <a href="#entry-m_rdu" class="skt-link">mṛdu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inflexible, cruel; violent (douleur) — <a href="#entry-ka_thina" class="skt-link">n.</a> chaudron de cuisine en terre — <a href="#entry-ka_thinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṭhinā</i> sucre cristallisé; sucrerie — <a href="#entry-ka_thinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṭhinī</i> craie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_thopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṭhopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_thopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Kaṭhopaniṣad «Leçon de <a href="#entry-Uka_tha" class="skt-link">Kaṭha</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-ka_tha" class="skt-link">kaṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कटि</span> <i>kaṭi</i> <a href="#entry-ka_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> taille; hanches; fessier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_tii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कटी</span> <i>kaṭī</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-ka_ti" class="skt-link">kaṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> taille; hanches; fessier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_tita_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṭitaṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-ta_ta" class="skt-link">taṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_tita_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> taille, hanches.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_ti" class="skt-link">kaṭi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_tu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कटु</span> <i>kaṭu</i> a. <a href="#entry-ka_tu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_tu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_tu" class="skt-link">f.</a> âcre, amer, épicé; aigu (son); forte (odeur) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> violent, impétueux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_tuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṭuka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_tuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_tuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_tukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaṭukā</i> id. — <a href="#entry-ka_tuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> amertume; goût brûlant; force en goût, son, odeur — <a href="#entry-ka_tuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] l'épicé, un type de goût [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_tuviira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṭuvīra</i> [<a href="#entry-viira" class="skt-link">vīra</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_tuviira" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Capsicum annuum</i>, piment; syn. <a href="#entry-raktamarica" class="skt-link">raktamarica</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_tu" class="skt-link">kaṭu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क</span> <i>ka_1</i> pn. <a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kā</i> <a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>kim</i> interr. qui? lequel? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>quis</i>; ang. who; all. wer; fr. qui.<br> <i>kaścana</i> m. quelqu'un, quiconque.<br> <i>kaścid</i> m. quelqu'un, quiconque; un certain; quelque personne que ce soit.<br> <i>na</i> <i>kaścid</i> personne, aucun.<br> <i>na</i> <i>kaścid</i> <i>api</i> absolument personne.<br> <i>kecid</i> quelques.<br> <i>kecit</i> <i>...</i> <i>kecit</i> <i>...</i> certains ..., d'autres ...<br> <i>kānicitad</i> <i>...</i> <i>kānicitad</i> <i>...</i> certains ... d'autres [n.].<br> <i>kānicid</i> <i>...</i> <i>kānicid</i> <i>...</i> id.<br> <i>yat</i> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>ca</i> n'importe quel, n'importe quoi.<br> <i>kasmiṃścid</i> en quelque; en n'importe; n'importe où.<br> <i>yasyai</i> <i>kasyai</i> <i>ca</i> <i>devatāyai</i> à une divinité quelqu'elle soit.<br> <i>yāni</i> <i>kāni</i> <i>ca</i> <i>mitrāṇi</i> tous les amis sans exception.<br> <i>kecana</i> certains; quelques uns.<br> <i>ko</i> <i>'pi</i> quelqu'un, un certain; quelque chose, je ne sais quel.<br> <i>ko</i> <i>nāma</i> qui donc?.<br> <i>ko</i> <i>nu</i> qui vraiment?.<br> <i>ko</i> <i>vā</i> qui pourrait bien?.<br> <i>na</i> <i>ko</i> <i>'pi</i> rien, personne.<br> <i>kaḥ</i> <i>svid</i> n'importe qui.<br> <i>ko</i> <i>'yam</i> <i>āyāti</i> qui vient ici?.<br> <i>ko</i> <i>hariṃ</i> <i>nindet</i> qui pourrait blâmer Hari?.<br> <i>kān</i> <i>kān</i> lesquels d'entre eux?.<br> <i>ke</i> <i>mama</i> <i>dhanvino'nye</i> Que les archers adverses pourraient-ils contre moi?.<br> <i>ke</i> <i>āvām</i> <i>paritrātum</i> Comment pourriez-vous me secourir?.<br> <i>keṣāṃ</i> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>śāstram</i> <i>adhyayanīyam</i> Quel livre doit-il être lu par qui?.<br> <i>kasya</i> <i>sa</i> <i>putra</i> <i>iti</i> <i>kathyatām</i> Que l'on dise de qui il est le fils.<br> <i>kasya</i> <i>sa</i> <i>putro</i> <i>na</i> <i>jñāyate</i> On ne sait pas de qui il est le fils.<br> <i>kasmiṃścinnagare</i> <i>'vadadrājā</i> Le roi habitait dans une ville.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क</span> <i>ka_2</i> <a href="#entry-ka_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Ka, un nom de <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a> — <a href="#entry-ka_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> joie, bonheur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> eau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tête <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 1.<br> <i>kasya</i> <i>paridadāmi</i> soc. Je (le) confie à <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a> (formule prononcée lors de l'<a href="#entry-upanayana" class="skt-link">upanayana</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">का</span> <i>kā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] pn. <a href="#entry-kaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> interr. qui? quelle? — iic. part. péjorative.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काण्ड</span> <i>kāṇḍa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kha_n_d" class="skt-link">khaṇḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> segment; not. portion de tige de bambou entre deux nœuds <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. chapitre, division, section d'un ouvrage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. division d'un rite sacrificiel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> département, division, sujet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tronc, branche, tige <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gerbe, botte, tas; multitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> os du bras ou de la jambe; not. fémur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> roseau, bambou — <a href="#entry-kaa_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṇḍā</i> ifc. section de, etc. — <a href="#entry-kaa_n_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṇḍī</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_n_dabhagna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kāṇḍabhagna</i> [<a href="#entry-bhagna" class="skt-link">bhagna</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_n_dabhagna" class="skt-link">n.</a> fracture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_n_dalaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kāṇḍalāva</i> [<a href="#entry-laava" class="skt-link">lāva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaa_n_dalaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_n_dalaava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_n_dalaavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṇḍalāvī</i> qui coupe une branche; élagueur.<br> <a href="#entry-kaa_n_da" class="skt-link">kāṇḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_n_damaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kāṇḍamaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaa_n_damaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_n_damaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_n_damayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṇḍamayī</i> fait de roseau ou de bambou — <a href="#entry-kaa_n_damayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṇḍamayī</i> variété de luth.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काण</span> <i>kāṇa</i> a. <a href="#entry-kaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṇā</i> <a href="#entry-kaa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṇī</i> borgne; troué, perçé, perforé (par ⟨i.⟩).<br> <i>akṣṇā</i> <i>kāṇaḥ</i> borgne d'un œil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_naada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काणाद</span> <i>kāṇāda</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ka_naada" class="skt-link">kaṇāda</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaa_naada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_naada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_naadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṇādā</i> phil. [«issu de <a href="#entry-Uka_naada" class="skt-link">Kaṇāda</a>»] logique.<br> <i>kāṇādam</i> <i>pāṇinīyaṃ</i> <i>ca</i> <i>sarvaśāstropakārakam</i> La logique et la linguistique sont des prérequis à toute science.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_nva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काण्व</span> <i>kāṇva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ka_nva" class="skt-link">kaṇva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaa_nva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_nva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_nvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṇvā</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a> — <a href="#entry-kaa_nva" class="skt-link">m.</a> descendant de <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_nvazaakhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kāṇvaśākhā</i> [<a href="#entry-zaakhaa" class="skt-link">śākhā</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_nvazaakhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. recension de la <a href="#entry-vaajasaneyisa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">vājasaneyisaṃhitā</a> (<a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> blanc) attribuée à <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kaa_nva" class="skt-link">kāṇva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काष्ठ</span> <i>kāṣṭha</i> <a href="#entry-kaa_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> bois, not. de chauffage; morceau de bois, bûche, fagot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_s_thacchid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kāṣṭhacchid</i> [<a href="#entry-chid_2" class="skt-link">chid_2</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_s_thacchid" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. pivert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_s_thama_n_dapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kāṣṭhamaṇḍapa</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_n_dapa" class="skt-link">maṇḍapa</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_s_thama_n_dapa" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du Kāṣṭhamaṇḍapa «Pavillon de bois», temple en bois construit en 1596 à Kathmandu (mod. Durbar Square), et qui a donné son nom à la ville.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_s_thavikret_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kāṣṭhavikretṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-vikret_r" class="skt-link">vikretṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_s_thavikret_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> marchand de bois.<br> <a href="#entry-kaa_s_tha" class="skt-link">kāṣṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_saaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काषाय</span> <i>kāṣāya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ka_saaya" class="skt-link">kaṣāya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaa_saaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_saaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_saayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṣāyā</i> de couleur safran; orange; brun — <a href="#entry-kaa_saaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> vêtement (not. monastique) de couleur safran ou brun.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_thaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काठक</span> <i>kāṭhaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ka_tha" class="skt-link">kaṭha</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_thaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> habitant du <a href="#entry-ka_tha" class="skt-link">kaṭha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. le peuple des <i>kaṭhākās</i> — <a href="#entry-kaa_thaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kāṭhaka, recension du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> noir attribué à <a href="#entry-Uka_tha" class="skt-link">Kaṭha</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukaa_thakag_rhyasuutra" class="skt-link">Kāṭhakagṛhyasūtra</a>, <a href="#entry-Umaadyamikaa" class="skt-link">Mādyamikā</a> — a. <a href="#entry-kaa_thaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_thaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaa_thakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāṭhakā</i> hist. attribué à <a href="#entry-Uka_tha" class="skt-link">Kaṭha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_thakag_rhyasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kāṭhakagṛhyasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rhyasuutra" class="skt-link">gṛhyasūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_thakag_rhyasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kāṭhakagṛhyasūtra, recueil d'hymnes à usage domestique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_thakasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kāṭhakasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_thakasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Kāṭhakasaṃhitā, recueil des premiers textes <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kaa_thaka" class="skt-link">kāṭhaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_thina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काठिन</span> <i>kāṭhina</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ka_thina" class="skt-link">kaṭhina</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_thina" class="skt-link">n.</a> dûreté; rigidité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaa_thinya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काठिन्य</span> <i>kāṭhinya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ka_thina" class="skt-link">kaṭhina</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-kaa_thinya" class="skt-link">n.</a> dûreté; rigidité; sévérité; fermeté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> difficulté; obscurité (du style).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काच</span> <i>kāca</i> <a href="#entry-kaaca" class="skt-link">m.</a> verre; cristal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. perles de verre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaacama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kācamaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-kaacama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> cristal de roche; quartz.<br> <a href="#entry-kaaca" class="skt-link">kāca</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaacana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">काचन</span> <i>kācana</i> <a href="#entry-kaacana" class="skt-link">n.</a> ficelle pour tenir un paquet ou pour relier les feuillets d'un manuscrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaacanaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kācanaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kaacanaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-kaacana" class="skt-link">kācana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaadambara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कादम्बर</span> <i>kādambara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kadamba" class="skt-link">kadamba</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-kaadambara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaadambara" class="skt-link">n.</a> peau sur le lait ou la crème — f. cf. <a href="#entry-kaadambarii" class="skt-link">kādambarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaadambarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कादम्बरी</span> <i>kādambarī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-kaadambara" class="skt-link">kādambara</a>] <a href="#entry-kaadambarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> suc des fleurs du <a href="#entry-kadamba" class="skt-link">kadamba</a>; liqueur enivrante obtenue par distillation de leur suc; myth. <a href="#entry-Uaadyaa" class="skt-link">Ādyā</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a> en boit dans un crâne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> liqueur en gén. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la nymphe Kādambarī «l'Enivrante», fille du roi <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha" class="skt-link">Citraratha</a> et de la nymphe <a href="#entry-Umadiraa" class="skt-link">Madirā</a>; elle jura de n'épouser aucun garçon tant que durerait le chagrin de <a href="#entry-Umahaazvetaa" class="skt-link">Mahāśvetā</a> pleurant le suicide de <a href="#entry-Upu_n_dariika_2" class="skt-link">Puṇḍarīka_2</a>; lorsque le prince <a href="#entry-Ucandraapii_da_1" class="skt-link">Candrāpīḍa_1</a> lui rendit visite, ils tombèrent amoureux, mais ils durent attendre le dénouement de bien des drames pour connaître enfin l'amour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. conte romanesque [<a href="#entry-kathaa" class="skt-link">kathā</a>] éponyme de <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na_2" class="skt-link">Bāṇa_2</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> dont elle est une héroïne, dans le genre littéraire sanskrit de fictions merveilleuses [<a href="#entry-utprek_saa" class="skt-link">utprekṣā</a>]; ce labyrinthe d'intrigues, de rebondissements et retours en arrière, est un chef-d'œuvre d'histoires imbriquées et de tournures poétiques [<a href="#entry-alafkaarazaastra" class="skt-link">alaṅkāraśāstra</a>], notamment de métaphores [<a href="#entry-upamaa_2" class="skt-link">upamā_2</a>] dans sa description d'<a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a> et du lac <a href="#entry-Uacchoda" class="skt-link">Acchoda</a>; l'atmosphère érotique, l'ironie se dégageant des procédés dithyrambiques, et le suspense subtilement entretenu rendent ce roman captivant; <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na_2" class="skt-link">Bāṇa_2</a> composa de son vivant la première partie [<a href="#entry-Upuurvabhaaga" class="skt-link">Pūrvabhāga</a>]; la deuxième partie [<a href="#entry-Uuttarabhaaga" class="skt-link">Uttarabhāga</a>] fut complétée par son fils <a href="#entry-Ubhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">Bhūṣaṇa</a> suivant ses plans; cf. <a href="#entry-Upu_n_dariika_2" class="skt-link">Puṇḍarīka_2</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucandraapii_da_1" class="skt-link">Candrāpīḍa_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. fleur du <a href="#entry-kadamba" class="skt-link">kadamba</a> envoyée comme flèche du désir par <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> au roi <a href="#entry-Uzuudraka_2" class="skt-link">Śūdraka_2</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] np. de la Kādambarī_1, rivière du <a href="#entry-Ujambudviipa" class="skt-link">Jambudvīpa</a> issue d'un tronc de <a href="#entry-kadamba" class="skt-link">kadamba</a>, coulant vers l'Ouest.<br> <i>kādambarī</i> <i>rasajñānām</i> <i>āhāro'pi</i> <i>na</i> <i>rocate</i> L'amateur de la <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a> (ou de l'alcool de <a href="#entry-kadamba" class="skt-link">kadamba</a>) en oublie même de se nourrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaadambariikathaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kādambarīkathā</i> [<a href="#entry-kathaa" class="skt-link">kathā</a>] <a href="#entry-kaadambariikathaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. histoire de <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaadambariikathaasaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
