[RV.] np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Kakṣīvān <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a> «qui vit dans la brousse», fils de <a href="#entry-Udiirghatamaa" class="skt-link">Dīrghatamā</a> et d'une servante du roi <a href="#entry-Ubali" class="skt-link">Bali</a>, auteur d'hymnes du RV.; <a href="#entry-Uca_n_da_1" class="skt-link">Caṇḍa_1</a>-<a href="#entry-Uca_n_dakauzika" class="skt-link">Caṇḍakauśika</a> est son fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kak_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कक्ष्य</span> <i>kakṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-kak_sa" class="skt-link">kakṣa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kak_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kak_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kak_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kakṣyā</i> qui vit dans les buissons; qui habite la savane <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bien nourri — <a href="#entry-kak_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kakṣyā</i> ventre; circonférence, tour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> enclos; appartement; classe d'une école.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ककार</span> <i>kakāra</i> [<i>(ka)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-kakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>ka</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kakkola">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कक्कोल</span> <i>kakkola</i> var. <i>kaṅkola</i> <a href="#entry-kakkola" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kakkolii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kakkolī</i> natu. bot. <i>Myrtus pimenta</i>, myrte; ses baies sont utilisées pour parfumer les cheveux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kakubh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ककुभ्</span> <i>kakubh</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-kakud" class="skt-link">kakud</a>] <a href="#entry-kakubh" class="skt-link">f.</a> sommet, pic, cime <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> région du ciel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kakubh (variante de <a href="#entry-Ukakud" class="skt-link">Kakud</a>), personnifiant un quartier du ciel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. nom d'un mètre de 3 vers [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>], de longueurs 8,12, 8.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kakud">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ककुद्</span> <i>kakud</i> var. <i>kakut</i> <a href="#entry-kakud" class="skt-link">f.</a> colline, sommet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bosse de bovin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kakud, fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a> — <a href="#entry-kakuda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kakuda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>kakuda</i> sommet; chef; symbole de royauté (not. le parasol blanc).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kakutstha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kakutstha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] <a href="#entry-kakutstha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> Kakutstha «Qui se tient sur la bosse (du taureau incarné par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> lors d'une bataille)»; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] il est dit fils de <a href="#entry-Uzazaada" class="skt-link">Śaśāda</a>, et ancêtre de <a href="#entry-Uraghu" class="skt-link">Raghu</a> par son fils <a href="#entry-Uanenaa" class="skt-link">Anenā</a>; ou bien [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] il est dit fils de <a href="#entry-Ubhagiiratha" class="skt-link">Bhagīratha</a> et père de <a href="#entry-Uraghu" class="skt-link">Raghu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi de <a href="#entry-Uvizaalaa" class="skt-link">Viśālā</a> Kakutstha_1, fils de <a href="#entry-Usomadatta_1" class="skt-link">Somadatta_1</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Usumati" class="skt-link">Sumati</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kakud" class="skt-link">kakud</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kal">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्</span> <i>kal</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(kalayati)</i> pr. md. <i>(kalayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kalita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> inciter; exciter; porter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire, accomplir, produire; émettre (un son), murmurer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> considérer, observer, remarquer; compter, mesurer; connaître; croire — ca. <i>(kālayati)</i> inciter; mettre en avant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> poursuivre, pourchasser; persécuter.<br> <i>mūrchāṃ</i> <i>kal</i> s'évanouir.<br> <i>culukaṃ</i> <i>jalasya</i> <i>kal</i> boire une gorgée d'eau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल</span> <i>kala</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kal" class="skt-link">kal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalā</i> rauque, indistinct, faible <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inarticulé, bas; doux, mélodieux (voix) — f. cf. <a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कला</span> <i>kalā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-kala" class="skt-link">kala</a>] <a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> petite partie d'un tout, portion, élément, atome; seizième partie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> not. seizième partie du cercle lunaire, cf. <a href="#entry-candrakalaa" class="skt-link">candrakalā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> unité de temps (approx. 1 mn) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. 1 degré d'arc (not. du zodiaque) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. temps d'une syllabe en prosodie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. atome de matière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [ChU.] quartier d'une des quatre quadripartitions [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] du <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. seizième partie de la manifestation divine complète [<a href="#entry-puur_naavataara" class="skt-link">pūrṇāvatāra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> l'un des 64 arts; syn. <a href="#entry-afgavidyaa" class="skt-link">aṅgavidyā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-catu_h_sa_s_tikalaas" class="skt-link">catuḥṣaṣṭikalās</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la Kalā, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Uvaiyaakara_nasiddhaantama_njuu_saa" class="skt-link">Vaiyākaraṇasiddhāntamañjūṣā</a> par <a href="#entry-Ubaalambha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bālambhaṭṭa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalaakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalākāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-kalaakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> artiste.<br> <a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalaapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलाप</span> <i>kalāpa</i> [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>-<a href="#entry-aap" class="skt-link">āp</a>] <a href="#entry-kalaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«ce qui tient ensemble»] lien, faisceau, botte; carquois <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> queue de paon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> collection d'objets tenus ensemble; not. livre — <a href="#entry-kalaapii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalāpī</i> brassée d'herbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalaapin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalāpin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-kalaapin" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. paon.<br> <a href="#entry-kalaapa" class="skt-link">kalāpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलभ</span> <i>kalabha</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-karabha" class="skt-link">karabha</a>] <a href="#entry-kalabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jeune éléphant (de moins de 30 ans).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalabhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalabhaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kalabhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphanteau<br> <a href="#entry-kalabha" class="skt-link">kalabha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalacuri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलचुरि</span> <i>kalacuri</i> <a href="#entry-kalacuri" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de la dynastie Kalacuri ou Kalacūri; leur capitale était <a href="#entry-Umaahi_smatii" class="skt-link">Māhiṣmatī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_naraaja" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇarāja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalafka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलङ्क</span> <i>kalaṅka</i> [<a href="#entry-kal" class="skt-link">kal</a>-<a href="#entry-afka" class="skt-link">aṅka</a>] <a href="#entry-kalafka" class="skt-link">m.</a> tache; marque; marque de rouille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> marque d'infamie, blâme — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(kalaṅkayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kalaṅkita</i><i>)</i> tacher; diffamer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalafkita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलङ्कित</span> <i>kalaṅkita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kalafka" class="skt-link">kalaṅka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalafkita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalafkita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalafkitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalaṅkitā</i> taché; diffamé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलह</span> <i>kalaha</i> <a href="#entry-kalaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> dispute, querelle; rixe, combat <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tromperie; mensonge — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(kalahāyate)</i> se quereller.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalaha_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalahaṃsa</i> [<a href="#entry-ha_msa" class="skt-link">haṃsa</a>] <a href="#entry-kalaha_msa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalaha_msii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalahaṃsī</i> natu. zoo. <i>Anser anser</i>, oie.<br> <a href="#entry-kala" class="skt-link">kala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalakala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalakala</i> [red.] <a href="#entry-kalakala" class="skt-link">m.</a> bruit confus, brouhaha; not. grondement de foule.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलम</span> <i>kalama</i> [<a href="#entry-kal" class="skt-link">kal</a>] <a href="#entry-kalama" class="skt-link">m.</a> riz récolté en hiver <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> calame, plume en roseau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. np. du clan <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a> Kalama du pays <a href="#entry-Ukosala" class="skt-link">Kosala</a>, à l'époque de <a href="#entry-Ubimbisaara" class="skt-link">Bimbisāra</a>; ses membres habitaient la ville de Keśaputra; bd. <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>, qui y fit un sermon, leur enseigna de se méfier des dogmes établis, des écritures, des traditions et des idées reçues <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. stylo <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. καλαμος; lat. <i>calamus</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalamuuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalamūka</i> [<a href="#entry-muuka" class="skt-link">mūka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalamuuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalamuuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalamuukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalamūkā</i> sourd-muet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलन</span> <i>kalana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kal" class="skt-link">kal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalanā</i> qui produit, qui cause ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-kalanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalanā</i> incitation; production.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalandaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलन्दक</span> <i>kalandaka</i> <a href="#entry-kalandaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Funambulus palmarum</i>, écureuil indien (chipmunk) à 3 (ou 5) rayures; myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] il aida <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> à construire le pont vers <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a> en transportant des grains de sable; pour le remercier, <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> le bénit avec sa main, origine des rayures.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलत्र</span> <i>kalatra</i> <a href="#entry-kalatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> femme, épouse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> femelle; croupe; vagin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> citadelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalavifka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलविङ्क</span> <i>kalaviṅka</i> <a href="#entry-kalavifka" class="skt-link">m.</a> moineau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> coucou.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलश</span> <i>kalaśa</i> <a href="#entry-kalaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> vase, pot, coupe, cruche; syn. <a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> baratte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. symbole sacré du pot à eau associé à deux yeux divins; cf. <a href="#entry-a_s_tamafgala" class="skt-link">aṣṭamaṅgala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arch. pinacle d'un temple (en forme de vase) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kalasa</i> id. — <a href="#entry-kalazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalaśī</i> pot à eau, jarre; baratte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kalaśi</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. καλιξ; lat. <i>calix</i>; fr. calice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalevara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलेवर</span> <i>kalevara</i> <a href="#entry-kalevara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalevara" class="skt-link">n.</a> corps <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>cadaver</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्हण</span> <i>kalhaṇa</i> <a href="#entry-kalha_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a> Kalhaṇa, fils de <a href="#entry-Ucampaka" class="skt-link">Campaka</a>, poète et historien du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> du 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur de l'épopée dynastique <a href="#entry-Uraajatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">Rājataraṅgiṇī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलि</span> <i>kali</i> [<a href="#entry-kal" class="skt-link">kal</a>] <a href="#entry-kali" class="skt-link">m.</a> coup de dé perdant (de 1) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] sa personnification Kali «Mauvais sort», esprit mauvais qui aida <a href="#entry-Upu_skara" class="skt-link">Puṣkara</a> à gagner aux dés les possessions de son frère <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dispute, discorde; mauvais objet parmi plusieurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalifga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलिङ्ग</span> <i>kaliṅga</i> <a href="#entry-kalifga" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du pays Kaliṅga et de son peuple les <i>kaliṅgās</i>, sur la côte du Coromandel, s'étendant au sud de <a href="#entry-Uka_taka" class="skt-link">Kaṭaka</a>; l'empereur <a href="#entry-Uazoka" class="skt-link">Aśoka</a> le conquit au terme d'une guerre sanglante; sa capitale est <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>; mod. Orissa du Sud <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] <a href="#entry-Usahadeva" class="skt-link">Sahadeva</a> le conquit pour qu'il paye tribut à <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du roi Kaliṅga_1, fils de <a href="#entry-Udiirghatamaa" class="skt-link">Dīrghatamā</a> et <a href="#entry-Usude_s_naa" class="skt-link">Sudeṣṇā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलिका</span> <i>kalikā</i> [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>] <a href="#entry-kalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ de lune (unité de temps) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bouton (de fleur); bourgeon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalikaataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलिकाता</span> <i>kalikātā</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-kaaliigha_t_ta" class="skt-link">kālīghaṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-kalikaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Kalikātā au Bengale (mod. Calcutta).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलिल</span> <i>kalila</i> [<a href="#entry-kal" class="skt-link">kal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalilā</i> plein de, couvert de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> impénétrable — <a href="#entry-kalila" class="skt-link">n.</a> fourré; tas; embarras.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalinda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलिन्द</span> <i>kalinda</i> <a href="#entry-kalinda" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Mont Kalinda, d'où est issue la <a href="#entry-Uyamunaa" class="skt-link">Yamunā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. géo. np. du peuple et du pays des <i>kalindās</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलित</span> <i>kalita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kal" class="skt-link">kal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalitā</i> incité; conduit; produit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tenu (not. épée).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalivarjya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalivarjya</i> [<a href="#entry-varjya" class="skt-link">varjya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalivarjya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalivarjya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalivarjyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalivarjyā</i> soc. qui doit être évité en cet âge dégradé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> obsolète.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalivi_dambana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaliviḍambana</i> [<a href="#entry-vi_dambana" class="skt-link">viḍambana</a>] <a href="#entry-kalivi_dambana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du poème satirique Kaliviḍambana, dû à <a href="#entry-Uniilaka_n_tha_2" class="skt-link">Nīlakaṇṭha_2</a> <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a>; c'est une satire de la société, et not. des astrologues.<br> <a href="#entry-kali" class="skt-link">kali</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaliyuga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaliyuga</i> [<a href="#entry-yuga" class="skt-link">yuga</a>] <a href="#entry-kaliyuga" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ âge du monde, âge de fer; la tradition fixe son commencement à la mort présumée de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> en l'an 3102 ant.; son roi en est <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a>; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] les <a href="#entry-zuudraas" class="skt-link">śūdrās</a> y acquièrent le droit de pratiquer l'ascèse [<a href="#entry-tapasya" class="skt-link">tapasya</a>]; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] il est incarné par <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्क</span> <i>kalka</i> <a href="#entry-kalka" class="skt-link">m.</a> résidu; ordure, saleté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hypocrisie, fausseté, tromperie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalkin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्किन्</span> <i>kalkin</i> <a href="#entry-kalkin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kalkī, 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; il viendra détruire l'univers à la fin du <a href="#entry-kaliyuga" class="skt-link">kaliyuga</a>; on le représente comme un cavalier blanc sur un cheval noir, ou comme un cavalier noir sur un cheval noir (<a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a>) accompagné d'un cavalier blanc sur un cheval blanc (<a href="#entry-Ubalabhadra" class="skt-link">Balabhadra</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kalki</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalla_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्लट</span> <i>kallaṭa</i> <a href="#entry-kalla_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du phil. <a href="#entry-trika" class="skt-link">trika</a> du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> Kallaṭa <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, élève de <a href="#entry-Uvasugupta" class="skt-link">Vasugupta</a> (fin du 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); sous sa direction il composa les Spandakārikās.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kallola">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्लोल</span> <i>kallola</i> <a href="#entry-kallola" class="skt-link">m.</a> vague.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kallolinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kallolinī</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] torrent, rivière.<br> <a href="#entry-kallola" class="skt-link">kallola</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalmaa_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्माष</span> <i>kalmāṣa</i> a. <a href="#entry-kalmaa_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalmaa_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalmaa_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalmāṣā</i> bariolé, bigarré; taché, tacheté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalmaa_sapaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalmāṣapāda</i> [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-kalmaa_sapaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> Kalmāṣapāda «aux pieds tachés»; il est <a href="#entry-Usaudaasa" class="skt-link">Saudāsa</a> «fils du roi <a href="#entry-Usudaasa_1" class="skt-link">Sudāsa_1</a>» de la dynastie solaire, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Umitrasaha" class="skt-link">Mitrasaha</a>; il fut maudit par son <a href="#entry-purohita" class="skt-link">purohita</a> <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> pour avoir refusé la priorité à son fils <a href="#entry-Uzakti_1" class="skt-link">Śakti_1</a> et l'avoir frappé; <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> lui envoya le mauvais esprit <a href="#entry-Ukifkara" class="skt-link">Kiṃkara</a>; un jour, un brâhmane vint lui demander un repas de mouton; le roi fit tuer un homme, qu'il lui servit comme repas; le brâhmane le maudit, et le condamna à devenir vampire [<a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>]; il dévora les 100 fils de <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a>, puis tenta de dévorer <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> lui même, qui finit par l'exorciser; pendant sa malédiction [<a href="#entry-zaapa" class="skt-link">śāpa</a>], il avait surpris un couple brâhmane faisant l'amour, avait dévoré l'homme, et avait été maudit par la femme, le condamnant à la stérilité; il demanda à <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> de guérir la reine, qui devint enceinte de lui; comme elle n'avait toujours pas enfanté après 12 ans de grossesse, elle s'ouvrit le ventre avec une pierre coupante pour délivrer son fils <a href="#entry-Uazmaka_1" class="skt-link">Aśmaka_1</a>; celui-ci continua la dynastie; le roi <a href="#entry-Udiliipa_2" class="skt-link">Dilīpa_2</a> est son descendant; cf. <a href="#entry-Uprav_rddha" class="skt-link">Pravṛddha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kalmaa_sa" class="skt-link">kalmāṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्प्</span> <i>kalp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-k_lp" class="skt-link">kḷp</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(kalpayati)</i> pr. md. <i>(kalpayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kalpita</i><i>)</i> faire, fabriquer; ordonner, nommer (à une fonction).<br> <i>bhāgaśas</i> <i>kalp</i> diviser en parts.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्प</span> <i>kalpa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_lp" class="skt-link">kḷp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalpaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalpā</i> faisable, possible, praticable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> propre à, apte à, habile — <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> moyen, procédé; précepte, règle, loi; rituel — ifc. semblable à, qui s'approche de, presque; à peu près, approximativement; modèle de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. grand cycle cosmologique de la création, où l'Univers matériel est créé, se dégrade et est annihilé lors du <a href="#entry-kalpaanta" class="skt-link">kalpānta</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. période cosmologique d'un jour (demi-journée) de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, durée d'une création de l'Univers, valant 1000 grandes ères [<a href="#entry-mahaayuga" class="skt-link">mahāyuga</a>] (environ 4,32 milliards d'années); chaque <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a> se compose de 14 ères de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a> [<a href="#entry-manvantara" class="skt-link">manvantara</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-mahaakalpa" class="skt-link">mahākalpa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le rituel, un des 6 membres du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> [<a href="#entry-vedaafga" class="skt-link">vedāṅga</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> alternative; syn. <a href="#entry-pak_sa" class="skt-link">pakṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> investigation, recherche; détermination <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. traitement des malades, thérapie; préparation des remèdes, pharmacie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>corpus</i>; pali <i>kappa</i>.<br> <i>mṛtakalpa</i> presque mort.<br> <i>svakarmaṇi</i> <i>na</i> <i>kalpaḥ</i> incompétent dans sa fonction.<br> <i>yadā</i> <i>na</i> <i>śāsituṃ</i> <i>kalpaḥ</i> s'il est incapable de gouverner.<br> <i>bhikṣukalpaḥ</i> qui n'est pas tout-à-fait un moine.<br> <i>tṛṇakalpaḥ</i> qui consiste presqu'entièrement d'herbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpānta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. fin du Monde (fin du présent <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>); cf. <a href="#entry-Uananta" class="skt-link">Ananta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpaatita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpātita</i> [<a href="#entry-bhava" class="skt-link">bhava</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpaatita" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. jn. classe d'êtres célestes [<a href="#entry-vaimaanika" class="skt-link">vaimānika</a>] des ères passées.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpabhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpabhava</i> [<a href="#entry-bhava" class="skt-link">bhava</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpabhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. jn. classe d'êtres célestes [<a href="#entry-vaimaanika" class="skt-link">vaimānika</a>] de l'ère présente.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpadru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpadru</i> [<a href="#entry-dru_3" class="skt-link">dru_3</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpadru" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-kalpav_rk_sa" class="skt-link">kalpavṛkṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpadruma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpadruma</i> [<a href="#entry-druma" class="skt-link">druma</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpadruma" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-kalpav_rk_sa" class="skt-link">kalpavṛkṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpadrumakalikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpadrumakalikā</i> [<a href="#entry-kalikaa" class="skt-link">kalikā</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpadrumakalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> jn. np. de la Kalpadrumakalikā ou Kalpadrukalikā «Bourgeon de l'arbre d'abondance», qui forme le <a href="#entry-Ukalpasuutra" class="skt-link">Kalpasūtra</a> jaïn.<br> <a href="#entry-kalpadruma" class="skt-link">kalpadruma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्पन</span> <i>kalpana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_lp" class="skt-link">kḷp</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpana" class="skt-link">n.</a> formation, fabrication; invention; ornement, parure — f. cf. <a href="#entry-kalpanaa" class="skt-link">kalpanā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्पना</span> <i>kalpanā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-kalpana" class="skt-link">kalpana</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> fabrication, préparation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> disposition, arrangement, composition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> supposition, hypothèse; imagination, illusion, fiction; invention.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpanaagaurava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpanāgaurava</i> [<a href="#entry-gaurava" class="skt-link">gaurava</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpanaagaurava" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. complexité d'une description.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpanaalaaghava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpanālāghava</i> [<a href="#entry-laaghava" class="skt-link">lāghava</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpanaalaaghava" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. principe d'économie d'hypothèses <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. présupposition minimale d'une énonciation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpanaazakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpanāśakti</i> [<a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpanaazakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> pouvoir d'imagination; inventivité.<br> <a href="#entry-kalpanaa" class="skt-link">kalpanā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्पनीय</span> <i>kalpanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-k_lp" class="skt-link">kḷp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalpaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalpaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalpaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalpanīyā</i> qui peut être accompli; praticable, possible <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui doit être supposé; qui doit être arrangé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> pl. soc. recueil d'injonctions rituelles; les injonctions du Kalpasūtra forment le <a href="#entry-vedaafga" class="skt-link">vedāṅga</a> du rituel; cf. not. <a href="#entry-Ubaudhaayana" class="skt-link">Baudhāyana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. texte jn. contant les vies des 24 saints [<a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a>]; il est attribué à <a href="#entry-Ubhadrabaahu" class="skt-link">Bhadrabāhu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpasuutra_tiikaakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpasūtraṭīkākāra</i> [<a href="#entry-_tiikaakaara" class="skt-link">ṭīkākāra</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpasuutra_tiikaakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. commentateur du Kalpasūtra.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpataru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpataru</i> [<a href="#entry-taru" class="skt-link">taru</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpataru" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. cf. <a href="#entry-kalpav_rk_sa" class="skt-link">kalpavṛkṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du Kalpataru, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Ubhaamatii" class="skt-link">Bhāmatī</a>, dû à <a href="#entry-Uamalaananda" class="skt-link">Amalānanda</a> <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaabhoga" class="skt-link">Ābhoga</a>, <a href="#entry-Uparimala" class="skt-link">Parimala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpav_rk_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpavṛkṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rk_sa" class="skt-link">vṛkṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpav_rk_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. arbre magique qui accorde ce qu'on désire; c'est l'un des 5 arbres du paradis [<a href="#entry-svarga" class="skt-link">svarga</a>] d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-pa_ncav_rk_sa" class="skt-link">pañcavṛkṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. par ext. palmier à noix de coco.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्पित</span> <i>kalpita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kalp" class="skt-link">kalp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalpita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalpita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalpitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalpitā</i> imaginé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpitatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpitatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpitatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fiction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalpitav_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalpitavṛtta</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtta" class="skt-link">vṛtta</a>] <a href="#entry-kalpitav_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. roman; récit fictif.<br> <a href="#entry-kalpita" class="skt-link">kalpita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalu_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कलुष</span> <i>kaluṣa</i> a. <a href="#entry-kalu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalu_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalu_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaluṣā</i> sale, trouble, turbide, vaseux; impur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rauque (voix) — <a href="#entry-kalu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> buffle — <a href="#entry-kalu_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaluṣā</i> bufflesse — <a href="#entry-kalu_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> saleté, impureté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faute, péché — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(kaluṣayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kaluṣita</i><i>)</i> salir, polluer, contaminer — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(kaluṣāyate)</i> se troubler, se salir.<br> <i>kaluṣīkṛ</i> polluer, salir.<br> <i>krodeṇa</i> <i>kaluṣīkṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] étranglé de colère.<br> <i>kaluṣībhū</i> se troubler; se salir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्य</span> <i>kalya</i> [<a href="#entry-kal" class="skt-link">kal</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalyā</i> bien-portant, sain; vigoureux; fort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> agréable, de bon augure; instructif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prêt; préparé à ⟨loc. inf.⟩ — <a href="#entry-kalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> santé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aube, matin; hier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. géo. peuple des Kalyās.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalyaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कल्याण</span> <i>kalyāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kalya" class="skt-link">kalya</a><a href="#entry--ana" class="skt-link">-ana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalyaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalyaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalyaa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalyāṇī</i> beau, excellent, heureux, noble — voc. adr. terme de politesse pour une personne (présente ou absente) — <a href="#entry-kalyaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> heureux évènement; bonne fortune, bonheur, prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bien, vertu (opp. <a href="#entry-paapa" class="skt-link">pāpa</a>); avantage, salut <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. cérémonie de mariage; cf. <a href="#entry-Ujaambavatii" class="skt-link">Jāmbavatī</a> — <a href="#entry-kalyaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> mus. nom d'un mode musical [<a href="#entry-raaga" class="skt-link">rāga</a>] nocturne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de la dynastie Kalyāṇa des <a href="#entry-Ucaalukyaas" class="skt-link">Cālukyās</a> de l'Ouest; du nom de leur capitale <a href="#entry-Ukalyaa_nii" class="skt-link">Kalyāṇī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-Ukalyaa_na" class="skt-link">Kalyāṇa</a> <a href="#entry-Umalla" class="skt-link">Malla</a>, écrivain de cour du prince afghan d'Oudh Lāḍkhān, vers 1500; il est l'auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uanafgarafga" class="skt-link">Anaṅgaraṅga</a> — <a href="#entry-kalyaa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalyāṇī</i> vache <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de la ville de Kalyāṇī au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a> (mod. Basavakalyāṇa dans le district de Bidar); elle fut fondée par le roi <a href="#entry-Usomezvara_1" class="skt-link">Someśvara_1</a>; <a href="#entry-Ubasava_n_na" class="skt-link">Basavaṇṇa</a> y enseigna.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalyaa_naacaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalyāṇācāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">ācāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kalyaa_naacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kalyaa_naacaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kalyaa_naacaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kalyāṇācārā</i> qui se conduit bien.<br> <a href="#entry-kalyaa_na" class="skt-link">kalyāṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kalyaa_namitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kalyāṇamitra</i> [<a href="#entry-mitra" class="skt-link">mitra</a>] <a href="#entry-kalyaa_namitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> ami estimé; noble conseiller <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> personne de bon conseil; opp. <a href="#entry-paapamitra" class="skt-link">pāpamitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्</span> <i>kam</i> pft. <i>(cakame)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kānta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ni</i><i>)</i> désirer, aimer, être amoureux de — ca. md. <i>(kāmayate)</i> ca. <i>(kāmayati)</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inspirer de l'amour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire aimer.<br> <i>kāmaye</i> <i>dātum</i> je souhaite donner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kam_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्</span> <i>kam_1</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-ka_2" class="skt-link">ka_2</a>] part. cl. lui-même (souligne le mot précédent) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vraiment (explétif intensif en fin de <a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> quoi? (interrogatif de surprise ou de reproche en début de phrase) — iic. tête <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> eau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> félicité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kama_n_dalu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कमण्डलु</span> <i>kamaṇḍalu</i> <a href="#entry-kama_n_dalu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kama_n_dalu" class="skt-link">n.</a> pot à eau utilisé par les ascètes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kamala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कमल</span> <i>kamala</i> [<a href="#entry-kam" class="skt-link">kam</a><a href="#entry--ala" class="skt-link">-ala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kamala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kamala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaamalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kāmalā</i> rose pâle — <a href="#entry-kamala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kamala" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Nelumbium speciosum</i>, lotus rose — <a href="#entry-kamalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kamalā</i> phil. [tantr.] np. de Kamalā «Lotus», l'une des 10 puissances [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] de la Déesse [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>]; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Usiddhilak_smii" class="skt-link">Siddhilakṣmī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Uraajatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">Rājataraṅgiṇī</a>] np. de Kamalā_1, danseuse du temple de <a href="#entry-Ukaarttikeya" class="skt-link">Kārttikeya</a> à <a href="#entry-Upu_n_dravardhana" class="skt-link">Puṇḍravardhana</a>, qui séduisit le roi <a href="#entry-Ujayaapii_da" class="skt-link">Jayāpīḍa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kamalaabiija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kamalābīja</i> [<a href="#entry-biija" class="skt-link">bīja</a>] <a href="#entry-kamalaabiija" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> ‘<a href="#entry-zrii_m" class="skt-link">śrīṃ</a>’ d'évocation de <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kamala" class="skt-link">kamala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kamalaak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kamalākṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sa" class="skt-link">akṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kamalaak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kamalaak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kamalaak_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kamalākṣā</i> qui a des yeux de lotus; se dit not. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> — <a href="#entry-kamalaak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kamalākṣa «aux Yeux de lotus», fils de <a href="#entry-Utaaraka" class="skt-link">Tāraka</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Utripura" class="skt-link">Tripura</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kamalak_setra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kamalakṣetra</i> [<a href="#entry-k_setra" class="skt-link">kṣetra</a>] <a href="#entry-kamalak_setra" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Kamalayakṣetra «Champ de lotus», au Tamil Nadu (mod. Thiruvarur); elle était une capitale du royaume <a href="#entry-Ucola" class="skt-link">Cola</a>, puis au 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle fut disputée entre le royaume <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_dya" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍya</a> et l'empire <a href="#entry-Uhoysala" class="skt-link">Hoysala</a>; elle devint un centre culturel de l'empire <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a>; son temple de <a href="#entry-Utyaagaraaja" class="skt-link">Tyāgarāja</a> est célèbre pour son festival de char.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kamalaziila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kamalaśīla</i> [<a href="#entry-ziila" class="skt-link">śīla</a>] <a href="#entry-kamalaziila" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe Kamalaśīla, élève de <a href="#entry-Uzaantarak_sita" class="skt-link">Śāntarakṣita</a>; on lui doit le commentaire analytique [<a href="#entry-pa_njikaa" class="skt-link">pañjikā</a>] de sa <a href="#entry-Utattvasafgraha" class="skt-link">Tattvasaṃgraha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kambala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्बल</span> <i>kambala</i> <a href="#entry-kambala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kambala" class="skt-link">n.</a> couverture ou étoffe de laine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kamboja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kamboja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] <a href="#entry-kamboja" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. géo. véd. clan royal [<a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a>] Kamboja des <a href="#entry-zakaas" class="skt-link">śakās</a>; pays et peuple de l'extrême nord-ouest de l'Inde, au nord du <a href="#entry-Ugandhaara" class="skt-link">Gandhāra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> leur roi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cheval ou éléphant du pays.<br> <a href="#entry-kambu" class="skt-link">kambu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kambu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्बु</span> <i>kambu</i> <a href="#entry-kambu" class="skt-link">m.</a> coquille, coquillage, conque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bracelet de coquillage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kambugriiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kambugrīva</i> [<a href="#entry-griivaa" class="skt-link">grīvā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kambugriiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kambugriiva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kambugriivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kambugrīvā</i> qui a le cou plissé comme une conque; syn. <a href="#entry-kambuka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kambukaṇṭha</a> — <a href="#entry-kambugriiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. tortue.<br> <i>buddhimān</i> <i>nītimān</i> <i>vāgmī</i> <i>śrīmān</i> <i>śatrunibarhaṇaḥ</i> <i>vipulāṃsaḥ</i> <i>mahābāhuḥ</i> <i>kambugrīvaḥ</i> <i>mahāhanuḥ</i> <i>ca</i> [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] (<a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>) est intelligent, sagace, éloquent, glorieux, il extermine ses ennemis; il a de larges épaules, des bras puissants, sa nuque est musclée à l'égal d'une conque, et sa mâchoire puissante.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kambuka_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kambukaṇṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kambuka_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kambuka_n_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kambuka_n_thii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kambukaṇṭhī</i> qui a le cou plissé comme une conque; syn. <a href="#entry-kambugriiva" class="skt-link">kambugrīva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kamp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्प्</span> <i>kamp</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(kampate)</i> pft. <i>(cakampe)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kampita</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(kampitum)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>-kampya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>abhi</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> trembler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être effrayé — ca. <i>(kampayati)</i> agiter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kampa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्प</span> <i>kampa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kamp" class="skt-link">kamp</a>] <a href="#entry-kampa" class="skt-link">m.</a> tremblement, agitation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> peur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kampana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्पन</span> <i>kampana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kamp" class="skt-link">kamp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kampana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kampana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kampanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kampanā</i> tremblant, instable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui fait trembler; qui secoue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kampavaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kampavāta</i> [<a href="#entry-vaata" class="skt-link">vāta</a>] <a href="#entry-kampavaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. maladie de Parkinson; cf. <a href="#entry-aatmaguptaa" class="skt-link">ātmaguptā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kampa" class="skt-link">kampa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kampila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्पिल</span> <i>kampila</i> <a href="#entry-kampila" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Kampila; son ermitage se trouvait à <a href="#entry-Ukaampilya" class="skt-link">Kāmpilya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kampili">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्पिलि</span> <i>kampili</i> <a href="#entry-kampili" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du petit royaume de Kampili, créé par le commandant <a href="#entry-Uhoysala" class="skt-link">Hoysala</a> dissident Siṅgeya <a href="#entry-Unaayaka" class="skt-link">Nāyaka</a> (1280–1300), auquel succéda son fils <a href="#entry-Ukampilideva" class="skt-link">Kampilideva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kampilideva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kampilideva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-kampilideva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Kampilideva de <a href="#entry-Ukampili" class="skt-link">Kampili</a>; il s'allia avec le roi Rāmadeva de <a href="#entry-Udevagiri" class="skt-link">Devagiri</a> contre le roi <a href="#entry-Uhoysala" class="skt-link">Hoysala</a> Ballala III et accueillit le cousin dissident du Sultan de Delhi Muhammad ibn Tughluk; ce dernier vint à la tête de son armée, prit le fort de Kummata, s'empara de <a href="#entry-Ukampili" class="skt-link">Kampili</a> en 1327, et fit décapiter <a href="#entry-Ukampilideva" class="skt-link">Kampilideva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kampili" class="skt-link">kampili</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kampita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्पित</span> <i>kampita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kamp" class="skt-link">kamp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kampita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kampita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kampitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kampitā</i> tremblant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kampya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कम्प्य</span> <i>kampya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-kamp" class="skt-link">kamp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kampya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kampya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kampyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kampyā</i> à secouer; à écarter, à chasser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्</span> <i>kan</i> var. <i>kā</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-kam" class="skt-link">kam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(kāyati)</i> pft. <i>(cākana)</i> pft. md. <i>(cake)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(akānīt)</i> véd. être satisfait, apprécier; accepter, agréer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> briller; désirer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन</span> <i>kana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kan" class="skt-link">kan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanā</i> jeune; petit (substitué à <a href="#entry-alpa" class="skt-link">alpa</a> pour le compar. et le super.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कनक</span> <i>kanaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kan" class="skt-link">kan</a>] <a href="#entry-kanaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> or — ifc. en or — a. <a href="#entry-kanaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kanaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kanakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanakā</i> doré — <a href="#entry-kanakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanakā</i> myth. «Dorée», np. de l'une des 7 langues d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanakadhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanakadhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] <a href="#entry-kanakadhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Karakadharastava, hymne dévotionnel à <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a> attribué à <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanakagamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanakagamana</i> [<a href="#entry-gamana" class="skt-link">gamana</a>] <a href="#entry-kanakagamana" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de Kanakagamana «Pluie d'or», <a href="#entry-buddha" class="skt-link">buddha</a> mythique de la 23⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ vie antérieure [<a href="#entry-jaataka" class="skt-link">jātaka</a>] de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>, deuxième de notre ère [<a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>]; une pluie d'or tomba sur l'Inde à sa naissance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>Konagamana</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanakam_rga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanakamṛga</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a>] <a href="#entry-kanakam_rga" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] daim d'or, en lequel se métamorphosa <a href="#entry-Umaariica" class="skt-link">Mārīca</a> pour attirer <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-maayaam_rga" class="skt-link">māyāmṛga</a>, <a href="#entry-hemam_rga" class="skt-link">hemamṛga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanakamaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanakamaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kanakamaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kanakamaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kanakamayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanakamayī</i> d'or.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanakaprabhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanakaprabhā</i> [<a href="#entry-prabhaa_2" class="skt-link">prabhā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-kanakaprabhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> «Splendeur de l'or», np. de femme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanakastambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanakastambha</i> [<a href="#entry-stambha" class="skt-link">stambha</a>] <a href="#entry-kanakastambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> colonne d'or.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanakastambharucira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanakastambharucira</i> [<a href="#entry-rucira" class="skt-link">rucira</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kanakastambharucira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kanakastambharucira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kanakastambharuciraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanakastambharucirā</i> qui brille avec ses colonnes d'or.<br> <a href="#entry-kanakastambha" class="skt-link">kanakastambha</a> <br> <a href="#entry-kanaka" class="skt-link">kanaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanakasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanakasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-kanakasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> chaîne en or.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कनत्</span> <i>kanat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-kan" class="skt-link">kan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kanat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kanat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kanantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanantī</i> brillant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्द</span> <i>kanda</i> <a href="#entry-kanda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kanda" class="skt-link">n.</a> bulbe, oignon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kandala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्दल</span> <i>kandala</i> <a href="#entry-kandala" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. fleur de la <a href="#entry-kandalii" class="skt-link">kandalī</a> — <a href="#entry-kandalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kandalī</i> natu. plante à fleurs blanches abondantes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kandara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्दर</span> <i>kandara</i> [<a href="#entry-kam_1" class="skt-link">kam_1</a>-<a href="#entry-dara" class="skt-link">dara</a>] <a href="#entry-kandara" class="skt-link">n.</a> caverne, ravin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kandarpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kandarpa</i> [<a href="#entry-darpa" class="skt-link">darpa</a>] <a href="#entry-kandarpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> amour; désir sexuel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kandarpa «Désir fou», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kandarpaketu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kandarpaketu</i> [<a href="#entry-ketu" class="skt-link">ketu</a>] <a href="#entry-kandarpaketu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uhitopadeza" class="skt-link">Hitopadeśa</a>] np. de Kandarpaketu «Qui a l'amour pour bannière», roi des génies célestes [<a href="#entry-Uvidyaadharaas" class="skt-link">Vidyādharās</a>]; il est fils de <a href="#entry-Ujiimuutaketu" class="skt-link">Jīmūtaketu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du prince charmant Kandarpaketu_1 «Champion de l'amour», héros du roman d'amour <a href="#entry-Uvaasavadattaa" class="skt-link">Vāsavadattā</a> de <a href="#entry-Usubandhu" class="skt-link">Subandhu</a>; parfait fils du parfait roi <a href="#entry-Ucintaama_ni_2" class="skt-link">Cintāmaṇi_2</a>, il vit dans un rêve érotique la princesse <a href="#entry-Uvaasavadattaa_1" class="skt-link">Vāsavadattā_1</a>; quittant son palais avec son ami <a href="#entry-Umakaranda" class="skt-link">Makaranda</a>, il rencontra un perroquet qui le mena vers sa promise qui avait fait le rêve symétrique.<br> <a href="#entry-kandarpa" class="skt-link">kandarpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kandhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṃdhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_mdhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui porte la tête»] cou <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [«qui contient l'eau»] océan.<br> <i>valitakaṃdhara</i> qui a le cou incliné.<br> <a href="#entry-kam_1" class="skt-link">kam_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanduka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्दुक</span> <i>kanduka</i> <a href="#entry-kanduka" class="skt-link">m.</a> balle (à jouer); ballon.<br> <i>kandukena</i> <i>krīḍ</i> jouer à la balle, au ballon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kandukakrii_daa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kandukakrīḍā</i> [<a href="#entry-krii_daa" class="skt-link">krīḍā</a>] <a href="#entry-kandukakrii_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> jeu de balle, de ballon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. thème iconographique de la jeune femme jouant à la balle.<br> <a href="#entry-kanduka" class="skt-link">kanduka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kani_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कनिष्ठ</span> <i>kaniṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-kana" class="skt-link">kana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kani_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kani_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kani_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaniṣṭhā</i> le plus jeune, le cadet; opp. <a href="#entry-jye_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyeṣṭha</a> — <a href="#entry-kani_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaniṣṭhā</i> auriculaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kani_s_thaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaniṣṭhaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kani_s_thaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kani_s_thaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kani_s_thikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaniṣṭhikā</i> le plus petit — <a href="#entry-kani_s_thikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaniṣṭhikā</i> petit doigt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kani_s_thaprathama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaniṣṭhaprathama</i> [<a href="#entry-prathama" class="skt-link">prathama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kani_s_thaprathama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kani_s_thaprathama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kani_s_thaprathamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaniṣṭhaprathamā</i> ayant en premier le plus petit.<br> <a href="#entry-kani_s_tha" class="skt-link">kaniṣṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kani_ska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कनिष्क</span> <i>kaniṣka</i> <a href="#entry-kani_ska" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Kaniṣka_1 qui fonda la dynastie <a href="#entry-Uku_saa_na" class="skt-link">Kuṣāṇa</a> en Inde du Nord au début du 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; sa capitale était <a href="#entry-Upuru_sapura" class="skt-link">Puruṣapura</a> (mod. Peshawar); il favorisa l'expansion du bouddhisme; il fonda l'ère <a href="#entry-zaka" class="skt-link">śaka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Kaniṣka_2, roi <a href="#entry-Uku_saa_na" class="skt-link">Kuṣāṇa</a> qui régna vers (226–240).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaniiyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कनीयस्</span> <i>kanīyas</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-kana" class="skt-link">kana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaniiyas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaniiyas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaniiyasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanīyasī</i> plus jeune, cadet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> moindre; insignifiant — <a href="#entry-kaniiyasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanīyasī</i> sœur cadette de l'épouse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanna_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्नड</span> <i>kannaḍa</i> <a href="#entry-kanna_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. pays et peuple Kannaḍa du <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>, de langue dravidienne; corrompu en Kanara par les portugais pour désigner sa bande côtière <a href="#entry-Ukaraavalii" class="skt-link">Karāvalī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्त</span> <i>kanta</i> var. <i>kaṃta</i> [<a href="#entry-kam" class="skt-link">kam</a><a href="#entry--ta" class="skt-link">-ta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kantā</i> heureux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanthaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्था</span> <i>kanthā</i> <a href="#entry-kanthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> guenille, chiffon — <a href="#entry-kantha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>kantha</i> ifc. ville <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>cento</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्ति</span> <i>kanti</i> [<a href="#entry-kam" class="skt-link">kam</a><a href="#entry--ti" class="skt-link">-ti</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kanti" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kanti" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> heureux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kantu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्तु</span> <i>kantu</i> [<a href="#entry-kam" class="skt-link">kam</a><a href="#entry--tu" class="skt-link">-tu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kantu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kantu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kantu" class="skt-link">f.</a> heureux — <a href="#entry-kantu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kantu «Heureux», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्य</span> <i>kanya</i> [super. <a href="#entry-kana" class="skt-link">kana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kanya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kanya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kanyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanyā</i> le plus petit; opp. <a href="#entry-uttama" class="skt-link">uttama</a> — <a href="#entry-kanyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanyā</i> jeune fille, vierge; femme; fille; femelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. signe zodiacal [<a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>] de la Vierge.<br> <i>devakanyā</i> jeune déesse.<br> <i>rājakanyā</i> princesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कन्या</span> <i>kanyā</i> iic. f. de <a href="#entry-kanya" class="skt-link">kanya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanyaakubja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanyākubja</i> [<a href="#entry-kubja" class="skt-link">kubja</a>] <a href="#entry-kanyaakubja" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-Ukanyakubja" class="skt-link">Kanyakubja</a> ou Kanyākubja, ancien royaume fondé par <a href="#entry-Uamaavasu" class="skt-link">Amāvasu</a>; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] <a href="#entry-Ugaadhi" class="skt-link">Gādhi</a>, puis son fils <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a>, en furent rois — <a href="#entry-kanyaakubjaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kanyākubjā</i> géo. sa capitale Kanyākubjā, très grande ville sur le Gange; elle doit son nom «Fille Bossue» à la légende du roi <a href="#entry-Ukuzanaabha" class="skt-link">Kuśanābha</a>, dont les 100 filles furent rendues bossues par <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a> pour n'avoir pas cédé à ses désirs; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Umahodayaa" class="skt-link">Mahodayā</a>; elle était renommée pour ses parfums et le savoir de ses brâhmanes; elle fut la capitale de <a href="#entry-Uyazovarmaa" class="skt-link">Yaśovarmā</a> au 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; elle fut ensuite l'enjeu de luttes entre les <a href="#entry-Uraa_s_trakuu_taas" class="skt-link">Rāṣṭrakūṭās</a> du Deccan, les <a href="#entry-Upratiihaaraas" class="skt-link">Pratīhārās</a> à l'Ouest et les <a href="#entry-Upaalaas" class="skt-link">Pālās</a> à l'Est; elle fut abandonnée après les raids de Mahmud de Gazni au 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il n'en reste qu'un immense champ de ruines près de Kannauj, à 80 km au nord-ouest de Kanpur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanyaakumaarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanyākumārī</i> [<a href="#entry-kumaarii" class="skt-link">kumārī</a>] <a href="#entry-kanyaakumaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-kanyakumaarii" class="skt-link">kanyakumārī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanyaapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanyāpura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-kanyaapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. appartement des femmes; harem.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanyaaratna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanyāratna</i> [<a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">ratna</a>] <a href="#entry-kanyaaratna" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«un bijou de fille»] jolie fille, beauté.<br> <a href="#entry-kanyaa" class="skt-link">kanyā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanyakaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanyakā</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kanyakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> jeune fille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanyakubja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanyakubja</i> var. <i>kanyākubja</i> [<a href="#entry-kubja" class="skt-link">kubja</a>] <a href="#entry-kanyakubja" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. du Kanyakubja, ancien royaume fondé par <a href="#entry-Uamaavasu" class="skt-link">Amāvasu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukanyaakubja" class="skt-link">Kanyākubja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kanyakumaarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kanyakumārī</i> var. <i>kanyākumārī</i> [<a href="#entry-kumaarii" class="skt-link">kumārī</a>] <a href="#entry-kanyakumaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Kanyakumārī ou Kanyākumārī «Jeune fille», épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>-<a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. ville de pélerinage du Tamil Nadu, à l'extrême Sud de l'Inde; on y vénère <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> (mod. Cap Comorin).<br> <a href="#entry-kanya" class="skt-link">kanya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapa_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कपट</span> <i>kapaṭa</i> <a href="#entry-kapa_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kapa_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> tromperie, fraude — abl. <i>kapaṭāt</i> ifc. sous les apparences de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapa_taprabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapaṭaprabandha</i> [<a href="#entry-prabandha" class="skt-link">prabandha</a>] <a href="#entry-kapa_taprabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> fraudes en série; machination, conspiration.<br> <a href="#entry-kapa_ta" class="skt-link">kapaṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapaa_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कपाट</span> <i>kapāṭa</i> <a href="#entry-kapaa_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> vantail; porte (de temple, de ville).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कपाल</span> <i>kapāla</i> <a href="#entry-kapaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kapaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> coupe, bol, écuelle; écuelle à aumônes; couvercle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> crâne, tête — <a href="#entry-kapaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> coquille d'œuf — <a href="#entry-kapaalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kapālī</i> soc. calotte crânienne utilisée comme bol d'aumônes par un ascète <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>caput</i>; fr. cap.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapaalabhaatii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapālabhātī</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaati" class="skt-link">bhāti</a>] <a href="#entry-kapaalabhaatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] exercice alternant souffle et rétention du souffle; exhalation forcée par à-coups.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapaalakuhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapālakuhara</i> [<a href="#entry-kuhara" class="skt-link">kuhara</a>] <a href="#entry-kapaalakuhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> cavité palatale; cf. <a href="#entry-khecariimudraa" class="skt-link">khecarīmudrā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapaalavrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapālavrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] <a href="#entry-kapaalavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [«vœu du crâne»] comportement transgressif d'ascètes shivaïstes se vêtant d'os et autres impuretés, accompagnés d'un chien noir, et consommant de l'alcool en excès.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapaalezvaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapāleśvāra</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-kapaalezvaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du temple de Kapāleśvāra, situé à mod. Mylapore, au sud de Chennai; myth. son <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> y fut établi par <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> pour se repentir après que <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> lui ait coupé sa 5 ème tête; le temple d'origine fut construit par les <a href="#entry-Upallavaas" class="skt-link">Pallavās</a> au 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, puis détruit par les portugais; le temple actuel date du 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il est le siège de nombreux festivals.<br> <a href="#entry-kapaala" class="skt-link">kapāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapaalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapālin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-kapaalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kapālī «Porteur de crâne», aspect de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>, l'un des 11 souffles divins [<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> en ascète mendiant l'aumône avec une calotte crânienne — <a href="#entry-kapaalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kapālinī</i> myth. np. de Kapālinī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a> «Qui porte la guirlande de crânes».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaparda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कपर्द</span> <i>kaparda</i> <a href="#entry-kaparda" class="skt-link">m.</a> coquille, coquillage; not. zoo. <i>Cypraea moneta</i>, dont le coquillage est utilisé comme monnaie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. cauri.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapardin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapardin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-kapardin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kapardī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «aux cheveux nattés en spirale».<br> <a href="#entry-kaparda" class="skt-link">kaparda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कफ</span> <i>kapha</i> <a href="#entry-kapha" class="skt-link">m.</a> le phlegme, l'une des trois humeurs [<a href="#entry-trido_sa" class="skt-link">tridoṣa</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-kha_ta" class="skt-link">khaṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-zle_sman" class="skt-link">śleṣman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapho_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कफोणि</span> <i>kaphoṇi</i> <a href="#entry-kapho_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kapho_ni" class="skt-link">f.</a> coude.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कपि</span> <i>kapi</i> <a href="#entry-kapi" class="skt-link">m.</a> singe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kapi, un <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> parmi les <a href="#entry-afgirasas" class="skt-link">aṅgirasas</a> — <a href="#entry-kapi" class="skt-link">f.</a> var. <i>kapī</i> singe femelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. κηπος; ang. ape.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapi_s_thala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapiṣṭhala</i> [<a href="#entry-sthala" class="skt-link">sthala</a>] <a href="#entry-kapi_s_thala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Kapiṣṭhala.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapi_s_thalasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapiṣṭhalasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-kapi_s_thalasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Kapiṣṭhalasaṃhitā, texte véd.<br> <a href="#entry-kapi_s_thala" class="skt-link">kapiṣṭhala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapikacchu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kapikacchu</i> [<a href="#entry-kacchu" class="skt-link">kacchu</a>] <a href="#entry-kapikacchu" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Mucuna pruriens</i>, pois mascate; syn. <a href="#entry-aatmaguptaa" class="skt-link">ātmaguptā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>kapikacchū</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कपिल</span> <i>kapila</i> [<a href="#entry-kapi" class="skt-link">kapi</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kapila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kapila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kapilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kapilā</i> brun, rougeâtre, roux [«de la couleur des singes»] — <a href="#entry-kapila" class="skt-link">m.</a> la couleur brun; animal brun <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Kapila «le Roux»; il réduisit en cendres d'un regard les 60000 fils du roi <a href="#entry-Usagara" class="skt-link">Sagara</a> pour avoir troublé sa méditation; il est considéré comme fondateur du systême <a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>; on dit qu'il est une manifestation de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, ou fils du <a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a> <a href="#entry-Ukardama" class="skt-link">Kardama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kapilavastu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
