    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कस्मात्</span> <i>kasmāt</i> [abl. <a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] adv. pourquoi? comment? d'où?<br> <i>kasmād</i> <i>agacchasi</i> d'où viens tu?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kastuurii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कस्तूरी</span> <i>kastūrī</i> <a href="#entry-kastuurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> musc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kastuuriigandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kastūrīgandha</i> [<a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kastuuriigandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kastuuriigandha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kastuuriigandhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kastūrīgandhī</i> musqué — <a href="#entry-kastuuriigandhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kastūrīgandhī</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Kastūrīgandhī «au Parfum de musc», épith. de <a href="#entry-Usatyavatii" class="skt-link">Satyavatī</a> après que <a href="#entry-Uparaazara" class="skt-link">Parāśara</a> eut changé son odeur de poisson en odeur de musc; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugandhavatii" class="skt-link">Gandhavatī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kastuuriim_rga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kastūrīmṛga</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a>] <a href="#entry-kastuuriim_rga" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. cerf musqué.<br> <a href="#entry-kastuurii" class="skt-link">kastūrī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kastuurikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कस्तूरिका</span> <i>kastūrikā</i> [<a href="#entry-kastuurii" class="skt-link">kastūrī</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kastuurikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. zoo. cerf musqué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> musc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kasun">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कसुन्</span> <i>kasun</i> nota. gram. <a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a> suffixe ‘<i>as</i>’ utilisé pour l'infinitif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कस्य</span> <i>kasya</i> [g. <a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] ind. de qui.<br> <i>kasya</i> <i>cit</i> adv. de qui que ce soit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kasyaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कस्याम्</span> <i>kasyām</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-kaa" class="skt-link">kā</a>] ind. dans quelque (f.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kasyaa_ncid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kasyāñcid</i> [<a href="#entry-cid_1" class="skt-link">cid_1</a>] adv. dans quelque (f.) que ce soit.<br> <i>kasyāñcidavasthāyām</i> dans quelque situation que ce soit.<br> <a href="#entry-kasyaam" class="skt-link">kasyām</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कतक</span> <i>kataka</i> <a href="#entry-kataka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Strychnos potatorum</i>, arbuste aux fruits de couleur orange aux vertus médicinales; la poudre de ses graines sert à purifier l'eau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-katama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कतम</span> <i>katama</i> [super. <a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] pn. <a href="#entry-katama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-katama" class="skt-link">n.</a> interr. lequel? qui? — <a href="#entry-katamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>katamā</i> laquelle?<br> <i>katamaś</i> <i>cana</i> aucun (dans phrase négative).<br> <i>katamo</i> <i>'pi</i> id.<br> <i>yatamat</i> <i>katamac</i> <i>ca</i> n'importe lequel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-katara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कतर</span> <i>katara</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] pn. <a href="#entry-katara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-katara" class="skt-link">n.</a> interr. lequel (des deux)? — <a href="#entry-kataraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>katarā</i> laquelle (des deux)?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kath">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कथ्</span> <i>kath</i> [vn. <i>kathā</i>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(kathayati)</i> impft. <i>(akathayat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(acakathat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kathita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> [«donner le comment»] dire, raconter, exposer; parler, appeler; admettre — ps. <i>(kathyate)</i> être considéré comme, se reconnaître comme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. quote.<br> <i>uttaraṃ</i> <i>kath</i> dire la réponse, donner la solution.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कथा</span> <i>kathā</i> [<a href="#entry-katham" class="skt-link">katham</a>] <a href="#entry-kathaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> explication, discours; récit, histoire (le «comment» d'un événement); conte, fable, geste <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le fait de parler de; entretien, conversation; récitation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>kathāsaritsāgara</i>] lit. np. de Kathā, la Grande Histoire.<br> <i>kā</i> <i>kathā</i> que dire de ⟨g. loc.⟩? à plus forte raison; sans parler de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathaachala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kathāchala</i> var. <i>kathācchala</i> [<a href="#entry-chala" class="skt-link">chala</a>] <a href="#entry-kathaachala" class="skt-link">n.</a> semblant d'histoire, couverture; enseignement déguisé en fable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathaapii_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kathāpīṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-pii_tha" class="skt-link">pīṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-kathaapii_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. [«origine de la Grande Histoire»] premier livre du <a href="#entry-Ukathaasaritsaagara" class="skt-link">Kathāsaritsāgara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathaasaritsaagara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kathāsaritsāgara</i> [<a href="#entry-sarit" class="skt-link">sarit</a>-<a href="#entry-saagara" class="skt-link">sāgara</a>] <a href="#entry-kathaasaritsaagara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Kathāsaritsāgara «Océan des fleuves des contes», version de la <a href="#entry-Ub_rhatkathaa" class="skt-link">Bṛhatkathā</a> due à <a href="#entry-Usomadeva" class="skt-link">Somadeva</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>; ce recueil de contes de 24000 vers a inspiré les Contes des Mille et Une Nuits; il contient not. la <a href="#entry-Uvetaalapa_ncavi_mzatikaa" class="skt-link">Vetālapañcaviṃśatikā</a>; il est divisé en 18 sections [<a href="#entry-lambaka" class="skt-link">lambaka</a>] et 124 chapitres [<a href="#entry-tarafga" class="skt-link">taraṃga</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathaavataara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kathāvatāra</i> [<a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a>] <a href="#entry-kathaavataara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. incarnation de la Grande Histoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कथक</span> <i>kathaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kath" class="skt-link">kath</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kathaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kathaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kathakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kathakā</i> qui raconte, qui récite — <a href="#entry-kathaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> narrateur; conteur; récitateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> locuteur d'un monologue; acteur principal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. membre de la suite de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-katham">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कथम्</span> <i>katham</i> adv. interr. comment? de quelle manière?<br> <i>katham</i> <i>api</i> adv. à peine, difficilement; en quelque sorte.<br> <i>kathaṃ</i> <i>cana</i> adv. en quelque façon; tant bien que mal, peu.<br> <i>kathaṃ</i> <i>cit</i> adv. à peine, difficilement; en quelque sorte.<br> <i>kathaṃ</i> <i>nu</i> adv. comment donc?<br> <i>katham</i> <i>etat</i> comment cela?<br> <i>kathaṃ</i> <i>rūpa</i> de quelle forme?<br> <i>kathaṃ</i> <i>vīrya</i> de quelle force?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कथन</span> <i>kathana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kath" class="skt-link">kath</a>] <a href="#entry-kathana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de raconter, récit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कथनीय</span> <i>kathanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-kath" class="skt-link">kath</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kathaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kathaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kathaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kathanīyā</i> qui mérite d'être raconté; à mentionner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathayitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कथयितव्य</span> <i>kathayitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-kath" class="skt-link">kath</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kathayitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kathayitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kathayitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kathayitavyā</i> qui mérite d'être raconté; à mentionner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कथित</span> <i>kathita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kath" class="skt-link">kath</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kathita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kathita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kathitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kathitā</i> dit, mentionné, raconté — <a href="#entry-kathita" class="skt-link">n.</a> récit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kathodghaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kathodghāta</i> [<a href="#entry-udghaata" class="skt-link">udghāta</a>] <a href="#entry-kathodghaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [théâtre] ouverture d'une pièce par un personnage qui reprend à son compte les paroles du prologue par le <a href="#entry-suutradhaara" class="skt-link">sūtradhāra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kathaa" class="skt-link">kathā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कति</span> <i>kati</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] pn. <a href="#entry-kati" class="skt-link">*</a> num. interr. combien? combien de? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>quot</i>; fr. quotidien.<br> <i>kati</i> <i>cid</i> quelques, plusieurs.<br> <i>katyapi</i> quelques, plusieurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-katipaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कतिपय</span> <i>katipaya</i> [<a href="#entry-kati" class="skt-link">kati</a>] a. <a href="#entry-katipaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-katipaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-katipayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>katipayī</i> quelques, quelques uns; plusieurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-katth">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कत्थ्</span> <i>katth</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(katthate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>katthita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> se vanter, se glorifier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> louer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blâmer, mépriser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-katthita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कत्थित</span> <i>katthita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-katth" class="skt-link">katth</a>] a. <a href="#entry-katthita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-katthita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-katthitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>katthitā</i> vantard — <a href="#entry-katthita" class="skt-link">n.</a> vantardise.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौण्ड</span> <i>kauṇḍa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ku_n_da" class="skt-link">kuṇḍa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kau_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauṇḍā</i> relatif au pot — <a href="#entry-kau_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kauṇḍa <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukau_n_dabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Kauṇḍabhaṭṭa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_n_dabha_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kauṇḍabhaṭṭa</i> <a href="#entry-kau_n_dabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kauṇḍabhaṭṭa, grammairien du 17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, neveu de <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_toji" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭoji</a> <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a>; il est l'auteur du traité de philosophie de la langue <a href="#entry-Uvaiyaakara_nabhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">Vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇa</a>, et de son résumé le <a href="#entry-Uvaiyaakara_nabhuu_sa_nasaara" class="skt-link">Vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kau_n_da" class="skt-link">kauṇḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_n_dinya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौण्डिन्य</span> <i>kauṇḍinya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ku_n_dina" class="skt-link">kuṇḍina</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kau_n_dinya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_n_dinya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_n_dinyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauṇḍinyā</i> originaire de <a href="#entry-Uku_n_dina_1" class="skt-link">Kuṇḍina_1</a> — <a href="#entry-kau_n_dinya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kauṇḍinya «originaire de <a href="#entry-Uku_n_dina_1" class="skt-link">Kuṇḍina_1</a>», auteur du <a href="#entry-Upa_ncaarthabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Pañcārthabhāṣya</a> (4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩-5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_siitaki">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौषीतकि</span> <i>kauṣītaki</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ku_siitaka" class="skt-link">kuṣītaka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kau_siitaki" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_siitaki" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_siitaki" class="skt-link">f.</a> relatif à <a href="#entry-Uku_siitaka" class="skt-link">Kuṣītaka</a> — <a href="#entry-kau_siitaki" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kauṣītaki <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> [KB.], supplément du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uzaafkaayana" class="skt-link">Śāṅkāyana</a> — <a href="#entry-kau_siitaki" class="skt-link">f.</a> [<i>kauṣītakyupaniṣad</i>] nom d'une leçon [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>], associée au <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौट</span> <i>kauṭa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuu_ta" class="skt-link">kūṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kau_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauṭā</i> malhonnête, frauduleux — <a href="#entry-kau_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> fraude, tromperie; mensonge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_talya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौटल्य</span> <i>kauṭalya</i> <a href="#entry-kau_talya" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-kau_tilya" class="skt-link">kauṭilya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_thaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौठार</span> <i>kauṭhāra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ku_thaara" class="skt-link">kuṭhāra</a>] <a href="#entry-kau_thaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de la principauté de Kauṭhāra du royaume hindouïsé du <a href="#entry-Ucampaa_1" class="skt-link">Campā_1</a> au Sud-Vietnam.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_tilya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौटिल्य</span> <i>kauṭilya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ku_tila" class="skt-link">kuṭila</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kau_tilya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_tilya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_tilyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauṭilyā</i> qui est de la lignée [<a href="#entry-gotra" class="skt-link">gotra</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uku_tila" class="skt-link">Kuṭila</a> — <a href="#entry-kau_tilya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kauṭilya <a href="#entry-Ucaa_nakya" class="skt-link">Cāṇakya</a>, brâhmane né à <a href="#entry-Ukaa_ncii" class="skt-link">Kāñcī</a> (?313–289 ant.); il aida <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta" class="skt-link">Candragupta</a> <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a> à renverser la dynastie <a href="#entry-Unanda" class="skt-link">Nanda</a> et fut son ministre à <a href="#entry-Upaa_taliputra" class="skt-link">Pāṭaliputra</a>; il est l'auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uarthazaastra" class="skt-link">Arthaśāstra</a>; on lui attribue la <a href="#entry-Ucaa_nakyaniiti" class="skt-link">Cāṇakyanīti</a>; ce théoricien du pragmatisme, fin diplomate, est comparé à Machiavel — a. <a href="#entry-kau_tilya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_tilya" class="skt-link">n.</a> (par ext.) retors — <a href="#entry-kau_tilya" class="skt-link">n.</a> torsion, courbure; fait d'être bouclé (cheveu) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> machiavélisme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_tilyazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kauṭilyaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-kau_tilyazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> diplomatie.<br> <a href="#entry-kau_tilya" class="skt-link">kauṭilya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kau_tumba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौटुम्ब</span> <i>kauṭumba</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ku_tumba" class="skt-link">kuṭumba</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kau_tumba" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_tumba" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kau_tumbaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauṭumbā</i> relatif à la famille — <a href="#entry-kau_tumba" class="skt-link">n.</a> relation familiale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaucumaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौचुमार</span> <i>kaucumāra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kucumaara" class="skt-link">kucumāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaucumaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaucumaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaucumaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaucumārā</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Ukucumaara" class="skt-link">Kucumāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaucumaarayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaucumārayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-kaucumaarayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> magie érotique du <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> selon <a href="#entry-Ukucumaara" class="skt-link">Kucumāra</a>; l'art du déguisement, un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-kaucumaara" class="skt-link">kaucumāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaufkuma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौङ्कुम</span> <i>kauṅkuma</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kufkuma" class="skt-link">kuṅkuma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaufkuma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaufkuma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaufkumaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauṅkumā</i> teint de safran.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौल</span> <i>kaula</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaulii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaulī</i> familial; qui concerne la famille — <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [tantr.] adorateur de <a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>-<a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a> et des puissances farouches [<a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a>] suivant le rituel «de la main gauche», ayant comme idéal le héros viril [<a href="#entry-viira" class="skt-link">vīra</a>] — <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">n.</a> ce rituel; cf. <a href="#entry-kulaacaara" class="skt-link">kulācāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaulaj_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaulajñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-kaulaj_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [«gnose clanique»] doctrine associée aux rites tantriques.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaulaj_naananir_naya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaulajñānanirṇaya</i> [<a href="#entry-nir_naya" class="skt-link">nirṇaya</a>] <a href="#entry-kaulaj_naananir_naya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Kaulajñānanirṇaya «Exposition de la doctrine <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>», du à <a href="#entry-Umatsyendranaatha" class="skt-link">Matsyendranātha</a> (10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il servit de socle à la doctrine <a href="#entry-yoginiikaulamata" class="skt-link">yoginīkaulamata</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kaulaj_naana" class="skt-link">kaulajñāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaularahasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaularahasya</i> [<a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">rahasya</a>] <a href="#entry-kaularahasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kaularahasya, exposé de la doctrine ésotérique <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaulava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौलव</span> <i>kaulava</i> var. <i>kaulaba</i> <a href="#entry-kaulava" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. troisième demi-jour lunaire [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] (mobile).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauleya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौलेय</span> <i>kauleya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kauleya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kauleya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauleyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauleyā</i> issu d'une famille noble <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [<a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">śākta</a>] pratiquant des rites <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a> — <a href="#entry-kauleya" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«animal domestique»] chien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauleyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kauleyaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kauleyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kauleyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauleyaka" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauleyaka</i> issu d'une famille noble <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> concernant une famille — <a href="#entry-kauleyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«animal domestique»] chien.<br> <a href="#entry-kauleya" class="skt-link">kauleya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauliina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौलीन</span> <i>kaulīna</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a><a href="#entry--iina" class="skt-link">-īna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kauliina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kauliina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauliinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaulīnā</i> qui relève d'une bonne famille — <a href="#entry-kauliina" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [<a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">śākta</a>] pratiquant des rites <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a> — <a href="#entry-kauliina" class="skt-link">n.</a> rumeur; rapport défavorable; diffamation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> action impropre; scandale familial <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bonne naissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauliinya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaulīnya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-kauliinya" class="skt-link">n.</a> bonne lignée, noblesse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> problème familial, scandale.<br> <a href="#entry-kauliina" class="skt-link">kaulīna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaulika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौलिक</span> <i>kaulika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-kaulika" class="skt-link">m.</a> tisserand <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] adepte du <a href="#entry-kulaacaara" class="skt-link">kulācāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaulya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौल्य</span> <i>kaulya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaulya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaulya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaulyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaulyā</i> issu d'une famille noble <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [<a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">śākta</a>] pratiquant des rites <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a> — <a href="#entry-kaulya" class="skt-link">n.</a> naissance noble, noblesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaumaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौमार</span> <i>kaumāra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kumaara" class="skt-link">kumāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaumaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaumaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaumaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaumārī</i> juvénil, virginal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. se rapportant à <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> — <a href="#entry-kaumaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> enfance; jeunesse, adolescence; virginité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du Kaumāra, épith. du <a href="#entry-Ukaatantra" class="skt-link">Kātantra</a> «Inspiré par <a href="#entry-Ukumaara" class="skt-link">Kumāra</a>» — <a href="#entry-kaumaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaumārī</i> myth. np. de Kaumārī, <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>, une <a href="#entry-saptamaat_rkaa" class="skt-link">saptamātṛkā</a>, symbolisant l'énergie juvénile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaumaarabh_rtya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaumārabhṛtya</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_rtya" class="skt-link">bhṛtya</a>] <a href="#entry-kaumaarabh_rtya" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. la pédiatrie, une section de l'<a href="#entry-Uaayurveda" class="skt-link">Āyurveda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kaumaara" class="skt-link">kaumāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaumodakii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौमोदकी</span> <i>kaumodakī</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kumuda" class="skt-link">kumuda</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kaumodakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Kaumodakī «qui fait la joie de la Terre», massue-sceptre [<a href="#entry-gadaa" class="skt-link">gadā</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, donnée par <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugadaadevii" class="skt-link">Gadādevī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaumuda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौमुद</span> <i>kaumuda</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kumuda" class="skt-link">kumuda</a>] <a href="#entry-kaumuda" class="skt-link">m.</a> le «joyeux» mois <a href="#entry-kaarttika" class="skt-link">kārttika</a> — <a href="#entry-kaumudii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaumudī</i> clair de lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. «éclaircissement» (not. de la grammaire de <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>); not. la <a href="#entry-Usiddhaantakaumudii" class="skt-link">Siddhāntakaumudī</a> ou Kaumudī de <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_toji" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭoji</a> <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a> (17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle) et sa version abrégée <a href="#entry-Ulaghukaumudii" class="skt-link">Laghukaumudī</a> de son disciple <a href="#entry-Uvaradaraaja_1" class="skt-link">Varadarāja_1</a>, qui donne un système grammatical simplifié pour les débutants; voir aussi <a href="#entry-Utarkakaumudii" class="skt-link">Tarkakaumudī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaumudii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौमुदी</span> <i>kaumudī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-kaumuda" class="skt-link">kaumuda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaumudiimahotsava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaumudīmahotsava</i> [<a href="#entry-mahotsava" class="skt-link">mahotsava</a>] <a href="#entry-kaumudiimahotsava" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Kaumudīmahotsava «Festival joyeux», pièce de la princesse <a href="#entry-Uvijayabha_t_taarikaa" class="skt-link">Vijayabhaṭṭārikā</a> (7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).<br> <a href="#entry-kaumudii" class="skt-link">kaumudī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaunda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौन्द</span> <i>kaunda</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kunda" class="skt-link">kunda</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaunda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaunda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaundii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaundī</i> relatif au jasmin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaunteya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौन्तेय</span> <i>kaunteya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuntii" class="skt-link">kuntī</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-kaunteya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] «Fils de <a href="#entry-Ukuntii" class="skt-link">Kuntī</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a>, de <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a>, ou d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौप</span> <i>kaupa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuupa" class="skt-link">kūpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaupii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaupī</i> venant d'un puits ou d'une citerne — <a href="#entry-kaupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> eau du puits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaupiina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौपीन</span> <i>kaupīna</i> [<a href="#entry-kaupa" class="skt-link">kaupa</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-kaupiina" class="skt-link">n.</a> parties génitales <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pièce d'étoffe pour les couvrir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faute, péché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaurava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौरव</span> <i>kaurava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuru" class="skt-link">kuru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaurava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaurava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauravaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauravā</i> myth. descendant de <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kauravās</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] les Kauravās, famille royale des descendants de <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a>; on désigne ainsi not. les descendants du roi <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāṃtanu</a>: l'aîné <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>, ses demi-frères <a href="#entry-Ucitraafgada" class="skt-link">Citrāṅgada</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvicitraviirya" class="skt-link">Vicitravīrya</a>, morts sans descendance, puis les enfants des veuves de <a href="#entry-Uvicitraviirya" class="skt-link">Vicitravīrya</a> par l'ascète <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a>: <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a> l'aveugle et <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_du" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍu</a> qui régna avant de renoncer à la couronne, reprise par <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a>, fils aîné de <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a>; dans la guerre fratricide qui s'ensuivit, les <a href="#entry-Ukauravaas" class="skt-link">Kauravās</a> désignent les hommes du clan de <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a>, opposé à celui de ses cousins les <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍavās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauravya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौरव्य</span> <i>kauravya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuru" class="skt-link">kuru</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kauravya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kauravya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauravyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauravyā</i> myth. descendant de <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-kaurava" class="skt-link">kaurava</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> Kauravya, de la lignée d'<a href="#entry-Uairaavata" class="skt-link">Airāvata</a>; il est père d'<a href="#entry-Uuluupii" class="skt-link">Ulūpī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaurma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौर्म</span> <i>kaurma</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuurma" class="skt-link">kūrma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kaurma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaurma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kaurmaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaurmā</i> relatif à la tortue ou à l'<a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a> <a href="#entry-Ukuurma" class="skt-link">Kūrma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaurmapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaurmapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kaurmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-kuurmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">kūrmapurāṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kaurma" class="skt-link">kaurma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kausala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौसल</span> <i>kausala</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kosala" class="skt-link">kosala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kausala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kausala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kausalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kausalā</i> relatif au <a href="#entry-Ukosala" class="skt-link">Kosala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kausalās</i> peuple des Kausalās.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kausalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौसल्य</span> <i>kausalya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kosala" class="skt-link">kosala</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kausalya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kausalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kausalyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kausalyā</i> relatif au <a href="#entry-Ukosala" class="skt-link">Kosala</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-kausalyaa" class="skt-link">kausalyā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kausalyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौसल्या</span> <i>kausalyā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-kausalya" class="skt-link">kausalya</a>] <a href="#entry-kausalyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de la reine Kausalyā «(Princesse) du <a href="#entry-Ukosala" class="skt-link">Kosala</a>», épouse principale du roi <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kausalyā_1, épouse de <a href="#entry-Upuuru" class="skt-link">Pūru</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kausalyaanandavardhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kausalyānandavardhana</i> [<a href="#entry-aanandavardhana" class="skt-link">ānandavardhana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kausalyaanandavardhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kausalyaanandavardhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kausalyaanandavardhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kausalyānandavardhanā</i> qui fait la joie de <a href="#entry-Ukausalyaa" class="skt-link">Kausalyā</a> — <a href="#entry-kausalyaanandavardhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kausalyaa" class="skt-link">kausalyā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaustubha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौस्तुभ</span> <i>kaustubha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kustubha" class="skt-link">kustubha</a>] <a href="#entry-kaustubha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kaustubha" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. du joyau Kaustubha «Qui appartient au soutien du Monde», obtenu lors du barattage de la mer de lait [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>]; <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> le porte sur sa poitrine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauthuma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौथुम</span> <i>kauthuma</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuthumin" class="skt-link">kuthumin</a>] <a href="#entry-kauthuma" class="skt-link">m.</a> patr. des descendants de <a href="#entry-Ukuthumii" class="skt-link">Kuthumī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. école védique de <a href="#entry-Ukuthumii" class="skt-link">Kuthumī</a>; not. école de chant <a href="#entry-Usaamaveda" class="skt-link">Sāmaveda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauthumaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kauthumaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kauthumaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kauthumaka, <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> de l'école de <a href="#entry-Ukuthumii" class="skt-link">Kuthumī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kauthuma" class="skt-link">kauthuma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kautsa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौत्स</span> <i>kautsa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kutsa" class="skt-link">kutsa</a>] <a href="#entry-kautsa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [Ragh.] np. du brâhmane Kautsa; à la fin de ses études, il voulut donner salaire [<a href="#entry-dak_si_naa" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇā</a>] à son <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> <a href="#entry-Uvaratantu" class="skt-link">Varatantu</a>; celui-ci lui dit d'abord que sa bonne complétion des études était sa récompense, mais à l'insistence de <a href="#entry-Ukautsa" class="skt-link">Kautsa</a> lui demanda 140 millions de pièces d'or (une <a href="#entry-ko_ti" class="skt-link">koṭi</a> pour chacune des 14 sciences enseignées); <a href="#entry-Ukautsa" class="skt-link">Kautsa</a> alla voir le roi <a href="#entry-Uraghu" class="skt-link">Raghu</a>, qui demanda l'or à <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, qui ordonna à <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> de faire pleuvoir les pièces d'or sur <a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. patr. de Kautsa_1, philosophe sceptique antique; cf. <a href="#entry-anarthaka" class="skt-link">anarthaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kautuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौतुक</span> <i>kautuka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kutuka" class="skt-link">kutuka</a>] <a href="#entry-kautuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> curiosité; impatience, désir (pour ⟨iic. loc.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> objet curieux, merveille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> divertissement, amusement; sport, spectacle; plaisir, bonheur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fête, festival; not. cérémonie du bracelet de mariage.<br> <i>kautukāt</i> adv. par curiosité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kautukasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kautukasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-kautukasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cordon de laine jaune attaché par sa mère au poignet de la mariée avant le mariage.<br> <a href="#entry-kautuka" class="skt-link">kautuka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kautuuhala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौतूहल</span> <i>kautūhala</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kutuuhala" class="skt-link">kutūhala</a>] <a href="#entry-kautuuhala" class="skt-link">n.</a> curiosité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> intérêt pour, violent désir pour ⟨loc. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>-acc. inf.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> spectacle curieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauza_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौश</span> <i>kauśa_1</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kauza_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kauza_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauzii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauśī</i> fait d'herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a> — <a href="#entry-kauza_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>kauśanagara</i>] géo. np. de Kauśa «Abondante en <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a>», épith. de la ville de <a href="#entry-Ukaanyakubja" class="skt-link">Kānyakubja</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-Ukuzasthala" class="skt-link">Kuśasthala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauza_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौश</span> <i>kauśa_2</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kauza_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kauza_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauzaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauśā</i> fait de soie, soyeux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauzaambii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौशाम्बी</span> <i>kauśāmbī</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuzaamba" class="skt-link">kuśāmba</a>] <a href="#entry-kauzaambii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Kauśāmbī «construite par <a href="#entry-Ukuzaamba" class="skt-link">Kuśāmba</a>», sur le cours inférieur de la <a href="#entry-Uyamunaa" class="skt-link">Yamunā</a>, capitale du pays <a href="#entry-Uvatsa" class="skt-link">Vatsa</a> (mod. Kosam, Uttar Pradesh); on dit [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] qu'elle fut fondée par le roi myth. <a href="#entry-Ukuzaamba" class="skt-link">Kuśāmba</a>; elle fut un centre bd. important du temps de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> qui y prêcha; elle était aussi ville sacrée pour les jaïnes; cf. <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauzala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौशल</span> <i>kauśala</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuzala" class="skt-link">kuśala</a>] <a href="#entry-kauzala" class="skt-link">n.</a> bien-être; prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aptitude, talent; expérience de ⟨loc. iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-kauzalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauśalī</i> salutation amicale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauzalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौशल्य</span> <i>kauśalya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuzala" class="skt-link">kuśala</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-kauzalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> bien-être; prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> habileté, talent; expérience de ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-kauzalyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauśalyā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-kausalya" class="skt-link">kausalya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauzeya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kauśeya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kauzeya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kauzeya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauzeyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauśeyā</i> soyeux — <a href="#entry-kauzeya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soie (indienne); tissu de soie, soirie; sari.<br> <a href="#entry-kauza_2" class="skt-link">kauśa_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauzika_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौशिक</span> <i>kauśika_1</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kauzika_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kauzika_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauzikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauśikā</i> formant un réceptable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de soie — <a href="#entry-kauzika_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> lexicographe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauzika_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौशिक</span> <i>kauśika_2</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuzika" class="skt-link">kuśika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kauzika_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kauzika_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kauzikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauśikī</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Ukuzika" class="skt-link">Kuśika</a>; descendant de <a href="#entry-Ukuzika" class="skt-link">Kuśika</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] descendant de <a href="#entry-Ukuza_1" class="skt-link">Kuśa_1</a> — <a href="#entry-kauzika_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kauśika, patr. de <a href="#entry-Ugaadhi" class="skt-link">Gādhi</a>, de <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a> — <a href="#entry-kauzikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kauśikī</i> myth. np. de la rivière Kauśikī; sur ses berges <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a> fit pénitence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kauzika_3">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कौशिक</span> <i>kauśika_3</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kuzaa" class="skt-link">kuśā</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-kauzika_3" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. hibou.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कव</span> <i>kava</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>] pf. (particule péjorative) mauvais, mal; petit; usé, déficient — a. <a href="#entry-kava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kavaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kavā</i> avare.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कवच</span> <i>kavaca</i> <a href="#entry-kavaca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kavaca" class="skt-link">n.</a> cuirasse, armure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavacamantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavacamantra</i> [<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] <a href="#entry-kavacamantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] formule magique de protection; on la porte en amulette dans une petite boîte de métal autour du cou.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavacin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavacin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kavacin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kavacin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kavacinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kavacinī</i> qui porte une cuirasse.<br> <a href="#entry-kavaca" class="skt-link">kavaca</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कवल</span> <i>kavala</i> <a href="#entry-kavala" class="skt-link">m.</a> bouchée, gorgée — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(kavalayati)</i> avaler, dévorer.<br> <i>kavalīkṛ</i> avaler; dévorer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कवर</span> <i>kavara</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>-<a href="#entry-vara_2" class="skt-link">vara_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kavara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kavara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kavaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kavarā</i> divers; mélangé — <a href="#entry-kavarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kavarī</i> tresse de cheveux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कवर्ग</span> <i>kavarga</i> [<i>(ka)</i>-<a href="#entry-varga" class="skt-link">varga</a>] <a href="#entry-kavarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. la classe des gutturales.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कवि</span> <i>kavi</i> [<a href="#entry-kuu" class="skt-link">kū</a>] <a href="#entry-kavi" class="skt-link">m.</a> poète <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. poète visionnaire; chantre d'hymnes révélés <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kavi «Poète», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uzukra" class="skt-link">Śukra</a> — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(kavīyati)</i> désirer un poète.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaviindra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavīndra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-kaviindra" class="skt-link">m.</a> titre de Kavīndra «prince parmi les poètes».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaviindraacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavīndrācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-kaviindraacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a> Kavīndrācārya <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a>; il vivait à la cour Moghole vers (1600–1675); il est l'auteur du Kavīndrakalpadruma (en Hindi) et du recueil de poésies Jagadvijayacchandas.<br> <a href="#entry-kavi" class="skt-link">kavi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavika_n_thaabhara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavikaṇṭhābharaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_thaabhara_na" class="skt-link">kaṇṭhābharaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kavika_n_thaabhara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kavikaṇṭhābharaṇa «Collier du poète», manuels de composition poétique dû à <a href="#entry-Uk_semendra" class="skt-link">Kṣemendra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavikaamadhenu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavikāmadhenu</i> [<a href="#entry-kaamadhenu" class="skt-link">kāmadhenu</a>] <a href="#entry-kavikaamadhenu" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Kavikāmadhenu «Trésor du poète», commentaire de l'<a href="#entry-Uamarako_sa" class="skt-link">Amarakoṣa</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Usubhuuticandra" class="skt-link">Subhūticandra</a>, composé entre 1050 et 1120.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavikalpadruma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavikalpadruma</i> [<a href="#entry-kalpadruma" class="skt-link">kalpadruma</a>] <a href="#entry-kavikalpadruma" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. np. du Kavikalpadruma, traité sur le verbe sanscrit dû à <a href="#entry-Uvopadeva" class="skt-link">Vopadeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavikratu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavikratu</i> [<a href="#entry-kratu" class="skt-link">kratu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kavikratu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kavikratu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kavikratu" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. qui possède la vision divine omnisciente.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaviraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavirāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raajan" class="skt-link">rājan</a>] <a href="#entry-kaviraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kavirāja, titre de «Roi des poètes»; épith. not. de <a href="#entry-Uvizvanaatha_1" class="skt-link">Viśvanātha_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavirahasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavirahasya</i> [<a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">rahasya</a>] <a href="#entry-kavirahasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de Kavirahasya «Poésie secrète» de <a href="#entry-Uhalaayudha_1" class="skt-link">Halāyudha_1</a>; c'est une grammaire du sanskrit déguisée en panégyrique royal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavisamaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavisamaya</i> [<a href="#entry-samaya" class="skt-link">samaya</a>] <a href="#entry-kavisamaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. convention poétique; license poétique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavisammelana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavisammelana</i> [<a href="#entry-sammelana" class="skt-link">sammelana</a>] <a href="#entry-kavisammelana" class="skt-link">n.</a> réunion de poètes, joute d'improvisation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavitā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-kavitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> poésie; état de poète.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavitaavali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavitāvali</i> [<a href="#entry-aavali" class="skt-link">āvali</a>] <a href="#entry-kavitaavali" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Kavitāvali, anthologie de poésie en 325 versets de <a href="#entry-Utulasiidaasa" class="skt-link">Tulasīdāsa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kavitaa" class="skt-link">kavitā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavitva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kavitva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-kavitva" class="skt-link">n.</a> art poétique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavizasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaviśasta</i> [<a href="#entry-zasta_1" class="skt-link">śasta_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kavizasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kavizasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kavizastaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaviśastā</i> récité par le chantre (se dit d'un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कव्य</span> <i>kavya</i> [<a href="#entry-kavi" class="skt-link">kavi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kavyā</i> véd. sage; prêtre préposé au sacrifice — <a href="#entry-kavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> oblation aux mânes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kayaadhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कयाधु</span> <i>kayādhu</i> [<a href="#entry-kravyaad" class="skt-link">kravyād</a>] <a href="#entry-kayaadhu" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Kayādhu «Vampire», épouse de <a href="#entry-Uhira_nyakazipu" class="skt-link">Hiraṇyakaśipu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कश</span> <i>kaśa</i> <a href="#entry-kaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> fouet; rênes — <a href="#entry-kazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaśā</i> fouet; corde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kazeru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कशेरु</span> <i>kaśeru</i> <a href="#entry-kazeru" class="skt-link">m.</a> colonne vertébrale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. l'une des 10 régions [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] formant le <a href="#entry-jambudviipa" class="skt-link">jambudvīpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kazipu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कशिपु</span> <i>kaśipu</i> <a href="#entry-kazipu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kazipu" class="skt-link">n.</a> coussin, oreiller; natte, matelas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kazmala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कश्मल</span> <i>kaśmala</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>-<a href="#entry-mala" class="skt-link">mala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kazmala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kazmala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kazmalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaśmalā</i> <a href="#entry-kazmalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaśmalī</i> sale, impur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> timide, pusillanime — <a href="#entry-kazmala" class="skt-link">n.</a> saleté; impureté, péché — <a href="#entry-kazmala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kazmala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kazmalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kaśmalā</i> stupéfaction; faiblesse, manque de courage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kazmiira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कश्मीर</span> <i>kaśmīra</i> <a href="#entry-kazmiira" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Kaśmīra (Cachemire), pays de l'Inde du Nord-Ouest, contrefort de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kaśmīrās</i> ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kazyapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कश्यप</span> <i>kaśyapa</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-kacchapa" class="skt-link">kacchapa</a>] <a href="#entry-kazyapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> tortue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kaśyapa, premier des <a href="#entry-saptar_si" class="skt-link">saptarṣi</a>; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] il est fils du <a href="#entry-brahmar_si" class="skt-link">brahmarṣi</a> <a href="#entry-Umariici" class="skt-link">Marīci</a>; c'est un géniteur universel prolifique; il épousa 13 des filles de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>; il est père des dieux [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] et plus tard de <a href="#entry-Uvaamana" class="skt-link">Vāmana</a> (par son épouse <a href="#entry-Uaditi" class="skt-link">Aditi</a>), des démons [<a href="#entry-daitya" class="skt-link">daitya</a>] (par son épouse <a href="#entry-Uditi" class="skt-link">Diti</a>), des ondins [<a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a>] (par son épouse <a href="#entry-Udanu" class="skt-link">Danu</a>), d'<a href="#entry-Uaru_na" class="skt-link">Aruṇa</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a> (par son épouse <a href="#entry-Uvinataa" class="skt-link">Vinatā</a>), des dragons [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>] (par son épouse <a href="#entry-Ukadruu" class="skt-link">Kadrū</a>), des vents [<a href="#entry-rudra" class="skt-link">rudra</a>] et de <a href="#entry-Urohi_nii_2" class="skt-link">Rohiṇī_2</a> (par son épouse <a href="#entry-Usurabhii" class="skt-link">Surabhī</a>), des génies centaures [<a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>] et gnomes [<a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a>] par son épouse [<a href="#entry-Umunii" class="skt-link">Munī</a>], et des animaux sauvages (par son épouse <a href="#entry-Ukrodhaa" class="skt-link">Krodhā</a>); il incarne la puissance divine de procréation; [HV.] <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>, il lui vola les vaches de son sacrifice pour les donner à <a href="#entry-Usurabhii" class="skt-link">Surabhī</a> et <a href="#entry-Uaditi" class="skt-link">Aditi</a>; <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> s'en étant plaint à <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, celui-ci proposa à <a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa</a> qu'en punition <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> devrait descendre sur Terre comme le vacher <a href="#entry-Uvasudeva" class="skt-link">Vasudeva</a>, pour épouser <a href="#entry-Usurabhii" class="skt-link">Surabhī</a> incarnée en <a href="#entry-Urohi_nii_3" class="skt-link">Rohiṇī_3</a> et <a href="#entry-Uaditi" class="skt-link">Aditi</a> incarnée en <a href="#entry-Udevakii" class="skt-link">Devakī</a>; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Tak.saka le rémunéra pour qu'il n'aille pas au secours de <a href="#entry-Upariik_sit" class="skt-link">Parīkṣit</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du philosophe Kaśyapa_1, fondateur du système <a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>, connu comme <a href="#entry-Uka_naada" class="skt-link">Kaṇāda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>Kassapa</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kazyapasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaśyapasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-kazyapasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] np. de la Kaśyapasaṃhitā, texte de démonologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kazyapasuunujye_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaśyapasūnujyeṣṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-suunu" class="skt-link">sūnu</a>-<a href="#entry-jye_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyeṣṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-kazyapasuunujye_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. «aîné des fils de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uhira_nyaak_sa" class="skt-link">Hiraṇyākṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kazyapa" class="skt-link">kaśyapa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_ncuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कञ्चुक</span> <i>kañcuka</i> <a href="#entry-ka_ncuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> veste, justaucorps, corset; cuirasse; vêtement; couverture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_ncukiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kañcukīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_ncukiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. chambellan.<br> <a href="#entry-ka_ncuka" class="skt-link">kañcuka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_ncukin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kañcukin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ka_ncukin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_ncukin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ka_ncukini" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kañcukini</i> cuirassé; porteur d'une cotte de maille ou d'une armure — ifc. couvert de — <a href="#entry-ka_ncukin" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. chambellan; gardien du gynécée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> débauché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ka_nja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kaṃja</i> var. <i>kañja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] <a href="#entry-ka_mja" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«produit par la tête»] chevelure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kaṃja, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> «né des eaux» — <a href="#entry-ka_mja" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«issu de l'eau»] lotus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kedaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केदार</span> <i>kedāra</i> <a href="#entry-kedaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> champ cultivé, champ irrigué; bassin entouré de digues <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kedāra «Bassin (sacré)», un nom de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>kedārabhaṭṭa</i>] hist. np. du mathématicien Kedāra_1 <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, du 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il fonda la combinatoire binaire dans son ouvrage de prosodie <a href="#entry-Uv_rttaratnaakara" class="skt-link">Vṛttaratnākara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kedaaranaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kedāranātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-kedaaranaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Kedāranātha, important lieu de pélerinage au nord de l'Uttar Pradesh; on y vénère l'un des 12 signes radieux de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> [<a href="#entry-jyotirlifga" class="skt-link">jyotirliṅga</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-kedaara" class="skt-link">kedāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kekaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केका</span> <i>kekā</i> [onomatopée] <a href="#entry-kekaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cri du paon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kekaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केकय</span> <i>kekaya</i> <a href="#entry-kekaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. nom du pays Kekaya à l'ouest de la Sutlej, et de son peuple guerrier; mod. il correspond aux districts de Jhelum, Shahpur et Gujrat au Pañjab; cf. <a href="#entry-Uazvapati" class="skt-link">Aśvapati</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kekayās</i> ses habitants, les Kekayās.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kelaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केला</span> <i>kelā</i> [vn. <i>keli</i>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(kelāyati)</i> s'amuser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-keli">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केलि</span> <i>keli</i> <a href="#entry-keli" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-keli" class="skt-link">f.</a> amusement, plaisir; jeu, passe-temps; ébat érotique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> déguisement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केन</span> <i>kena</i> [i. <a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] conj. comment? pourquoi?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केन्द्र</span> <i>kendra</i> <a href="#entry-kendra" class="skt-link">n.</a> centre d'un cercle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. κεντρον; ang. center; fr. centre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kenopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kenopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-kenopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la leçon [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] Kenopaniṣad (commençant par <a href="#entry-kena" class="skt-link">kena</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-kena" class="skt-link">kena</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kerala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केरल</span> <i>kerala</i> <a href="#entry-kerala" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du pays Kerala sur la côte de Malabar; sa capitale est <a href="#entry-Uanantazayana" class="skt-link">Anantaśayana</a> (Trivandrum) — <a href="#entry-kerala" class="skt-link">m.</a> son peuple; son roi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>keralās</i> ses habitants — <a href="#entry-keralii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>keralī</i> femme du <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kesara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केसर</span> <i>kesara</i> var. <i>keśara</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">keśa</a>] <a href="#entry-kesara" class="skt-link">n.</a> sourcil; poil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fibre; étamine (not. de lotus) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Crocus sativus</i>, safran <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> queue de yack (utilisée comme chasse-mouches [<a href="#entry-caamara" class="skt-link">cāmara</a>]) — <a href="#entry-kesara" class="skt-link">m.</a> crinière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>nāgakesara</i>] natu. bot. <i>Mesua Ferrea</i>, arbre à l'écorce rougeâtre, au bois très dense, et aux fleurs blanches très odorantes aux nombreuses étamines, donnant des baies rouges; on l'utilise en pharmacopée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. hair; all. haar.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kesaraagra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kesarāgra</i> [<a href="#entry-agra" class="skt-link">agra</a>] <a href="#entry-kesaraagra" class="skt-link">n.</a> extrémité de la crinière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kesarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kesarin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kesarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kesarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kesari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kesariṇī</i> muni d'une crinière — <a href="#entry-kesarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> lion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du singe Kesarī; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukezarii" class="skt-link">Keśarī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de la dynastie Kesarī_1 «Lignée du lion», qui régna sur le <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a> du 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ au 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; son fondateur est <a href="#entry-Uyayaati_1" class="skt-link">Yayāti_1</a>; elle remplaça les dominateurs ioniens [<a href="#entry-yavanaas" class="skt-link">yavanās</a>], et le bouddhisme fut délaissé pour le culte de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> — <a href="#entry-kesari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kesariṇī</i> lionne.<br> <a href="#entry-kesara" class="skt-link">kesara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-keta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केत</span> <i>keta</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cit_1" class="skt-link">cit_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-keta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-keta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ketaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ketā</i> qui sait — <a href="#entry-keta" class="skt-link">m.</a> désir, intention <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> habitation, abri; marque, forme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ketaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केतक</span> <i>ketaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-cit_1" class="skt-link">cit_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ketaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Pandanus odoratissimus</i>, arbre pandane; plante épineuse aux feuilles jaunes et aux fleurs parfumées (sur les plants mâles) — <a href="#entry-ketakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ketakī</i> la fleur du pandane, au parfum suave; myth. c'est l'une des flèches de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> [<a href="#entry-pu_spabaa_na" class="skt-link">puṣpabāṇa</a>], car elle «jette le trouble dans le sang»; la <a href="#entry-ketakii" class="skt-link">ketakī</a> est maudite par <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, car elle fut produite comme témoin par <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> prétendant avoir vu son visage, lorsqu'il chercha le sommet de la colonne de feu [<a href="#entry-tejolifga" class="skt-link">tejoliṅga</a>]; on dit aussi que <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> troubla l'ascèse de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> en s'ornant de <a href="#entry-ketakii" class="skt-link">ketakī</a>, provoquant sa malédiction et son bannissement; odieuse à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> [<a href="#entry-zivadvi_s_taa" class="skt-link">śivadviṣṭā</a>], on ne peut lui en offrir; elle symbolise en poésie la douleur de la séparation [<a href="#entry-viraha" class="skt-link">viraha</a>] avec l'être aimé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ketana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केतन</span> <i>ketana</i> [<a href="#entry-keta" class="skt-link">keta</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-ketana" class="skt-link">n.</a> siège, résidence; marque, attribut; enseigne, étendard, drapeau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ketu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केतु</span> <i>ketu</i> [<a href="#entry-keta" class="skt-link">keta</a>] <a href="#entry-ketu" class="skt-link">m.</a> lampe; torche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> splendeur; marque, symbole; enseigne, drapeau, bannière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. étoile filante; comète <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Ketu «Météore», fils de <a href="#entry-U_r_sabha" class="skt-link">Ṛṣabha</a>, divinité du solstice d'hiver, queue du «dragon descendant» <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a>; on le surnomme le chauve (<a href="#entry-Umu_n_da" class="skt-link">Muṇḍa</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uakeza" class="skt-link">Akeśa</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ketumaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ketumāla</i> [<a href="#entry-maala" class="skt-link">māla</a>] <a href="#entry-ketumaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> [<i>ketumālavarṣa</i>] myth. l'un des 9 bassins versants [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] du monde, situé à l'Ouest.<br> <a href="#entry-ketu" class="skt-link">ketu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ketumat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ketumat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ketumat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ketumat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ketumatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ketumatī</i> fait de splendeur — <a href="#entry-ketumat" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Ketumān «le Splendide», fils de <a href="#entry-Udhanvantari" class="skt-link">Dhanvantari</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Ubhiimaratha" class="skt-link">Bhīmaratha</a> — <a href="#entry-ketumatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ketumatī</i> géo. np. de Ketumatī «la Splendide», un nom de Bénarès [<a href="#entry-Ukaazi" class="skt-link">Kāśi</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épouse du <a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a> <a href="#entry-Usumaali" class="skt-link">Sumāli</a>; elle engendra <a href="#entry-Ukaikasii" class="skt-link">Kaikasī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केवल</span> <i>kevala</i> a. <a href="#entry-kevala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kevala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kevalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kevalā</i> <a href="#entry-kevalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kevalī</i> seul, isolé; exclusif; pur, sans mélange; abstrait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> absolu, entier — <a href="#entry-kevala" class="skt-link">m.</a> ifc. un simple ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-kevala" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. doctrine de l'unité de l'Esprit.<br> <i>kevalam</i> adv. seulement, uniquement, entièrement, purement, complètement, exclusivement.<br> <i>na</i> <i>kevalam</i> <i>...</i> <i>api</i> adv. non seulement ... mais encore.<br> <i>na</i> <i>kevalam</i> <i>...</i> <i>kintu</i> adv. non seulement ... mais encore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevalaadin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kevalādin</i> [<a href="#entry-aadin" class="skt-link">ādin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kevalaadin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kevalaadin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kevalaadinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kevalādinī</i> qui mange tout seul.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevalaadvaita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kevalādvaita</i> [<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>] <a href="#entry-kevalaadvaita" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. non-dualité selon <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevalaanvayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kevalānvayin</i> [<a href="#entry-anvayin" class="skt-link">anvayin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kevalaanvayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kevalaanvayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kevalaanvayinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kevalānvayinī</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] se dit d'une inférence [<a href="#entry-anumaana" class="skt-link">anumāna</a>] qui utilise seulement les inférences positives.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevalanaiyaayika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kevalanaiyāyika</i> [<a href="#entry-naiyaayika" class="skt-link">naiyāyika</a>] <a href="#entry-kevalanaiyaayika" class="skt-link">m.</a> un simple logicien (ignorant des autres sciences).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevalavaiyaakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kevalavaiyākaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaiyaakara_na" class="skt-link">vaiyākaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kevalavaiyaakara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> un simple grammairien (ignorant des autres sciences).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevalavyatirekin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kevalavyatirekin</i> [<a href="#entry-vyatirekin" class="skt-link">vyatirekin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kevalavyatirekin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kevalavyatirekin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kevalavyatireki_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kevalavyatirekiṇī</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] se dit d'une inférence [<a href="#entry-anumaana" class="skt-link">anumāna</a>] qui utilise seulement les preuves par contradiction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevalavyatirekiprakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
