    <i>kevalavyatirekiprakaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakara_na" class="skt-link">prakaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kevalavyatirekiprakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] démonstration par la négation; ce procédé de preuve est expliqué dans la <a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇi</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kevalavyatirekin" class="skt-link">kevalavyatirekin</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kevalin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kevalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kevalin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kevalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kevalinī</i> seul, isolé; exceptionnel — <a href="#entry-kevalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> ascète méditant.<br> <a href="#entry-kevala" class="skt-link">kevala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kevarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केवर्त</span> <i>kevarta</i> var. <i>kaivarta</i> <a href="#entry-kevarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> pêcheur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-keyuura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केयूर</span> <i>keyūra</i> <a href="#entry-keyuura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-keyuura" class="skt-link">n.</a> bracelet (ornement de bras, au dessus du coude).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-keyuuradharaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>keyūradharā</i> [<a href="#entry-dharaa" class="skt-link">dharā</a>] <a href="#entry-keyuuradharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de l'<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a> Keyūradharā «qui porte un ornement de bras».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-keyuuraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>keyūraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-keyuuraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. du jeune <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> Keyūraka «au bracelet de rubis», porteur de <a href="#entry-vii_naa" class="skt-link">vīṇā</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-keyuurin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>keyūrin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-keyuurin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-keyuurin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-keyuuri_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>keyūriṇī</i> qui porte un bracelet au bras.<br> <a href="#entry-keyuura" class="skt-link">keyūra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-keza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केश</span> <i>keśa</i> <a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">m.</a> cheveu, chevelure; crinière — a. <a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kezii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>keśī</i> ifc. qui a les cheveux ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <i>keśān</i> <i>dhṛ</i> laisser pousser ses cheveux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kezaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>keśānta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-kezaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> ligne terminale des cheveux sur le front, frange <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> longue mèche, cheveux en natte, etc. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. rite [<a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a>] de rasage du crâne et des poils du corps; on l'effectue à 16 ans [<a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>], 22 ans [<a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a>] ou 24 ans [<a href="#entry-vaizya" class="skt-link">vaiśya</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-godaana" class="skt-link">godāna</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">keśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kezapak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>keśapakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-pak_sa" class="skt-link">pakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-kezapak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> mèche de cheveux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kezara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केशर</span> <i>keśara</i> [<a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">keśa</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-kezara" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-kesara" class="skt-link">kesara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kezarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>keśarin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kezarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kezarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kezari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>keśariṇī</i> qui a une crinière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du singe Keśarī ou <a href="#entry-Ukesarii" class="skt-link">Kesarī</a> «Crinière», époux d'<a href="#entry-Ua_njanaa" class="skt-link">Añjanā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kezara" class="skt-link">keśara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kezava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केशव</span> <i>keśava</i> [<a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">keśa</a><a href="#entry--va" class="skt-link">-va</a>] <a href="#entry-kezava" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Keśava, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «aux cheveux longs» ou «Pourfendeur du démon <a href="#entry-Ukezii" class="skt-link">Keśī</a>»; un temple de <a href="#entry-Usomanaathapura" class="skt-link">Somanāthapura</a> lui est consacré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Keśava_1 <a href="#entry-Ubhaaratii" class="skt-link">Bhāratī</a>, un <a href="#entry-maayaavaadin" class="skt-link">māyāvādin</a>; <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> devint sannyaasin (prit l'habit [<a href="#entry-ve_sa" class="skt-link">veṣa</a>]) sous sa direction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Keśava_2 <a href="#entry-Ubhaaratii" class="skt-link">Bhāratī</a>, un poète défié et humilié par le jeune <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> à <a href="#entry-Umaayaapura" class="skt-link">Māyāpura</a>.<br> <i>ākāśāt</i> <i>patitam</i> <i>toyam</i> <i>yathā</i> <i>gacchati</i> <i>sāgaram</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>sarvadevanamaskāraḥ</i> <i>keśavam</i> <i>pratigacchati</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> Comme l'eau descendue du ciel va à l'océan, ainsi les prières à tous les dieux reviennent à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kezavaapana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>keśavāpana</i> [<a href="#entry-vaapana" class="skt-link">vāpana</a>] <a href="#entry-kezavaapana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de raser les cheveux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kezavadaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>keśavadāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-kezavadaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Keśavadāsa «Serviteur de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>», poète et grammairien du 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur de la première grammaire de <a href="#entry-Uhindii" class="skt-link">Hindī</a> [Brajbhāṣā] et de la Rāmacandracandrikā, traité de métrique du sanskrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kezavadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>keśavadeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-kezavadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du grand temple de Keśavadeva, à <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a>; il fut détruit par Aurangzeb en 1669.<br> <a href="#entry-kezava" class="skt-link">keśava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kezin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">केशिन्</span> <i>keśin</i> [<a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">keśa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kezin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kezin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kezinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>keśinī</i> chevelu — <a href="#entry-kezin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a> Keśī «Crinière»; lors de la guerre avec les dieux, il tenta d'enlever les deux beautés célestes <a href="#entry-Udaityasenaa" class="skt-link">Daityasenā</a> et <a href="#entry-Udevasenaa" class="skt-link">Devasenā</a>; la première accepta d'être son épouse, mais la deuxième cria au secours, et fut sauvée par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> qui la donna en mariage à <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>; un <a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Ukezii" class="skt-link">Keśī</a>, allié de <a href="#entry-Uka_msa" class="skt-link">Kaṃsa</a>, se rendit à <a href="#entry-Ugokula" class="skt-link">Gokula</a> sous la forme d'un cheval pour tuer <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, mais fut détruit par ce dernier, qui acquit ainsi le nom de <a href="#entry-Ukezava" class="skt-link">Keśava</a> — <a href="#entry-kezinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>keśinī</i> myth. np. de Keśinī «aux beaux cheveux», épouse d'<a href="#entry-Uajamii_dha" class="skt-link">Ajamīḍha</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Ujahnu" class="skt-link">Jahnu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Keśinī_1, fille du roi du <a href="#entry-Uvidarbha" class="skt-link">Vidarbha</a>, première épouse de <a href="#entry-Usagara" class="skt-link">Sagara</a>, mère d'<a href="#entry-Uasama_njaa" class="skt-link">Asamañjā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Keśinī_2, épith. de la <a href="#entry-raak_sasii" class="skt-link">rākṣasī</a> <a href="#entry-Ukaikasii" class="skt-link">Kaikasī</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uvizravaa" class="skt-link">Viśravā</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukumbhakar_na" class="skt-link">Kumbhakarṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukhara" class="skt-link">Khara</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvibhii_sa_na" class="skt-link">Vibhīṣaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_m">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किं</span> <i>kiṃ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_mstughna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किंस्तुघ्न</span> <i>kiṃstughna</i> cf. <a href="#entry-kintughna" class="skt-link">kiṃtughna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_mv_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃvṛtta</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtta" class="skt-link">vṛtta</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mv_rtta" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme du commun, brute.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_mzuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃśuka</i> [<a href="#entry-zuka" class="skt-link">śuka</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mzuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Butea frondosa</i>, arbre Flamboyant; ses fleurs écarlates apparaissent au printemps avant les feuilles; il est comparé en poésie à un guerrier ou à un tigre rougi de sang, ou à un incendie; syn. <a href="#entry-palaaza" class="skt-link">palāśa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ki_m" class="skt-link">kiṃ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किण</span> <i>kiṇa</i> <a href="#entry-ki_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> callosité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cicatrice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_skindha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किष्किन्ध</span> <i>kiṣkindha</i> <a href="#entry-ki_skindha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de la montagne Kiṣkindha du Sud de l'Inde, contenant la grotte <a href="#entry-Uki_skindhaa" class="skt-link">Kiṣkindhā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. territoire au nord de Mysore, près des sources de la <a href="#entry-Upampaa" class="skt-link">Pampā</a>; myth. c'était le royaume de <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a>, usurpé par son frère <a href="#entry-Uvaalii" class="skt-link">Vālī</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-ki_skindhaa" class="skt-link">kiṣkindhā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_skindhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किष्किन्धा</span> <i>kiṣkindhā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-ki_skindha" class="skt-link">kiṣkindha</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_skindhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de la grotte Kiṣkindhā, qui était la capitale de <a href="#entry-Uvaalii" class="skt-link">Vālī</a> l'usurpateur, puis restituée à <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a> par <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_skindhaakaa_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṣkindhākāṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kaa_n_da" class="skt-link">kāṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_skindhaakaa_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kiṣkindhākāṇḍa, 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>, où <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> rencontre <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> et <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ki_skindhaa" class="skt-link">kiṣkindhā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किटि</span> <i>kiṭi</i> <a href="#entry-ki_ti" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. porc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kif">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किङ्</span> <i>kiṅ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kifkara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃkara</i> var. <i>kiṅkara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mkara" class="skt-link">m.</a> serviteur; esclave <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du mauvais esprit Kiṃkara; <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> lui fit posséder le roi <a href="#entry-Ukalmaa_sapaada" class="skt-link">Kalmāṣapāda</a> pour le punir d'avoir offensé son fils <a href="#entry-Uzakti_1" class="skt-link">Śakti_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kifki_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किङ्किणी</span> <i>kiṅkiṇī</i> <a href="#entry-kifki_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> clochette <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kiṅkiṇi</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kifkiraata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃkirāta</i> [<a href="#entry-kiraata" class="skt-link">kirāta</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mkiraata" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. variété d'arbre Ashok (bot. <i>Saraca asoka</i>).<br> <a href="#entry-kif" class="skt-link">kiṅ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kii_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीट</span> <i>kīṭa</i> <a href="#entry-kii_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kii_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kii_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kīṭī</i> insecte; ver, chenille — ifc. vil, méprisable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kii_tama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kīṭamaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-kii_tama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Lampyris noctiluca</i>, luciole, ver luisant.<br> <a href="#entry-kii_ta" class="skt-link">kīṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiicaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीचक</span> <i>kīcaka</i> <a href="#entry-kiicaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Bambusa arundinacea</i>, bambou épineux; il peut atteindre 30 m de haut <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Kīcaka «Bambou», commandant en chef de l'armée du roi <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a>, et son gendre comme frère de la reine <a href="#entry-Usude_s_naa_1" class="skt-link">Sudeṣṇā_1</a>; il importuna <a href="#entry-Udraupadii" class="skt-link">Draupadī</a> lors de la 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ année d'exil des <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>, dissimuléée comme la <a href="#entry-sairandhrii" class="skt-link">sairandhrī</a> <a href="#entry-Umaalinii_1" class="skt-link">Mālinī_1</a>; celle-ci demanda protection à <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a>, qui le massacra en l'écrabouillant en une boule de chair <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. géo. np. du pays des Kīcakās, tribu des <a href="#entry-Ukekayaas" class="skt-link">Kekayās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiicakavadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kīcakavadha</i> [<a href="#entry-vadha" class="skt-link">vadha</a>] <a href="#entry-kiicakavadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du poème Kīcakavadha «Mise à mort de <a href="#entry-Ukiicaka" class="skt-link">Kīcaka</a>» de <a href="#entry-Uniitivarmaa" class="skt-link">Nītivarmā</a>; il conte les évènements du <a href="#entry-viraa_taparvan" class="skt-link">virāṭaparvan</a> avec de nombreux procédés d'interprétation multiple [<a href="#entry-zle_sa" class="skt-link">śleṣa</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-kiicaka" class="skt-link">kīcaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiid_rza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीदृश</span> <i>kīdṛśa</i> [<a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a>-<a href="#entry-d_rz_2" class="skt-link">dṛś_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kiid_rza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kiid_rza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kiid_rzii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kīdṛśī</i> interr. de quelle sorte? ressemblant à quoi? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excl. quel ...! — a. <a href="#entry-kiid_rz" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kiid_rz" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kiid_rz" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kīdṛś</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiil">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कील्</span> <i>kīl</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kīlati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kīlita</i><i>)</i> attacher, lier (à un poteau); cheviller; épingler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कील</span> <i>kīla</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kiil" class="skt-link">kīl</a>] <a href="#entry-kiila" class="skt-link">m.</a> piquet, pieu; pal; cheville, aiguille; lance; coin de bûcheron <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> poteau; not. poteau pour attacher le bétail <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cadran solaire; syn. <a href="#entry-mayuukha" class="skt-link">mayūkha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arme; coude; coup de coude — <a href="#entry-kiilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kīlā</i> poteau; épingle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiilaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीलक</span> <i>kīlaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kiil" class="skt-link">kīl</a>] <a href="#entry-kiilaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> aiguille; cheville; coin (en bois); attelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pieu à attacher le bétail <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Kīlaka «Pieu», personnifiant l'an 42 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>] — <a href="#entry-kiilikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kīlikā</i> aiguille; cheville.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiilita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीलित</span> <i>kīlita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kiil" class="skt-link">kīl</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kiilita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kiilita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kiilitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kīlitā</i> attaché à un poteau; empalé; attaché, lié, sequestré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> érigé en poteau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiinaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीनाश</span> <i>kīnāśa</i> <a href="#entry-kiinaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> paysan, cultivateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manant, pauvre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiir_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीर्ण</span> <i>kīrṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-k_rr" class="skt-link">kṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kiir_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kiir_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kiir_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kīrṇā</i> répandu, épars, disséminé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> couvert de, plein de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीर्त्</span> <i>kīrt</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-kiirti" class="skt-link">kīrti</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(kīrtayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kīrtita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> mentionner; appeler; annoncer, nommer, célébrer, glorifier ⟨g.⟩; réciter, raconter — ps. <i>(kīrtyate)</i> s'appeler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirtana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीर्तन</span> <i>kīrtana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kiirt" class="skt-link">kīrt</a>] <a href="#entry-kiirtana" class="skt-link">n.</a> mention, récit; éloge, louange <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> litanie de louanges à une divinité; chant dévotionnel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीर्ति</span> <i>kīrti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_r_2" class="skt-link">kṛ_2</a>] <a href="#entry-kiirti" class="skt-link">f.</a> mention; gloire, renommée — <a href="#entry-kiirti" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Kīrti (ou Kīrtti) «Glorieux»; avec son frère <a href="#entry-Uvaasa" class="skt-link">Vāsa</a> il était gardien de l'<a href="#entry-ekaamrak_setra" class="skt-link">ekāmrakṣetra</a>; il possédait le monstre <a href="#entry-Uraktabiija" class="skt-link">Raktabīja</a>; quand <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> leur rendit visite, ils se disputèrent pour la posséder, et <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> proposa que celui qui la prendrait sur ses épaules la gagnerait; de ses deux talons elle les expédia sous terre, affectés à la garde de son bassin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirtimukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kīrtimukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] <a href="#entry-kiirtimukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. de Kīrtimukha «Face de gloire», monstre issu de la colère de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> face à l'impudence de <a href="#entry-Ujalandhara" class="skt-link">Jalandhara</a>; il jaillit de son troisième œil pour dévorer <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a> le messager, qui se plaça sous la protection de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, ne laissant à <a href="#entry-Ukiirtimukha" class="skt-link">Kīrtimukha</a> que la possibilité de s'auto-dévorer; lorsqu'il n'en resta que la tête, <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> l'honora en lui confiant la garde de ses sanctuaires; arch. sa tête est un motif décorant fréquemment les frontons et linteaux dans les temples indiens, indonésiens et khmers; il orne aussi des bijoux tels que les boucles de ceintures.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirtimuku_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kīrtimukuṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-muku_ta" class="skt-link">mukuṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-kiirtimuku_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kiirtimuku_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> haute tiare cylindrique; <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> la porte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirtiraata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kīrtirāta</i> [<a href="#entry-raata" class="skt-link">rāta</a>] <a href="#entry-kiirtiraata" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi Kīrtirāta <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Umahiidhraka" class="skt-link">Mahīdhraka</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Umahaaromaa" class="skt-link">Mahāromā</a>; aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uk_rtiraata" class="skt-link">Kṛtirāta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirtiratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kīrtiratha</i> [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] <a href="#entry-kiirtiratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi Kīrtiratha <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Upratiindhaka" class="skt-link">Pratīndhaka</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Udevamii_dha" class="skt-link">Devamīḍha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirtita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कीर्तित</span> <i>kīrtita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kiirt" class="skt-link">kīrt</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kiirtita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kiirtita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kiirtitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kīrtitā</i> dit, nommé, mentionné; connu, célèbre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirtivaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kīrtivāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaasa_3" class="skt-link">vāsa_3</a>] <a href="#entry-kiirtivaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. myth. <a href="#entry-Ukiirti" class="skt-link">Kīrti</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaasa" class="skt-link">Vāsa</a> ensemble.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiirtivaasezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kīrtivāseśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-kiirtivaasezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kīrtivāseśvara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Vainqueur de <a href="#entry-Ukiirti" class="skt-link">Kīrti</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaasa" class="skt-link">Vāsa</a>»; il est adoré à <a href="#entry-Ubhuvanezvarii_1" class="skt-link">Bhuvaneśvarī_1</a> comme <a href="#entry-Ulifgaraaja" class="skt-link">Liṅgarāja</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «vêtu de gloire», arborant la dépouille du <a href="#entry-gajaasura" class="skt-link">gajāsura</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kiirtivaasa" class="skt-link">kīrtivāsa</a> <br> <a href="#entry-kiirti" class="skt-link">kīrti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किल</span> <i>kila</i> part. cl. certes, comme on dit, comme on sait; apparemment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ⟨interr.⟩ n'est-ce-pas?<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>kila</i> quel dommage!<br> <i>naiva</i> <i>kila</i> non!<br> <i>yadi</i> <i>kila</i> peut-être.<br> <i>smarati</i> <i>kila</i> vous vous rappelez, n'est-ce-pas?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kilbi_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किल्बिष</span> <i>kilbiṣa</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-kilvi_sa" class="skt-link">kilviṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-kilbi_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-kilvi_sa" class="skt-link">kilviṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kilbi_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kilbiṣin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kilbi_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kilbi_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kilbi_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kilbiṣiṇī</i> coupable, pécheur.<br> <a href="#entry-kilbi_sa" class="skt-link">kilbiṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kilvi_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किल्विष</span> <i>kilviṣa</i> var. <i>kilbiṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-vi_sa" class="skt-link">viṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-kilvi_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> faute, péché; offense, injustice — <a href="#entry-kilvi_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kilviṣā</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kim">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किम्</span> <i>kim</i> [n. <a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] pn. <a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">n.</a> interr. qui? quel? lequel? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> que? pourquoi? comment? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> assez de ⟨i.⟩; pourquoi donc? à quoi bon ⟨i. g.⟩? — (en début de phrase) est-ce que? qu'est-ce que?<br> <i>atha</i> <i>kim</i> certainement.<br> <i>kim</i> <i>atra</i> à quoi bon?<br> <i>kim</i> <i>anyad</i> quoi encore?<br> <i>kim</i> <i>api</i> plutôt, de préférence.<br> <i>kim</i> <i>artham</i> pourquoi faire? pour quel motif?<br> <i>kim</i> <i>iti</i> pourquoi?<br> <i>kim</i> <i>iva</i> quoi donc? comment donc?<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>vā</i> à quoi bon ⟨i.⟩?<br> <i>kimu</i> adv. à plus forte raison, d'autant moins que.<br> <i>kimuta</i> adv. à plus forte raison, d'autant moins que.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>kila</i> quel dommage!<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>ca</i> de plus, en outre.<br> <i>kiṃcana</i> adv. quelque chose.<br> <i>na</i> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>cana</i> rien.<br> <i>kiṃcid</i> quelque chose, un peu, quelque.<br> <i>akiṃ</i> <i>cid</i> rien.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>tu</i> var. <i>kintu</i> mais, néanmoins.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>nu</i> à plus forte raison <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vraiment? combien en plus? combien en moins?<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>punar</i> à plus forte raison.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>vā</i> à plus forte raison.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>svid</i> pourquoi, comment donc?<br> <i>yat</i> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>ca</i> n'importe quel, n'importe quoi.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>yuddhena</i> à quoi bon se battre?.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>bahunā</i> pourquoi tant de paroles?.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>vilambena</i> à quoi sert de traîner?.<br> <i>nirujaḥ</i> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>auṣadhaiḥ</i> à quoi sert un remède à un homme bien portant?.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kimpuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃpuruṣa</i> var. <i>kimpuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mpuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kimpuru_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kimpuruṣī</i> myth. chimère; les <i>kimpuruṣās</i> habitent le <a href="#entry-Uhemakuu_ta" class="skt-link">Hemakūṭa</a> et sont aux ordres de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; ils comprennent les <a href="#entry-gandharvaas" class="skt-link">gandharvās</a> et les <a href="#entry-apsarasas" class="skt-link">apsarasas</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-kinnara" class="skt-link">kinnara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kimpuru_savar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃpuruṣavarṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mpuru_savar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du Kiṃpuruṣavarṣa, l'un des 9 versants du Monde; syn. <a href="#entry-kinnaravar_sa" class="skt-link">kiṃnaravarṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kimpuru_sa" class="skt-link">kiṃpuruṣa</a> <br> <a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kimu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किमु</span> <i>kimu</i> var. <i>kimuta</i> [<a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a>-<a href="#entry-u" class="skt-link">u</a>] adv. à plus forte raison, d'autant moins que.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किन्</span> <i>kin</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kindama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃdama</i> var. <i>kindama</i> [<a href="#entry-dama" class="skt-link">dama</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mdama" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a> Kiṃdama; il s'accoupla avec sa compagne dans la forêt sous l'apparence de daims; <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_du" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍu</a> le frappa d'une flèche; en mourant il le maudit de ne pouvoir faire l'acte d'amour sans en mourir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kinnara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃnara</i> var. <i>kinnara</i> [<a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a>-<a href="#entry-nara" class="skt-link">nara</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mnara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kinnarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kinnarī</i> myth. [«être anthropomorphe»] un <a href="#entry-kinnara" class="skt-link">kinnara</a> être céleste fabuleux à corps d'homme et tête de cheval ou d'oiseau; les <i>kinnarās</i> sont associés aux centaures [<a href="#entry-gandharvaas" class="skt-link">gandharvās</a>]; ils sont serviteurs de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-mayu" class="skt-link">mayu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kinnaravar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃnaravarṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mnaravar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. du Kiṃnaravarṣa, l'un des 9 bassins versants [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] du Monde, au nord de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-kimpuru_savar_sa" class="skt-link">kiṃpuruṣavarṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kinnara" class="skt-link">kiṃnara</a> <br> <a href="#entry-kin" class="skt-link">kin</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kintu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃtu</i> var. <i>kintu</i> [<a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a>-<a href="#entry-tu" class="skt-link">tu</a>] adv. mais, néanmoins.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kintughna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃtughna</i> var. <i>kiṃstughna</i> [<a href="#entry-ghna" class="skt-link">ghna</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mtughna" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. [«qui détruit tout sauf»] premier demi-jour lunaire [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] fixe.<br> <a href="#entry-kintu" class="skt-link">kiṃtu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किर</span> <i>kira</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_rr" class="skt-link">kṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kirā</i> qui disperse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kira_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किरण</span> <i>kiraṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-k_rr" class="skt-link">kṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-kira_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> poussière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rayon (de lumière, de soleil, de lune).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kira_naavalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiraṇāvalī</i> [<a href="#entry-aavalii" class="skt-link">āvalī</a>] <a href="#entry-kira_naavalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] np. de la Kiraṇāvalī «Recueil de rayons», commentaire du <a href="#entry-Upadaarthadharmasafgraha" class="skt-link">Padārthadharmasaṃgraha</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uudayana_1" class="skt-link">Udayana_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kira_naavaliiprakaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiraṇāvalīprakāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakaaza" class="skt-link">prakāśa</a>] <a href="#entry-kira_naavaliiprakaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] np. du Kiraṇāvalīprakāśa «Éclat du Recueil de rayons», commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Ukira_naavalii" class="skt-link">Kiraṇāvalī</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uvardhamaana_1" class="skt-link">Vardhamāna_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kira_naavalii" class="skt-link">kiraṇāvalī</a> <br> <a href="#entry-kira_na" class="skt-link">kiraṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiraata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किरात</span> <i>kirāta</i> <a href="#entry-kiraata" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. habitant des montagnes de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. chasseur tribal vivant dans la forêt, de race jaune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kirātās</i> np. du peuple des Kirātās; les Tibétains; les Népalais; les sauvages; cf. <a href="#entry-Uyalambara" class="skt-link">Yalambara</a> — <a href="#entry-kiraatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kirātī</i> soc. femme de tribu montagnarde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiraataarjuniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kirātārjunīya</i> [<a href="#entry-arjuna" class="skt-link">arjuna</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-kiraataarjuniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> Kirātārjunīya de <a href="#entry-Ubhaaravi" class="skt-link">Bhāravi</a>; il conte le combat entre <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>, parti au Mont <a href="#entry-Uindrakiila" class="skt-link">Indrakīla</a> pour obtenir des dieux l'arme <a href="#entry-Upaazupata" class="skt-link">Pāśupata</a>, et le dieu <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> sous les traits d'un montagnard sauvage [<a href="#entry-kiraata" class="skt-link">kirāta</a>]; à mains nues il parvint à se saisir du dieu et à le tenir soulevé de terre, exploit marquant le point culminant du sentiment héroïque [<a href="#entry-viirarasa" class="skt-link">vīrarasa</a>] évoqué par l'œuvre; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> se révèle alors à lui et lui donne l'arme souhaitée; ce motif est représenté not. au Prasat Thom à Angkor et au Banteay Srei à Īśvarapura.<br> <a href="#entry-kiraata" class="skt-link">kirāta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kirii_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किरीट</span> <i>kirīṭa</i> <a href="#entry-kirii_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> diadème; crête; tiare.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kirii_tin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kirīṭin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kirii_tin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kirii_tin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kirii_ti_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kirīṭiṇī</i> qui porte un diadème — <a href="#entry-kirii_tin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Kirīṭin, épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> «Couronné (par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>)».<br> <a href="#entry-kirii_ta" class="skt-link">kirīṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kirmiira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किर्मीर</span> <i>kirmīra</i> a. <a href="#entry-kirmiira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kirmiira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kirmiiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kirmīrā</i> bigarré — <a href="#entry-kirmiira" class="skt-link">m.</a> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a> Kirmīra «Tacheté», frère de <a href="#entry-Ubaka_1" class="skt-link">Baka_1</a> et ami de <a href="#entry-Uhi_dimba" class="skt-link">Hiḍimba</a>; il attaqua les <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a> pendant leur exil dans la forêt; <a href="#entry-Udhaumya" class="skt-link">Dhaumya</a> détruisit sa <a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">māyā</a>, et <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a> le tua.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kisalaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किसलय</span> <i>kisalaya</i> <a href="#entry-kisalaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> bourgeon, jeune pousse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kitava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कितव</span> <i>kitava</i> <a href="#entry-kitava" class="skt-link">m.</a> joueur (not. aux dés) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> trompeur, fripon; brigand.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kitavavyavahaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kitavavyavahāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vyavahaara" class="skt-link">vyavahāra</a>] <a href="#entry-kitavavyavahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> jeu (avec pari).<br> <a href="#entry-kitava" class="skt-link">kitava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiyanmaatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiyanmātra</i> [<a href="#entry-maatra" class="skt-link">mātra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kiyanmaatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kiyanmaatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kiyanmaatraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kiyanmātrā</i> de peu d'importance — <a href="#entry-kiyanmaatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> bagatelle.<br> <a href="#entry-kiyat" class="skt-link">kiyat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kiyat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कियत्</span> <i>kiyat</i> [<a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a><a href="#entry--yat" class="skt-link">-yat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kiyat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kiyat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kiyatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kiyatī</i> interr. dans quelle mesure? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> de quelle taille? combien? à quelle distance? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> petit, insignifiant.<br> <i>kiyantaṃ</i> <i>kālam</i> pour combien de temps?<br> <i>kiyacciram</i> pour combien de temps?<br> <i>kiyatyadhvani</i> à quelle distance?<br> <i>kiyadetad</i> de quelle importance est-ce envers ⟨g.⟩?<br> <i>tena</i> <i>kiyān</i> <i>arthah</i> quel profit en retire-t-on?<br> <i>kiyaccireṇa</i> en combien de temps? dans quel délai?<br> <i>kiyaddūre</i> à quelle distance?<br> <i>kiyad</i> <i>rodimi</i> à quoi bon pleurer?<br> <i>kiyad</i> <i>asubhis</i> à quoi bon vivre?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kizara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किशर</span> <i>kiśara</i> <a href="#entry-kizara" class="skt-link">m.</a> denrée parfumée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kisara</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kizora">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किशोर</span> <i>kiśora</i> <a href="#entry-kizora" class="skt-link">m.</a> poulain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> garçon, adolescent — ifc. petit de — <a href="#entry-kizorii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kiśorī</i> pouline <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jeune fille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_n">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">किञ्</span> <i>kiñ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-kim" class="skt-link">kim</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_nca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃca</i> var. <i>kiñca</i> [<a href="#entry-ca" class="skt-link">ca</a>] adv. de plus, en outre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_ncana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃcana</i> [<a href="#entry-cana" class="skt-link">cana</a>] adv. quelque chose <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kānicana</i> quelques choses.<br> <i>na</i> <i>kiṃcana</i> rien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_nciccalana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃciccalana</i> [<a href="#entry-calana" class="skt-link">calana</a>] <a href="#entry-ki_mciccalana" class="skt-link">n.</a> mouvement imperceptible; mouvement sur place.<br> <a href="#entry-ki_ncid" class="skt-link">kiṃcid</a> <br> <a href="#entry-ki_n" class="skt-link">kiñ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ki_ncid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kiṃcid</i> var. <i>kiñcid</i> [<a href="#entry-cid_1" class="skt-link">cid_1</a>] adv. quelque chose, un peu, quelque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लान्त</span> <i>klānta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-klam" class="skt-link">klam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-klaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-klaanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-klaantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>klāntā</i> fatigué, épuisé; maigre, émacié <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> déprimé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klaibya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लैब्य</span> <i>klaibya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kliiba" class="skt-link">klība</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-klaibya" class="skt-link">n.</a> impuissance; couardise.<br> <i>klaibyaṃ</i> <i>mā</i> <i>sma</i> <i>gamaḥ</i> <i>spārtha</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [BhG.] Ô Arjuna! N'agis pas comme un eunuque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लम्</span> <i>klam</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-zram" class="skt-link">śram</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(klāmyati)</i> pft. <i>(caklāma)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>klānta</i><i>)</i> être las, être fatigué, être épuisé; se fatiguer — ca. <i>(klāmayati)</i> fatiguer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लम</span> <i>klama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-klam" class="skt-link">klam</a>] <a href="#entry-klama" class="skt-link">m.</a> fatigue; épuisement; langueur — <a href="#entry-klamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>klamā</i> ifc. fatigue de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klav">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लव्</span> <i>klav</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(klavate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>klavita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> avoir peur; perdre ses moyens, se troubler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klavita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लवित</span> <i>klavita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-klav" class="skt-link">klav</a>] a. <a href="#entry-klavita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-klavita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-klavitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>klavitā</i> bafouillé, bégayé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kled">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लेद्</span> <i>kled</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-klid" class="skt-link">klid</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(kledayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kledita</i><i>)</i> humidifier, mouiller; gâter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kleda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लेद</span> <i>kleda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-klid" class="skt-link">klid</a>] <a href="#entry-kleda" class="skt-link">m.</a> humidité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> suintement; purulence; putréfaction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kledana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लेदन</span> <i>kledana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-klid" class="skt-link">klid</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kledana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kledana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kledanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kledanā</i> qui humidifie, qui mouille; qui coule — <a href="#entry-kledana" class="skt-link">m.</a> phlegme — <a href="#entry-kledana" class="skt-link">n.</a> humidification.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kledita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लेदित</span> <i>kledita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kled" class="skt-link">kled</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kledita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kledita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kleditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kleditā</i> ramolli; gâté, souillé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kleza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लेश</span> <i>kleśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kliz" class="skt-link">kliś</a>] <a href="#entry-kleza" class="skt-link">m.</a> tourment, difficulté; souffrance, affliction; passion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. facteur d'affliction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kli_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लिष्ट</span> <i>kliṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kliz" class="skt-link">kliś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kli_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kli_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kli_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kliṣṭā</i> tourmenté, peiné, affligé; souffrant.<br> <i>kliṣṭam</i> adv. en peine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kli_s_taakli_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kliṣṭākliṣṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-akli_s_ta" class="skt-link">akliṣṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kli_s_taakli_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kli_s_taakli_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kli_s_taakli_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kliṣṭākliṣṭā</i> plus ou moins affligé.<br> <i>vṛttayaḥ</i> <i>pañcatayyaḥ</i> <i>kliṣṭākliṣṭāḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Upata_njali_1" class="skt-link">Patañjali_1</a>] Les règles à 5 composantes sont plus ou moins entachées d'erreur.<br> <a href="#entry-kli_s_ta" class="skt-link">kliṣṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लिद्</span> <i>klid</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(klidyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>klinna</i><i>)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(aciklidat)</i> se mouiller, devenir humide; se ramollir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> suinter; pourrir, se putréfier — ca. <i>(kledayati)</i> humidifier, mouiller; gâter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klii_m">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लीं</span> <i>klīṃ</i> part. phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] son du <a href="#entry-kaamabiija" class="skt-link">kāmabīja</a>, <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> d'évocation de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kliiba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लीब</span> <i>klība</i> var. <i>klīva</i> <a href="#entry-kliiba" class="skt-link">m.</a> travesti; homosexuel (passif) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> eunuque — a. <a href="#entry-kliiba" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kliiba" class="skt-link">n.</a> impuissant; castré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> efféminé; timoré; faible; couard <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (nuage) n'apportant pas de pluie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. (substantif) de genre neutre — <a href="#entry-kliiba" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>klībaliṅga</i>] gram. genre neutre — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(klībāyate)</i> être un couard — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(klībate)</i> se comporter comme un eunuque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kliibataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>klībatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-kliibataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> impuissance; faiblesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kliibatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>klībatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-kliibatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kliibavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>klībavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] adv. d'une manière efféminée.<br> <a href="#entry-kliiba" class="skt-link">klība</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klinna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लिन्न</span> <i>klinna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-klid" class="skt-link">klid</a>] a. <a href="#entry-klinna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-klinna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-klinnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>klinnā</i> humide, mouillé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui pleure (œil) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gâté, putréfié, pourri <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tendre (cœur).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klinnanetra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>klinnanetra</i> [<a href="#entry-netra" class="skt-link">netra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-klinnanetra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-klinnanetra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-klinnanetraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>klinnanetrā</i> qui a les larmes aux yeux.<br> <a href="#entry-klinna" class="skt-link">klinna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-klinnatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>klinnatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-klinnatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'être humide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kliz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्लिश्</span> <i>kliś</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(kliśnāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kliṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> affliger, tourmenter, faire souffrir, peiner — ca. <i>(kleśayati)</i> id. — ps. <i>(kliśyate)</i> être tourmenté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">को</span> <i>ko</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] pf. d'appréciation ou de dépréciation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोण</span> <i>koṇa</i> <a href="#entry-ko_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> coin, angle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. angle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> point cardinal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 4 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bâton, baguette; baguette de tambour.<br> <i>trikoṇa</i> triangle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_naarka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>koṇārka</i> [<a href="#entry-arka" class="skt-link">arka</a>] <a href="#entry-ko_naarka" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du temple de Koṇārka «Lieu du Soleil», sur le rivage à l'est de <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>, construit par le roi <a href="#entry-Unarasi_mha_2" class="skt-link">Narasiṃha_2</a>; on a employé pour sa construction des poutres métalliques.<br> <a href="#entry-ko_na" class="skt-link">koṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_nika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोणिक</span> <i>koṇika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ko_nika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ko_nika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ko_nikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>koṇikā</i> latéral.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोष्ठ</span> <i>koṣṭha</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-ko_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> estomac, ventre; viscère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> appartement intérieur — <a href="#entry-ko_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> grenier, magasin; cassette, coffre, trésor; carapace <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mur d'enceinte; enclos, cour intérieure; cadre; case (not. d'échiquier).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_s_thaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>koṣṭhaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-ko_s_thaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ko_s_thaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> réceptacle; grenier, magasin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mur d'enceinte; enclos, cour intérieure — <a href="#entry-ko_s_thikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>koṣṭhikā</i> marmite.<br> <i>koṣṭhikāyantra</i> [<a href="#entry-yantra" class="skt-link">yantra</a>] n. (alchimie) cornue.<br> <a href="#entry-ko_s_tha" class="skt-link">koṣṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोष</span> <i>koṣa</i> cf. <a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_tara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोटर</span> <i>koṭara</i> <a href="#entry-ko_tara" class="skt-link">n.</a> creux d'arbre; cavité.<br> <i>viśvam</i> <i>tiṣṭhati</i> <i>kukṣikoṭare</i> Tout l'univers repose dans le creux de la matrice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोटि</span> <i>koṭi</i> <a href="#entry-ko_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> bout, extrémité, pointe; corne (de la lune); sommet; excellence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dix millions; multitude, nombre incommensurable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. sommet d'un triangle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>crore</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_tijiivaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>koṭijīvā</i> [<a href="#entry-jiivaa" class="skt-link">jīvā</a>] <a href="#entry-ko_tijiivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> math. cosinus d'un angle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_tijyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>koṭijyā</i> [<a href="#entry-jyaa_3" class="skt-link">jyā_3</a>] <a href="#entry-ko_tijyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-ko_tijiivaa" class="skt-link">koṭijīvā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_tika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोटिक</span> <i>koṭika</i> <a href="#entry-ko_tika" class="skt-link">m.</a> grenouille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> coccinelle — <a href="#entry-ko_tikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>koṭikā</i> ifc. le plus méprisable des.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_tikaasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>koṭikāsya</i> [<a href="#entry-aasya" class="skt-link">āsya</a>] <a href="#entry-ko_tikaasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Koṭikāsya «Tête de grenouille», fils d'un roi <a href="#entry-Usuratha" class="skt-link">Suratha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ko_tika" class="skt-link">koṭika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_tiphala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>koṭiphala</i> [<a href="#entry-phala" class="skt-link">phala</a>] <a href="#entry-ko_tiphala" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. zénith du Soleil; opp. <a href="#entry-baahuphala" class="skt-link">bāhuphala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ko_tizas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>koṭiśas</i> [<a href="#entry--zas" class="skt-link">-śas</a>] adv. par millions, en quantité innombrable.<br> <i>kotiśo</i> <i>dhanyavādaḥ</i> adr. mille mercis.<br> <a href="#entry-ko_ti" class="skt-link">koṭi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kodrava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोद्रव</span> <i>kodrava</i> [<a href="#entry-ko" class="skt-link">ko</a>-<a href="#entry-drava" class="skt-link">drava</a>] <a href="#entry-kodrava" class="skt-link">n.</a> brouet de millet (mangé par les pauvres).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kofka_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोङ्कण</span> <i>koṅkaṇa</i> <a href="#entry-kofka_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. nom du pays Koṅkaṇa (Deccan occidental, vers mod. Goa) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>koṅkaṇās</i> géo. son peuple — <a href="#entry-kofka_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>koṅkaṇā</i> femme du <a href="#entry-kofka_na" class="skt-link">koṅkaṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Koṅkaṇā, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ure_nukaa" class="skt-link">Reṇukā</a> — <a href="#entry-kofka_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>koṅkaṇī</i> langue du <a href="#entry-kofka_na" class="skt-link">koṅkaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kojaagara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोजागर</span> <i>kojāgara</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>-<a href="#entry-jaagara" class="skt-link">jāgara</a>] <a href="#entry-kojaagara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. fête de la pleine lune [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] du mois <a href="#entry-aazvina" class="skt-link">āśvina</a>; on y célèbre divers jeux, et on fête <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>, qui serait apparue en disant: Qui est éveillé? [<a href="#entry-kojaagara" class="skt-link">kojāgara</a>], pour promettre la richesse à ceux qui veillent; la lumière de la Lune est supposée bénéfique cette nuit-là; on laisse une jarre de lait s'en imprégner, à minuit on regarde le reflet de la Lune sur le lait avant de le boire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-koka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोक</span> <i>koka</i> [onomatopée] <a href="#entry-koka" class="skt-link">m.</a> loup <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> oie rouge; coucou; grenouille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>occa</i>; fr. oie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kokanada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kokanada</i> [<a href="#entry-nada" class="skt-link">nada</a>] <a href="#entry-kokanada" class="skt-link">n.</a> fleur du lotus rouge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kokanada, serviteur de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. géo. np. du peuple des Kokanadavas.<br> <a href="#entry-koka" class="skt-link">koka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kokila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोकिल</span> <i>kokila</i> [<a href="#entry-koka" class="skt-link">koka</a>] <a href="#entry-kokila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kokilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kokilā</i> natu. zoo. <i>Eudynamis Orientalis</i>, coucou indien; (poésie) son cri éveille le désir amoureux; cf. <a href="#entry-anyapu_s_ta" class="skt-link">anyapuṣṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-anyabh_rta" class="skt-link">anyabhṛta</a>, syn. <a href="#entry-anyavaapa" class="skt-link">anyavāpa</a>, <a href="#entry-parabh_rta" class="skt-link">parabhṛta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kokilasandeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kokilasandeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-sandeza" class="skt-link">sandeśa</a>] <a href="#entry-kokilasandeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Kokilasandeśa «Message du coucou», poème d'amour d'<a href="#entry-Uudda_n_da" class="skt-link">Uddaṇḍa</a> <a href="#entry-Uzaastrii" class="skt-link">Śāstrī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kokila" class="skt-link">kokila</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kola">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोल</span> <i>kola</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kul" class="skt-link">kul</a>] <a href="#entry-kola" class="skt-link">m.</a> porc, verrat — <a href="#entry-kola" class="skt-link">n.</a> fruit du jujube.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kolaahala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोलाहल</span> <i>kolāhala</i> [onomatopée] <a href="#entry-kolaahala" class="skt-link">n.</a> grognement, rugissement; bruit confus, tumulte, vacarme, charivari — <a href="#entry-kolaahala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du mont Kolāhala «Tumultueux» au <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>; pris de désir pour la rivière <a href="#entry-Uzuktimatii" class="skt-link">Śuktimatī</a>, il la détourna; à sa disparition, les habitants se plaignirent au roi <a href="#entry-Uuparicara" class="skt-link">Uparicara</a> <a href="#entry-Uvasu_2" class="skt-link">Vasu_2</a>, qui donna à la montagne un coup de pied tel qu'il la fendit en deux, libérant la rivière, et deux enfants nés de leur liaison; le fils devint commandant de l'armée d'<a href="#entry-Uuparicara" class="skt-link">Uparicara</a>, qui épousa la fille <a href="#entry-Ugirikaa" class="skt-link">Girikā</a> comme reine — <a href="#entry-kolaahalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kolāhalā</i> mus. nom d'un mode [<a href="#entry-raaga" class="skt-link">rāga</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-komala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोमल</span> <i>komala</i> a. <a href="#entry-komala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-komala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-komalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>komalā</i> tendre, doux; agréable, plaisant, charmant; joli.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-komalaafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>komalāṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-komalaafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> au corps charmant.<br> <a href="#entry-komala" class="skt-link">komala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kopa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोप</span> <i>kopa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kup" class="skt-link">kup</a>] <a href="#entry-kopa" class="skt-link">m.</a> colère; passion, rage, fureur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hypocondrie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> incompatibilité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. élision du <a href="#entry-visarga" class="skt-link">visarga</a> devant une voyelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kopaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोपक</span> <i>kopaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kup" class="skt-link">kup</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kopaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kopaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kopakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kopakā</i> irascible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kopin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोपिन्</span> <i>kopin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kup" class="skt-link">kup</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kopin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kopin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kopinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kopinī</i> qui s'irrite, irascible, colérique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-koraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोरक</span> <i>koraka</i> <a href="#entry-koraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-koraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> bourgeon; bouton (de fleur).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kosala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोसल</span> <i>kosala</i> <a href="#entry-kosala" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Kosala, pays du Nord de l'Inde sur les bords de la <a href="#entry-Usarayuu" class="skt-link">Sarayū</a>, ayant pour capitale <a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> (mod. Oudh); sa capitale devint <a href="#entry-Uzraavastii" class="skt-link">Śrāvastī</a> pendant la période bouddhiste du roi <a href="#entry-Uzraavasta" class="skt-link">Śrāvasta</a>; au 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant. il fut absorbé par le <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a> voisin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. royaume de la dynastie solaire [<a href="#entry-suuryava_mza" class="skt-link">sūryavaṃśa</a>] de <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a> et <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kovida">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोविद</span> <i>kovida</i> [<a href="#entry-ko" class="skt-link">ko</a>-<a href="#entry-vid_3" class="skt-link">vid_3</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kovida" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kovida" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kovidaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kovidā</i> expérimenté, compétent, habile, expert, adroit (à ⟨g. loc. iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-koza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कोश</span> <i>kośa</i> <a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">m.</a> étui, fourreau, bourse; vase, réceptacle, boîte, récipient <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bourgeon, bouton (de fleur); cocon, not. de ver à soie; membrane, scrotum <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> trésor; lexique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] l'une des cinq enveloppes de l'âme, qui sont: <a href="#entry-annamayakoza" class="skt-link">annamayakośa</a> l'enveloppe matérielle [«qui se nourrit»] et <a href="#entry-praa_namayakoza" class="skt-link">prāṇamayakośa</a> l'enveloppe des souffles vitaux, formant le corps grossier [<a href="#entry-sthuulazariira" class="skt-link">sthūlaśarīra</a>]; <a href="#entry-manomayakoza" class="skt-link">manomayakośa</a> l'enveloppe de pensée et <a href="#entry-vij_naanamayakoza" class="skt-link">vijñānamayakośa</a> l'enveloppe de discernement, formant le corps subtil ou intellectuel [<a href="#entry-suuk_smazariira" class="skt-link">sūkṣmaśarīra</a>]; et enfin <a href="#entry-aanandamayakoza" class="skt-link">ānandamayakośa</a>, l'enveloppe spirituelle ou extatique formant le corps causal ou originel [<a href="#entry-kaara_nazariira" class="skt-link">kāraṇaśarīra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>koṣa</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kozaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kośaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kozaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> testicule.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kozakaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kośakāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-kozakaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. anthologie de poésie.<br> <a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रा</span> <i>krā</i> [<a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-kraa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui marche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kraanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रान्त</span> <i>krānta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kraanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kraantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krāntā</i> parti; étendu, étalé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> offensif; qui attaque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> surpassé; envahi — <a href="#entry-kraanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. déclinaison — <a href="#entry-kraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> pas, enjambée.<br> <i>viṣṇoḥ</i> <i>krānta</i> myth. l'enjambée de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kraanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रान्ति</span> <i>krānti</i> [<a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] <a href="#entry-kraanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait d'aller, pas, allure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de surpasser, supériorité; attaque, offensive <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. déclinaison d'une planète; course du Soleil, écliptique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kraantima_n_dala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krāntimaṇḍala</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">maṇḍala</a>] <a href="#entry-kraantima_n_dala" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. course du Soleil, écliptique (syn. <a href="#entry-v_rtta" class="skt-link">vṛtta</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kraantiv_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krāntivṛtta</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtta" class="skt-link">vṛtta</a>] <a href="#entry-kraantiv_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. course du Soleil, écliptique (syn. <a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">maṇḍala</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-kraanti" class="skt-link">krānti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krakaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रकच</span> <i>krakaca</i> <a href="#entry-krakaca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krakaca" class="skt-link">n.</a> scie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> instrument de musique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रम्</span> <i>kram</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(krāmati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kramati)</i> pft. <i>(cakrāma)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <i>(akran)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(akramīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>krānta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>kramitvā</i>, <i>krāntvā</i>, <i>-kramya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ati</i>, <i>anu</i>, <i>apa</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>parā</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> marcher, s'avancer; marcher sur ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dépasser, dominer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> attaquer, soumettre, subjuguer — pr. md. <i>(kramate)</i> var. <i>krāmate</i> faire des progrès vers, s'efforcer de ⟨dat.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prospérer, avoir du succès — ca. <i>(kramayati)</i> ca. <i>(krāmayati)</i> conduire — int. <i>(caṅkramīti)</i> toujours dominer — int. md. <i>(caṅkramyate)</i> flâner, se promener.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रम</span> <i>krama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] <a href="#entry-krama" class="skt-link">m.</a> pas, marche, démarche; manière d'agir, façon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ordre, succession, série, suite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> processus; ordonnancement d'un rite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. récitation particulière du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> [<a href="#entry-vik_rtipaa_tha" class="skt-link">vikṛtipāṭha</a>]: chaque mot est répété avant le mot suivant.<br> <i>krameṇa</i> var. <i>kramāt</i> adv. successivement, en ordre, graduellement.<br> <i>ekakrameṇa</i> adv. solitairement, entre soi.<br> <i>kramatas</i> adv. graduellement, au fur et à mesure.<br> <i>kramotkramāt</i> dans l'ordre et dans l'ordre inverse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krama_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रमण</span> <i>kramaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] <a href="#entry-krama_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> pas; pied — <a href="#entry-krama_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de marcher — ifc. fait de mettre le pied sur; fait de transgresser ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kramabheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kramabheda</i> [<a href="#entry-bheda" class="skt-link">bheda</a>] <a href="#entry-kramabheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. faute de séquencement, un défault esthétique de la poésie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kramadiipikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kramadīpikā</i> [<a href="#entry-diipikaa" class="skt-link">dīpikā</a>] <a href="#entry-kramadiipikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] np. de la Kramadīpikā, recueil de rituel <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kramaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रमक</span> <i>kramaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kramaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kramaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kramikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kramikā</i> qui va, qui avance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méthodique — <a href="#entry-kramaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> succession <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> étudiant régulier dans sa progression <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. qui connaît le <a href="#entry-kramapaa_tha" class="skt-link">kramapāṭha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kramapaa_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kramapāṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-paa_tha" class="skt-link">pāṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-kramapaa_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. récitation du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> par la lecture pas à pas combinant le <a href="#entry-sa_mhitaapaa_tha" class="skt-link">saṃhitāpāṭha</a> et le <a href="#entry-padapaa_tha" class="skt-link">padapāṭha</a> pour éviter toute erreur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kramazas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kramaśas</i> [<a href="#entry--zas" class="skt-link">-śas</a>] adv. graduellement, par étapes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sériellement, point par point.<br> <a href="#entry-krama" class="skt-link">krama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kramela">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रमेल</span> <i>kramela</i> [<a href="#entry-krama" class="skt-link">krama</a>] <a href="#entry-kramela" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. chameau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. καμηλος; ang. camel; fr. chameau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kramelaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रमेलक</span> <i>kramelaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kramelaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kramika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रमिक</span> <i>kramika</i> [<a href="#entry-krama" class="skt-link">krama</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kramika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kramika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kramikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kramikā</i> hérité en ligne directe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krand">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रन्द्</span> <i>krand</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(krandati)</i> pft. <i>(cakranda)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(akrandīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>krandita</i><i>)</i> hennir; rugir, crier; grincer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gémir; se lamenter; pleurer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krandita">
