  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रन्दित</span> <i>krandita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-krand" class="skt-link">krand</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krandita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krandita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kranditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kranditā</i> pleuré, crié — <a href="#entry-krandita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lamentation, pleurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kratu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रतु</span> <i>kratu</i> [<a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a><a href="#entry--tu" class="skt-link">-tu</a>] <a href="#entry-kratu" class="skt-link">m.</a> projet, intention; compréhension, intelligence; accomplissement, œuvre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kratu «Intellect», un géniteur [<a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a>], issu de la pensée de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, personnifiant l'Intelligence, qqf. listé comme un <a href="#entry-saptar_si" class="skt-link">saptarṣi</a>; il a pour épouses <a href="#entry-Ukriyaa_1" class="skt-link">Kriyā_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Usantati" class="skt-link">Santati</a>; il est père des 60000 <a href="#entry-Uvaalakhilyaas" class="skt-link">Vālakhilyās</a>.<br> <i>ā</i> <i>no</i> <i>bhadrāḥ</i> <i>kratavo</i> <i>yantu</i> <i>viśvataḥ</i> [RV.] Que de nobles projets nous viennent de tous côtés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krau_nca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रौञ्च</span> <i>krauñca</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-kru_nc_1" class="skt-link">kruñc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-krau_nca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krau_ncii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krauñcī</i> natu. zoo. <i>Anthropoides virgo</i>, héron; grue, courlis [«qui a un long bec courbe»]; myth. la peine [<a href="#entry-zoka" class="skt-link">śoka</a>] de cet oiseau perdant son compagnon tué par un chasseur [<a href="#entry-ni_saada" class="skt-link">niṣāda</a>] incita <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a> à composer le <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a> en vers [<a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'un des 7 continents [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du Mont Krauñca, dans l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [SkP.] np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Ukrau_nca" class="skt-link">Krauñca</a> «Héron», fils du mont <a href="#entry-Umainaaka" class="skt-link">Maināka</a>; il barra la route à <a href="#entry-Uagastya" class="skt-link">Agastya</a> en se transformant en montagne et en provoquant la tempête; avec quelques gouttes d'eau bénite, <a href="#entry-Uagastya" class="skt-link">Agastya</a> le maudit d'un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> pour qu'il reste montagne jusqu'à recevoir une flèche de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] lors d'une terrible bataille de <a href="#entry-Usubrahma_nya" class="skt-link">Subrahmaṇya</a> contre les <a href="#entry-asuraas" class="skt-link">asurās</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na" class="skt-link">Bāṇa</a> vint se cacher au mont <a href="#entry-Ukrau_nca" class="skt-link">Krauñca</a>; <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> trancha la montagne en deux avec une flèche que lui avait donné <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>, libérant <a href="#entry-Ukrau_nca" class="skt-link">Krauñca</a>; [VaP.] après que <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> eut tué <a href="#entry-Utaaraka" class="skt-link">Tāraka</a>, <a href="#entry-Umahi_sa" class="skt-link">Mahiṣa</a> se cacha au mont <a href="#entry-Ukrau_nca" class="skt-link">Krauñca</a>; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> demanda à <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> de le tuer en détruisant <a href="#entry-Ukrau_nca" class="skt-link">Krauñca</a>; <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>, qui était comme <a href="#entry-Ukrau_nca" class="skt-link">Krauñca</a> petit-fils de <a href="#entry-Uhimavaan" class="skt-link">Himavān</a>, hésita; ils parièrent sur leurs forces respectives en faisant la course autour de la montagne; <a href="#entry-Ukrau_nca" class="skt-link">Krauñca</a>, arbitre, déclara à tort <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> vainqueur, et <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> furieux occit <a href="#entry-Umahi_sa" class="skt-link">Mahiṣa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukrau_nca" class="skt-link">Krauñca</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kravis">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रविस्</span> <i>kravis</i> <a href="#entry-kravis" class="skt-link">n.</a> viande crue; charogne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>cruor</i>, <i>caro</i>; ang. carrion; fr. cru, charogne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kravya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रव्य</span> <i>kravya</i> [<a href="#entry-kravis" class="skt-link">kravis</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kravya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kravya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kravyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kravyā</i> carnassier; cruel — <a href="#entry-kravya" class="skt-link">n.</a> viande crue; charogne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kravyaad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kravyād</i> [<a href="#entry-ad_2" class="skt-link">ad_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kravyaad" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kravyaad" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kravyaad" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui mange de la viande crue ou des cadavres <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. vampire [<a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. bûcher funéraire.<br> <a href="#entry-kravya" class="skt-link">kravya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रय</span> <i>kraya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-krii" class="skt-link">krī</a>] <a href="#entry-kraya" class="skt-link">m.</a> achat <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prix d'achat.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krayakriita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krayakrīta</i> [<a href="#entry-kriita" class="skt-link">krīta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krayakriita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krayakriita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krayakriitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krayakrītā</i> acheté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krayavikraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krayavikraya</i> [<a href="#entry-vikraya" class="skt-link">vikraya</a>] <a href="#entry-krayavikraya" class="skt-link">m.</a> achat et vente, commerce <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>krayavikrayau</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-kraya" class="skt-link">kraya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krayya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रय्य</span> <i>krayya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-krii" class="skt-link">krī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krayya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krayya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krayyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krayyā</i> destiné à être acheté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kreya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रेय</span> <i>kreya</i> [<a href="#entry-krii" class="skt-link">krī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kreya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kreya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kreyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kreyā</i> qu'on désire acheter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्री</span> <i>krī</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(krīṇāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(krīṇīte)</i> impft. <i>(akrīṇāt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>krīta</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(kretum)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>krītvā</i>, <i>-krīya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> acquérir; acheter (qqc. ⟨acc.⟩ à qqn. ⟨g. abl.⟩) — dés. md. <i>(cikrīṣate)</i> désirer acquérir — ca. <i>(krāpayati)</i> faire acheter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रीड्</span> <i>krīḍ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(krīḍati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>krīḍiṣyati</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>krīḍita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> jouer, s'amuser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'amuser à ⟨i.⟩ — ca. <i>(krīḍayati)</i> faire jouer; laisser jouer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_daa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रीडा</span> <i>krīḍā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-krii_d" class="skt-link">krīḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-krii_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> jeu, amusement, sport; jeu amoureux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aire de jeu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_daafga_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krīḍāṅgaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-afga_na" class="skt-link">aṅgaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-krii_daafga_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> aire de jeu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_daakauzalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krīḍākauśalya</i> [<a href="#entry-kauzalya" class="skt-link">kauśalya</a>] <a href="#entry-krii_daakauzalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Krīḍākauśalya, section des jeux (6 . 20) du <a href="#entry-Ub_rhajjyoti_saar_nava" class="skt-link">Bṛhajjyotiṣārṇava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_dana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रीडन</span> <i>krīḍana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-krii_d" class="skt-link">krīḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-krii_dana" class="skt-link">n.</a> jeu, amusement, sport.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_danaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krīḍanaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krii_danaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krii_danaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krii_danikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krīḍanikā</i> qui joue, qui s'amuse — <a href="#entry-krii_danaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krii_danaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> jouet — <a href="#entry-krii_danikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krīḍanikā</i> nounou occupant les enfants avec des jeux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_danakataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krīḍanakatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-krii_danakataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> état d'amusement, pratique de jeu.<br> <i>krīḍanakatayā</i> en manière de jeu.<br> <a href="#entry-krii_danaka" class="skt-link">krīḍanaka</a> <br> <a href="#entry-krii_dana" class="skt-link">krīḍana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_dita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रीडित</span> <i>krīḍita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-krii_d" class="skt-link">krīḍ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krii_dita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krii_dita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krii_ditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krīḍitā</i> qui a joué — <a href="#entry-krii_dita" class="skt-link">n.</a> jeu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_dotsava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krīḍotsava</i> [<a href="#entry-utsava" class="skt-link">utsava</a>] <a href="#entry-krii_dotsava" class="skt-link">m.</a> tournoi.<br> <a href="#entry-krii_daa" class="skt-link">krīḍā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_m">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रीं</span> <i>krīṃ</i> part. phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] son de l'<a href="#entry-aadyaabiija" class="skt-link">ādyābīja</a>, <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> d'évocation de <a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krii_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्री</span> <i>krī_1</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-krii" class="skt-link">krī</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-krii_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krii_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krii_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> acheté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रीत</span> <i>krīta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-krii" class="skt-link">krī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kriita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kriita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kriitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krītā</i> acheté; acquis.<br> <i>śatena</i> <i>krīta</i> acheté pour cent (roupies).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krimi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रिमि</span> <i>krimi</i> [<a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] <a href="#entry-krimi" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-k_rmi" class="skt-link">kṛmi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रिया</span> <i>kriyā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> acte, action, exécution, performance (de ⟨iic.⟩); activité, travail, œuvre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rite, cérémonie; not. rite funéraire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kriyā «Cérémonie», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>; elle épousa <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>, dont elle eut comme fils <a href="#entry-Uda_n_da" class="skt-link">Daṇḍa</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaya" class="skt-link">Naya</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvinaya" class="skt-link">Vinaya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kriyā_1, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ukratu" class="skt-link">Kratu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. verbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaakaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyākāla</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_1" class="skt-link">kāla_1</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaakaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. [<a href="#entry-aayurveda" class="skt-link">āyurveda</a>] temps approprié à commencer le traitement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyākośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. lexique de verbes, arrange en thésaurus; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaakhyaatacandrikaa" class="skt-link">Ākhyātacandrikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaakrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyākrama</i> [<a href="#entry-krama" class="skt-link">krama</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaakrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. mode conditionnel; cf. <a href="#entry-kriyaatipatti" class="skt-link">kriyātipatti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaapada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyāpada</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaapada" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. forme verbale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaaprabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyāprabandha</i> [<a href="#entry-prabandha" class="skt-link">prabandha</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaaprabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> action ininterrompue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaatipatti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyātipatti</i> [<a href="#entry-atipatti" class="skt-link">atipatti</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaatipatti" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. suffixe du conditionnel; cf. <a href="#entry-l_rf" class="skt-link">lṛṅ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaavibhakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyāvibhakti</i> [<a href="#entry-vibhakti" class="skt-link">vibhakti</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaavibhakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. désinence du verbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaavikalpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyāvikalpa</i> [<a href="#entry-vikalpa" class="skt-link">vikalpa</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaavikalpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> arrangement d'un ouvrage avec des figures de rhétorique [<a href="#entry-alafkaara" class="skt-link">alaṃkāra</a>], l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaavize_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyāviśeṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vize_sa_na" class="skt-link">viśeṣaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaavize_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. [«ce qui précise une action»] adverbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyāyoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [mod.] pratique du <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> intégrée à la vie active, doctrine enseignée par <a href="#entry-Uyogaananda" class="skt-link">Yogānanda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyaazabda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kriyāśabda</i> [<a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a>] <a href="#entry-kriyaazabda" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. mot d'action; forme verbale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dérivé verbal primaire; syn. <a href="#entry-k_rdanta" class="skt-link">kṛdanta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kriyaa" class="skt-link">kriyā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kriyamaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रियमाण</span> <i>kriyamāṇa</i> [ppr. ps. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kriyamaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kriyamaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kriyamaa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kriyamāṇā</i> étant fait, étant accompli — <a href="#entry-kriyamaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. part du <a href="#entry-karma" class="skt-link">karma</a> à venir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kro_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रोड</span> <i>kroḍa</i> <a href="#entry-kro_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kro_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> poitrine, thorax <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> intérieur d'un objet, creux, cavité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> verrat, sanglier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kroḍa «Cronos», un nom de <a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a> (Saturne) — <a href="#entry-kro_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kroḍā</i> sein; ventre — <a href="#entry-kro_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kroḍī</i> truie, laie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. Κρονος.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kro_s_t_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रोष्टृ</span> <i>kroṣṭṛ</i> var. <i>kroṣṭu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kruz" class="skt-link">kruś</a>] <a href="#entry-kro_s_t_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. chacal [«qui crie»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kroṣṭā, deuxième fils de <a href="#entry-Uyadu" class="skt-link">Yadu</a> — <a href="#entry-kro_s_trii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kroṣṭrī</i> chacal femelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kro_s_tu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रोष्टु</span> <i>kroṣṭu</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-kro_s_t_r" class="skt-link">kroṣṭṛ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kro_s_tuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kroṣṭuka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kro_s_tuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. chacal — <a href="#entry-kro_s_tukii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kroṣṭukī</i> natu. chacal femelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kroṣṭukī, fille de <a href="#entry-Ukrodhavazaa" class="skt-link">Krodhavaśā</a>, génitrice des singes jaunes.<br> <a href="#entry-kro_s_tu" class="skt-link">kroṣṭu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krodh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रोध्</span> <i>krodh</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-krudh_1" class="skt-link">krudh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(krodhayati)</i> irriter, mettre en colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रोध</span> <i>krodha</i> [<a href="#entry-krodh" class="skt-link">krodh</a>] <a href="#entry-krodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> colère; fureur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Krodha, fils de <a href="#entry-Ulobha" class="skt-link">Lobha</a> et de <a href="#entry-Unik_rti" class="skt-link">Nikṛti</a> personnifiant la colère — <a href="#entry-krodhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krodhā</i> myth. np. de Krodhā «Colère», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>; elle engendra tous les animaux et monstres carnassiers, tels les ogres [<a href="#entry-pizaacaas" class="skt-link">piśācās</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukrodhavazaa" class="skt-link">Krodhavaśā</a>.<br> <i>krodham</i> <i>gam</i> se mettre en colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krodhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रोधन</span> <i>krodhana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-krodh" class="skt-link">krodh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krodhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krodhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krodhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krodhanā</i> irascible; en colère — <a href="#entry-krodhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Krodhana «Irascible», personnifiant l'an 59 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krodhaparyaakula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krodhaparyākula</i> [<a href="#entry-paryaakula" class="skt-link">paryākula</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krodhaparyaakula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krodhaparyaakula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krodhaparyaakulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krodhaparyākulā</i> rempli de colère, troublé par la colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krodhavaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krodhavaśa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaza" class="skt-link">vaśa</a>] <a href="#entry-krodhavaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> pouvoir de la colère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>krodhavaśās</i> myth. esprits mauvais — <a href="#entry-krodhavazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krodhavaśā</i> myth. np. de Krodhavaśā, fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>; elle engendra les animaux par ses dix filles <a href="#entry-Um_rgii" class="skt-link">Mṛgī</a>, <a href="#entry-Um_rgamandaa" class="skt-link">Mṛgamandā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uharii" class="skt-link">Harī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhadramaataa" class="skt-link">Bhadramātā</a>, <a href="#entry-Umaatafgii_1" class="skt-link">Mātaṅgī_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzaarduulii" class="skt-link">Śārdūlī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzvetaa" class="skt-link">Śvetā</a>, <a href="#entry-Usurabhii_1" class="skt-link">Surabhī_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Usurasaa" class="skt-link">Surasā</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukadruu" class="skt-link">Kadrū</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-krodha" class="skt-link">krodha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krodhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रोधिन्</span> <i>krodhin</i> [<a href="#entry-krodha" class="skt-link">krodha</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krodhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krodhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krodhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krodhinī</i> colérique, violent — <a href="#entry-krodhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Krodhī «Brutal», personnifiant l'an 38 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kroza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रोश</span> <i>krośa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kruz" class="skt-link">kruś</a>] <a href="#entry-kroza" class="skt-link">m.</a> cri, appel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> portée de voix (mesure de distance, environ 3 km).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krozamaatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krośamātra</i> [<a href="#entry-maatra" class="skt-link">mātra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krozamaatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krozamaatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krozamaatraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krośamātrā</i> qui est à portée de voix.<br> <a href="#entry-kroza" class="skt-link">krośa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krozat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रोशत्</span> <i>krośat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-kruz" class="skt-link">kruś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-krozat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-krozat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-krozantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krośantī</i> se lamentant.<br> <i>krośatas</i> adv. en dépit des lamentations de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krudh_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रुध्</span> <i>krudh_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(krudhyati)</i> pft. <i>(cukroda)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kruddha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'irriter contre qqn. ⟨g. dat.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être irrité, se mettre en colère — ca. <i>(krodhayati)</i> irriter, mettre en colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-krudh_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रुध्</span> <i>krudh_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-krudh_1" class="skt-link">krudh_1</a>] <a href="#entry-krudh_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> colère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>krudhā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kruura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रूर</span> <i>krūra</i> [<a href="#entry-kravis" class="skt-link">kravis</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kruura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kruura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kruuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>krūrā</i> sanglant, cruel, méchant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> effrayant; effroyable — <a href="#entry-kruura" class="skt-link">n.</a> cruauté, méchanceté; rudesse; acte cruel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>crudelis</i>; fr. cruel.<br> <i>krūram</i> adv. terriblement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kruurad_rz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krūradṛś</i> [<a href="#entry-d_rz_2" class="skt-link">dṛś_2</a>] <a href="#entry-kruurad_rz" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Krūradṛṭ, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a> «Qui a le mauvais œil»; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] d'un regard il noircit le palais d'or de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> qui l'avait enfermé dans une citerne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kruuragraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krūragraha</i> [<a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a>] <a href="#entry-kruuragraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. planète malfaisante; opp. <a href="#entry-saumyagraha" class="skt-link">saumyagraha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kruuralocana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>krūralocana</i> [<a href="#entry-locana" class="skt-link">locana</a>] <a href="#entry-kruuralocana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Krūralocana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a> «Qui a le mauvais œil»; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukruurad_r_t" class="skt-link">Krūradṛṭ</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kruura" class="skt-link">krūra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kruurataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रूरता</span> <i>krūratā</i> [<a href="#entry-kruura" class="skt-link">krūra</a><a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-kruurataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cruauté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kruz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रुश्</span> <i>kruś</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(krośati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kruṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> crier, gémir, pleurer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>crocito</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kru_nc_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रुञ्च्</span> <i>kruñc_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-kuc" class="skt-link">kuc</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kruñcati)</i> courber, tordre; rapetisser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kru_nc_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रुञ्च्</span> <i>kruñc_2</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-kru_nc_1" class="skt-link">kruñc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-kru_nc_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> grue, héron <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kruñca</i> id.; syn. <a href="#entry-krau_nca" class="skt-link">krauñca</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ktavatu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्तवतु</span> <i>ktavatu</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>]; il forme des ppa. comme <i>pakvavat</i> «cuisinier», signifiant l'agent du verbe vu comme dénotant l'objet produit par son action
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ktvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्त्वा</span> <i>ktvā</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>]; forme des absolutifs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_dya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुड्य</span> <i>kuḍya</i> <a href="#entry-ku_dya" class="skt-link">n.</a> mur, cloison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण्ड्</span> <i>kuṇḍ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(kuṇḍate)</i> brûler — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kuṇḍati)</i> mutiler — ca. <i>(kuṇḍayati)</i> protéger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण्ड</span> <i>kuṇḍa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ku_n_d" class="skt-link">kuṇḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> pot, jarre, bol, pichet; récipient; not. pot à eau — <a href="#entry-ku_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> fosse dans le sol, puits; bassin, pièce d'eau sacrée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. not. fosse de briques servant d'autel védique — <a href="#entry-ku_n_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇḍī</i> récipient en forme de bol — <a href="#entry-ku_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇḍā</i> mutilation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_daka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण्डक</span> <i>kuṇḍaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ku_n_d" class="skt-link">kuṇḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_n_daka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_daka" class="skt-link">n.</a> pot — <a href="#entry-ku_n_dikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇḍikā</i> pot; pot à eau d'ascète.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_dala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण्डल</span> <i>kuṇḍala</i> <a href="#entry-ku_n_dala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_dala" class="skt-link">n.</a> boucle d'oreille, anneau, bracelet — <a href="#entry-ku_n_dalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇḍalī</i> astr. horoscope.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_dalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण्डलिन्</span> <i>kuṇḍalin</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_n_dala" class="skt-link">kuṇḍala</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_n_dalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_dalin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_dalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇḍalinī</i> qui a des boucles d'oreille — <a href="#entry-ku_n_dalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇḍalinī</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] «la Lovée», épith. de la <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> sous la forme de la serpente Kuṇḍalinī, force psychique qui s'élève lors du <i>kuṇḍalinīyoga</i> pour éveiller successivement les 6 centres d'énergie [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] en les transperçant; cf. <a href="#entry-Usu_sum_naa" class="skt-link">Suṣumṇā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_dapaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṇḍapāya</i> [<a href="#entry-paaya" class="skt-link">pāya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_n_dapaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_dapaaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_dapaayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇḍapāyā</i> soc. (sacrifice) où l'on boit (le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>) dans un bol.<br> <a href="#entry-ku_n_da" class="skt-link">kuṇḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_dina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण्डिन</span> <i>kuṇḍina</i> <a href="#entry-ku_n_dina" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Kuṇḍina, un des 100 fils de <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a> — <a href="#entry-ku_n_dina" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Kuṇḍina_1, capitale du pays <a href="#entry-Uvidarbha" class="skt-link">Vidarbha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. c'était la capitale du roi <a href="#entry-Ubhiima_1" class="skt-link">Bhīma_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_th">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण्ठ्</span> <i>kuṇṭh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kuṇṭhati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kuṇṭhita</i><i>)</i> être estropié, infirme; être émoussé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être paresseux; être obtus, stupide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण्ठ</span> <i>kuṇṭha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ku_n_th" class="skt-link">kuṇṭh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇṭhā</i> émoussé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> stupide, abruti; lent, paresseux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_n_thita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण्ठित</span> <i>kuṇṭhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ku_n_th" class="skt-link">kuṇṭh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_n_thita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_thita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_n_thitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇṭhitā</i> émoussé; obtus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुण</span> <i>kuṇa</i> <a href="#entry-ku_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> mite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_napa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुणप</span> <i>kuṇapa</i> <a href="#entry-ku_napa" class="skt-link">n.</a> cadavre; charogne; engrais d'origine animale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excrément — a. <a href="#entry-ku_napa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_napa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_napaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṇapā</i> qui empeste comme un cadavre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_napajala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṇapajala</i> [<a href="#entry-jala" class="skt-link">jala</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_napajala" class="skt-link">n.</a> purin.<br> <a href="#entry-ku_napa" class="skt-link">kuṇapa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुष्ठ</span> <i>kuṣṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Saussurea costus</i>, chardon de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>, grande astéracée dont la racine est utilisée en médecine — <a href="#entry-ku_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> lèpre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_s_thaanvita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṣṭhānvita</i> [<a href="#entry-anvita" class="skt-link">anvita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_s_thaanvita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_s_thaanvita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_s_thaanvitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṣṭhānvitā</i> affligé de lèpre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_s_thin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṣṭhin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_s_thin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_s_thin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_s_thinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṣṭhinī</i> lépreux.<br> <a href="#entry-ku_s_tha" class="skt-link">kuṣṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_saa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुषाण</span> <i>kuṣāṇa</i> <a href="#entry-ku_saa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du peuple Kuṣāṇa (Scythe) parti de Bactriane au début de l'ère chrétienne pour fonder en Inde du Nord une dynastie <a href="#entry-zaka" class="skt-link">śaka</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukani_ska_1" class="skt-link">Kaniṣka_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_siitaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुषीतक</span> <i>kuṣītaka</i> <a href="#entry-ku_siitaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> sorte d'oiseau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> np. d'homme Kuṣītaka.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_t">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट्</span> <i>kuṭ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(kuṭati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kuṭita</i><i>)</i> être courbe; se courber; pencher; se tordre, vriller <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être fourbe, être malhonnête.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट्ट</span> <i>kuṭṭa</i> <a href="#entry-ku_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> broyage, concassage — ifc. broyage par — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(kuṭṭayati)</i> écraser, broyer; pulvériser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. résoudre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_t_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट्टक</span> <i>kuṭṭaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ku_t_ta" class="skt-link">kuṭṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_t_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_t_taka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_t_tikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭṭikā</i> ifc. qui coupe, qui casse, qui broie — <a href="#entry-ku_t_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> action de broyer, de casser, de découper <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. résolution d'équations linéaires diophantiennes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_t_tana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṭṭana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ku_t_ta" class="skt-link">kuṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_t_tana" class="skt-link">n.</a> action de broyer, de casser, de couper, de battre — <a href="#entry-ku_t_tanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭṭanī</i> sorte de lance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> catin, putain.<br> <a href="#entry-ku_t_taka" class="skt-link">kuṭṭaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_t_tanii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट्टनी</span> <i>kuṭṭanī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-ku_t_tana" class="skt-link">kuṭṭana</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_t_tanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> catin, putain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_t_taniimata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṭṭanīmata</i> [<a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">mata</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_t_taniimata" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kuṭṭanīmata «Conseils de la catin», poème narratif libertin du 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle en vers dû à <a href="#entry-Udaamodaragupta" class="skt-link">Dāmodaragupta</a>, de genre érotico-satirique (Mālatī, une courtisane débutante de Bénarès, demande le conseil d'une professionnelle expérimentée, qui lui explique l'art de séduire et de voler les hommes fortunés).<br> <a href="#entry-ku_t_tanii" class="skt-link">kuṭṭanī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_t_tima">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट्टिम</span> <i>kuṭṭima</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_t_tin" class="skt-link">kuṭṭin</a><a href="#entry--ma" class="skt-link">-ma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_t_tima" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_t_tima" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_t_timaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭṭimā</i> pavé; couvert de mosaïques — <a href="#entry-ku_t_tima" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_t_tima" class="skt-link">n.</a> sol pavé, carrelé; carrelage en mosaïque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sol aplani pour construire un bâtiment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_t_tin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट्टिन्</span> <i>kuṭṭin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ku_t_ta" class="skt-link">kuṭṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_t_tin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_t_tin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_t_ti_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭṭiṇī</i> qui broie, qui concasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_t_tita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट्टित</span> <i>kuṭṭita</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ku_t_ta" class="skt-link">kuṭṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_t_tita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_t_tita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_t_titaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭṭitā</i> broyé, moulu; battu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> écrasé; aplati.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट</span> <i>kuṭa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ku_t" class="skt-link">kuṭ</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> maison; famille — <a href="#entry-ku_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> bastion; fort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> marteau; hache.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुटक</span> <i>kuṭaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ku_t" class="skt-link">kuṭ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_taka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_tikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭikā</i> courbé; tordu — <a href="#entry-ku_tika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>kuṭika</i> id. — <a href="#entry-ku_taka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>kuṭaka</i> araire — <a href="#entry-ku_tikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭikā</i> hutte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. hutte funéraire pour abriter les cendres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_thaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुठार</span> <i>kuṭhāra</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-ku_ta" class="skt-link">kuṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_thaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hache.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_thaarikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṭhārikā</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_thaarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> hachette.<br> <a href="#entry-ku_thaara" class="skt-link">kuṭhāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुटि</span> <i>kuṭi</i> var. <i>kuṭī</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ku_t" class="skt-link">kuṭ</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> courbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hutte; échoppe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_ticara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṭicara</i> [<a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">cara</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_ticara" class="skt-link">m.</a> crocodile [«qui emporte la hutte (dans l'eau)»].<br> <a href="#entry-ku_ti" class="skt-link">kuṭi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_tii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुटी</span> <i>kuṭī</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_ti" class="skt-link">kuṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> courbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hutte; échoppe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_tiik_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṭīkṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">kṛta</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_tiik_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> (tissu) frisé; not. vêtement de laine.<br> <a href="#entry-ku_tii" class="skt-link">kuṭī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_tila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुटिल</span> <i>kuṭila</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_ti" class="skt-link">kuṭi</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_tila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_tila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_tilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭilā</i> plié, courbé, tordu, arqué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tortueux, fourbe; malhonnête — <a href="#entry-ku_tila" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Kuṭila «Bossu» — <a href="#entry-ku_tilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭilā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Kutilā «Serpentine», épith. de la <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_1" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_tilaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṭilaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_tilaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_tilaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_tilikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭilikā</i> courbé, tordu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aux aguets.<br> <a href="#entry-ku_tila" class="skt-link">kuṭila</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_tira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुटिर</span> <i>kuṭira</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_ti" class="skt-link">kuṭi</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_tira" class="skt-link">n.</a> hutte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kuṭīra</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_tumba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुटुम्ब</span> <i>kuṭumba</i> <a href="#entry-ku_tumba" class="skt-link">n.</a> famille, ménage, maison <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> charges de famille; soucis familiaux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lien familial; progéniture.<br> <i>kuṭumbārtham</i> adv. pour le compte de la famille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_tumbaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṭumbaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_tumbaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <i>ayaṃ</i> <i>nijaḥ</i> <i>paro</i> <i>veti</i> <i>gaṇanā</i> <i>laghucetasām</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>udāracaritānāṃ</i> <i>tu</i> <i>vasudhaivakuṭumbakam</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-Uhitopadeza" class="skt-link">Hitopadeśa</a>] Ceci est aux miens, ceci est à un étranger, ainsi calculent les mesquins; alors que pour les hommes généreux le Monde entier est leur famille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_tumbin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuṭumbin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_tumbin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_tumbin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_tumbinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭumbinī</i> qui vit en famille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> membre de la famille (y compris les serviteurs) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>kuṭumbinau</i> le père et la mère de famille — <a href="#entry-ku_tumbinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuṭumbinī</i> mère de famille.<br> <a href="#entry-ku_tumba" class="skt-link">kuṭumba</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कु</span> <i>ku_1</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_1" class="skt-link">ka_1</a>] pf. d'interrogation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> particule péjorative; mauvais, mal, méchant, honteux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> quelque peu, un peu, plutôt <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bien, bon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कु</span> <i>ku_2</i> <a href="#entry-ku_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kubera">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुबेर</span> <i>kubera</i> [<a href="#entry-kuvera" class="skt-link">kuvera</a>] <a href="#entry-kubera" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kubera «Difforme», issu du géniteur [<a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a>] <a href="#entry-Upulastya" class="skt-link">Pulastya</a> et de la Parole [<a href="#entry-Ugo" class="skt-link">Go</a>]; fâché de sa dévotion envers <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, dont il était exclu, <a href="#entry-Upulastya" class="skt-link">Pulastya</a> se recréa sous la forme de <a href="#entry-Uvizravaa" class="skt-link">Viśravā</a> pour l'enfanter d'<a href="#entry-Ui_davi_daa" class="skt-link">Iḍaviḍā</a>; <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> est le régent des océans et des rivières; il fit de sévères austérités pour obtenir de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> d'être un protecteur de l'univers [<a href="#entry-lokapaala" class="skt-link">lokapāla</a>], et d'obtenir le titre de Roi des rois [<a href="#entry-Uraajaraaja" class="skt-link">Rājarāja</a>]; il est le gardien [<a href="#entry-dikpaala" class="skt-link">dikpāla</a>] de la direction du Nord; Crésus indien, <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> est dieu de la richesse, gardien des neuf trésors [<a href="#entry-navanidhi" class="skt-link">navanidhi</a>], chef des génies [<a href="#entry-yak_saas" class="skt-link">yakṣās</a>] et des esprits cachés [<a href="#entry-guhyakaas" class="skt-link">guhyakās</a>]; les presqu'hommes [<a href="#entry-kinnaraas" class="skt-link">kinnarās</a>] et les chimères [<a href="#entry-kimpuru_saas" class="skt-link">kimpuruṣās</a>] sont ses serviteurs; son demi-frère <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> usurpa son trône de <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a> et lui déroba son char volant Fleuri [<a href="#entry-Upu_spaka" class="skt-link">Puṣpaka</a>]; il établit sa capitale <a href="#entry-Ualakaa" class="skt-link">Alakā</a> sur le Mont <a href="#entry-Ugandhamaadana" class="skt-link">Gandhamādana</a>; son palais, magnifique, fut construit par <a href="#entry-Uvizvakarmaa" class="skt-link">Viśvakarmā</a>; son jardin merveilleux <a href="#entry-Ucaitraratha" class="skt-link">Caitraratha</a> se trouve sur le Mont <a href="#entry-Umandara" class="skt-link">Mandara</a>; il est représenté difforme et obèse [<a href="#entry-kumbhaa_n_da" class="skt-link">kumbhāṇḍa</a>], les yeux exorbités par l'alcool; on dit qqf. qu'il a un œil, trois jambes et huit dents; il est l'ami de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> [<a href="#entry-Uharasakhi" class="skt-link">Harasakhi</a>]; la mangouste [<a href="#entry-nakula" class="skt-link">nakula</a>] lui apporte des trésors; sa monture est un homme [<a href="#entry-naravaahana" class="skt-link">naravāhana</a>], un cheval ou une mangouste; <a href="#entry-U_rddhi" class="skt-link">Ṛddhi</a> ou <a href="#entry-Ucaarvii" class="skt-link">Cārvī</a> est son épouse; il a pour fils <a href="#entry-Unalakuubara" class="skt-link">Nalakūbara</a> et <a href="#entry-Uma_nigriiva" class="skt-link">Maṇigrīva</a>, et pour fille <a href="#entry-Umiinaak_sii" class="skt-link">Mīnākṣī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Udhanezvara" class="skt-link">Dhaneśvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Unararaaja" class="skt-link">Nararāja</a>, <a href="#entry-Umayuraaja" class="skt-link">Mayurāja</a>, <a href="#entry-Uratnagarbha" class="skt-link">Ratnagarbha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaizrava_na" class="skt-link">Vaiśravaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Upaa_ncika" class="skt-link">Pāñcika</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuberapuujaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuberapūjā</i> [<a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>] <a href="#entry-kuberapuujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-dhanvantaritrayodazii" class="skt-link">dhanvantaritrayodaśī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kubera" class="skt-link">kubera</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kubja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुब्ज</span> <i>kubja</i> a. <a href="#entry-kubja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kubja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kubjaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kubjā</i> bossu, courbé — <a href="#entry-kubja" class="skt-link">m.</a> bossu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. κυπτω; lat. <i>gibbus</i>.<br> <i>kubjībhū</i> se courber.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kubjaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kubjaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kubjaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kubjaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kubjikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kubjikā</i> bossu, courbé; infirme — <a href="#entry-kubjaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> bossu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Rosa moschata</i>, rosier musqué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Trapa bispinosa</i>, plante aquatique des lacs du Cahemire; son fruit, la noix <i>sinhara</i>, est un aliment et une plante médicinale — f. cf. <a href="#entry-kubjikaa" class="skt-link">kubjikā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kubja" class="skt-link">kubja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kubjikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुब्जिका</span> <i>kubjikā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-kubjaka" class="skt-link">kubjaka</a>] <a href="#entry-kubjikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>] np. de la déesse Kubjikā, aspect androgyne de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>; lors de son festival au Népal, elle est représentée par une fillette de 8 ans; son culte est expliqué dans le <a href="#entry-Umanthaanabhairava" class="skt-link">Manthānabhairava</a> <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kubjikaamata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kubjikāmata</i> [<a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">mata</a>] <a href="#entry-kubjikaamata" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] np. du Kubjikāmata, doctrine <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kubjikaatantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kubjikātantra</i> [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-kubjikaatantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kubjikātantra, ouvrage ésotérique de tradition <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kubjikaayantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kubjikāyantra</i> [<a href="#entry-yantra" class="skt-link">yantra</a>] <a href="#entry-kubjikaayantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. diagramme géométrique de <a href="#entry-Ukubjikaa" class="skt-link">Kubjikā</a>, support des dix puissances [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] de la Déesse; autour du <a href="#entry-bindu" class="skt-link">bindu</a> central, siège de <a href="#entry-Usiddhilak_smii" class="skt-link">Siddhilakṣmī</a>, un triangle puis un hexagone portent les neuf autres.<br> <a href="#entry-kubjikaa" class="skt-link">kubjikā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुच्</span> <i>kuc</i> var. <i>kuñc</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(kucati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kuñcita</i>, <i>kucita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> courber — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(kuñcate)</i> se contracter, se resserrer; friser — ca. <i>(kuñcayati)</i> courber; froncer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुच</span> <i>kuca</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kuc" class="skt-link">kuc</a>] <a href="#entry-kuca" class="skt-link">m.</a> poitrine, sein <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>kucau</i> les (deux) seins (de la femme).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kucabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kucabandha</i> [<a href="#entry-bandha" class="skt-link">bandha</a>] <a href="#entry-kucabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soutien-gorge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kucazaa_tii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kucaśāṭī</i> [<a href="#entry-zaa_tii" class="skt-link">śāṭī</a>] <a href="#entry-kucazaa_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> vêtement couvrant la poitrine.<br> <a href="#entry-kuca" class="skt-link">kuca</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kucela">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुचेल</span> <i>kucela</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-cela" class="skt-link">cela</a>] <a href="#entry-kucela" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kucela «Mal vêtu», épith. du pauvre <a href="#entry-Usudaama" class="skt-link">Sudāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kucumaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुचुमार</span> <i>kucumāra</i> <a href="#entry-kucumaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de Kucumāra, auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uaupani_sadaadhikara_na" class="skt-link">Aupaniṣadādhikaraṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kud_r_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुदृष्टि</span> <i>kudṛṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-d_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">dṛṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-kud_r_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> vue erronée; système non orthodoxe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kudaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुदाल</span> <i>kudāla</i> <a href="#entry-kudaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Bauhinia variegata</i>, ébène de montagne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kudaalasafgama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kudālasaṃgama</i> [<a href="#entry-safgama" class="skt-link">saṃgama</a>] <a href="#entry-kudaalasa_mgama" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Kudālasaṃgama, lieu de pélerinage au confluent de la <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_naa_1" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇā_1</a> et de la <a href="#entry-Umalaprabhaa" class="skt-link">Malaprabhā</a> au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>; c'est un lieu de pélerinage <a href="#entry-lifgaayat" class="skt-link">liṅgāyat</a> important.<br> <a href="#entry-kudaala" class="skt-link">kudāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kudeha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुदेह</span> <i>kudeha</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-deha" class="skt-link">deha</a>] <a href="#entry-kudeha" class="skt-link">m.</a> corps misérable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kudeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुदेश</span> <i>kudeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-deza" class="skt-link">deśa</a>] <a href="#entry-kudeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> mauvais pays (où la vie est difficile).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kudhii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुधी</span> <i>kudhī</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-dhii_2" class="skt-link">dhī_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kudhii" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kudhii" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kudhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> peu intelligent, sot, niais; fou.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kufkuma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुङ्कुम</span> <i>kuṅkuma</i> <a href="#entry-kufkuma" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Crocus sativus</i>, crocus; safran <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arabe curcum; gr. κροκος; lat. <i>crocus</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुह</span> <i>kuha</i> <a href="#entry-kuha" class="skt-link">m.</a> tromperie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुहक</span> <i>kuhaka</i> [<a href="#entry-kuha" class="skt-link">kuha</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kuhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> tricheur; prestidigitateur; imposteur — <a href="#entry-kuhaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> tour de magie; tromperie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> stimulus du rire, chatouillement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुहन</span> <i>kuhana</i> [<a href="#entry-kuha" class="skt-link">kuha</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuhanā</i> envieux, hypocrite — <a href="#entry-kuhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. d'homme «Tartuffe» — <a href="#entry-kuhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuhanā</i> hypocrisie; envie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुहर</span> <i>kuhara</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_2" class="skt-link">ku_2</a>-<a href="#entry-hara" class="skt-link">hara</a>] <a href="#entry-kuhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> cavité, caverne; trou, terrier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुज</span> <i>kuja</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_2" class="skt-link">ku_2</a>-<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] <a href="#entry-kuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Kuja «né de la Terre», un nom de Mars; cf. <a href="#entry-Uafgaaraka" class="skt-link">Aṅgāraka</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhuumija" class="skt-link">Bhūmija</a>, <a href="#entry-Umafgala" class="skt-link">Maṅgala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuk_si">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुक्षि</span> <i>kukṣi</i> <a href="#entry-kuk_si" class="skt-link">m.</a> ventre; bas-ventre; matrice <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cavité en gén. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du roi Kukṣi «Ventru», fils aîné d'<a href="#entry-Uik_svaaku" class="skt-link">Ikṣvāku</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uvikuk_si" class="skt-link">Vikukṣi</a> et <a href="#entry-Uzazaada" class="skt-link">Śaśāda</a>; <a href="#entry-Ukakutstha" class="skt-link">Kakutstha</a> est son fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kukku_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुक्कुट</span> <i>kukkuṭa</i> [onomatopée] <a href="#entry-kukku_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. coq — <a href="#entry-kukku_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kukkuṭī</i> poule.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kukku_taasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kukkuṭāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-aasana" class="skt-link">āsana</a>] <a href="#entry-kukku_taasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] posture partant de <a href="#entry-padmaasana" class="skt-link">padmāsana</a>, les mains passant entre les cuisses et les mollets pour se soulever de terre.<br> <a href="#entry-kukku_ta" class="skt-link">kukkuṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kukku_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kukkuṭaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kukku_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Phasianus Gallus</i>, faisan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. fils d'un tribal [<a href="#entry-ni_saada" class="skt-link">niṣāda</a>] par une servante [<a href="#entry-zuudraa" class="skt-link">śūdrā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kukku_tamayuurii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kukkuṭamayūrī</i> [<a href="#entry-mayuurii" class="skt-link">mayūrī</a>] <a href="#entry-kukku_tamayuurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> mâle et femelle paons.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kukkura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुक्कुर</span> <i>kukkura</i> <a href="#entry-kukkura" class="skt-link">m.</a> chien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kul">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुल्</span> <i>kul</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kolati)</i> accumuler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुल</span> <i>kula</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kul" class="skt-link">kul</a>] <a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">n.</a> troupe, foule; horde; troupeau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. famille; race; clan; tribu, caste; noblesse, dynastie royale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> maison familiale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] doctrine de la bonne famille; [<a href="#entry-Uaanandabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Ānandabhaṭṭa</a>] elle prescrit 9 obligations: bonne conduite [<a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">ācāra</a>], discipline [<a href="#entry-vinaya" class="skt-link">vinaya</a>], acquisition de la connaissance [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>], bonne position sociale [<a href="#entry-prati_s_thaa_2" class="skt-link">pratiṣṭhā_2</a>], pélerinage [<a href="#entry-tiirthadarzana" class="skt-link">tīrthadarśana</a>], obédience aux textes sacrés [<a href="#entry-ni_s_thaa_2" class="skt-link">niṣṭhā_2</a>], endogamie [<a href="#entry-aav_rtti" class="skt-link">āvṛtti</a>], pratique des austérités [<a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a>] et générosité [<a href="#entry-daana" class="skt-link">dāna</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a> — <a href="#entry-kulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kulā</i> ifc. id.<br> <i>caurasyakula</i> bande de voleurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulaacaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulācāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaara" class="skt-link">ācāra</a>] <a href="#entry-kulaacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [tantr.] la «voie du clan», doctrine développée dans le <a href="#entry-Ukulaar_nava" class="skt-link">Kulārṇava</a> <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>, enseignant le monisme par la transgression des contraires; cf. <a href="#entry-makaarapa_ncaka" class="skt-link">makārapañcaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulaadhiina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulādhīna</i> [<a href="#entry-adhiina" class="skt-link">adhīna</a>] <a href="#entry-kulaadhiina" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. monastère [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>] du village d'origine d'une famille, en gén. <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुलाल</span> <i>kulāla</i> <a href="#entry-kulaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> potier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulaalacakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulālacakra</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] <a href="#entry-kulaalacakra" class="skt-link">n.</a> tour (de potier).<br> <a href="#entry-kulaala" class="skt-link">kulāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulaar_nava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulārṇava</i> [<a href="#entry-ar_nava" class="skt-link">arṇava</a>] <a href="#entry-kulaar_nava" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>kulārṇavatantra</i>] lit. «Océan de la voie du clan», np. du Kulārṇava <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>, de doctrine shivaïte non-dualiste <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>; il fut compilé au 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle — <a href="#entry-kulaar_nava" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>kulārṇavatantra</i>] lit. id.<br> <a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulacaritra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulacaritra</i> [<a href="#entry-caritra" class="skt-link">caritra</a>] <a href="#entry-kulacaritra" class="skt-link">n.</a> coutume.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulacuu_daama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulacūḍāmaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-cuu_daama_ni" class="skt-link">cūḍāmaṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-kulacuu_daama_ni" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>kulacūḍāmaṇitantra</i>] lit. np. du Kulacūḍāmaṇi <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>, texte <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a> tardif (14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuladevataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuladevatā</i> [<a href="#entry-devataa" class="skt-link">devatā</a>] <a href="#entry-kuladevataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. divinité tutélaire d'une lignée (opp. <a href="#entry-i_s_tadevataa" class="skt-link">iṣṭadevatā</a>, divinité d'élection) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>kuladevī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuladharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuladharma</i> [<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] <a href="#entry-kuladharma" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [«religion du clan»] rites tantriques; cf. <a href="#entry-kulaacaara" class="skt-link">kulācāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulaguru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulaguru</i> [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] <a href="#entry-kulaguru" class="skt-link">m.</a> précepteur de famille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुलक</span> <i>kulaka</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kulaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> noyau d'un fruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. suite de phrases partageant le gouvernement d'un verbe (ellipse) — ifc. multitude — <a href="#entry-kulaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> patron d'une guilde; artisan de bonne famille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulamuktikallolinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulamuktikallolinī</i> [<a href="#entry-mukti" class="skt-link">mukti</a>-<a href="#entry-kallolinii" class="skt-link">kallolinī</a>] <a href="#entry-kulamuktikallolinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Kulamuktikallolinī «Torrent de libération du clan», texte <a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">śākta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulapati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-kulapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> chef de famille; chef de clan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kulapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. recueil de récits et légendes concernant les origines d'une caste, d'une tribu ou d'une dynastie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulaputra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulaputra</i> [<a href="#entry-putra" class="skt-link">putra</a>] <a href="#entry-kulaputra" class="skt-link">m.</a> fils de (bonne) famille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulasantati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulasantati</i> [<a href="#entry-santati" class="skt-link">santati</a>] <a href="#entry-kulasantati" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [tantr.] la «descendance de la famille», rite symbolique [<a href="#entry-cakrapuujaa" class="skt-link">cakrapūjā</a>] effectué par le geste [<a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>] du toucher de son majeur-<a href="#entry-yoni" class="skt-link">yoni</a> avec son pouce-<a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>, évoquant l'union rituelle de sa <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> intérieure identifiée à <a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>-<a href="#entry-Uca_n_dikaa" class="skt-link">Caṇḍikā</a> avec l'Être Suprême <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulattha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulattha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] <a href="#entry-kulattha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Dolichos uniflorus</i>, légume à gousse utilisé pour nourrir le bétail.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulayaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulayāga</i> [<a href="#entry-yaaga" class="skt-link">yāga</a>] <a href="#entry-kulayaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-trika" class="skt-link">trika</a>] technique de transgression tantrique, lors de la phase d'expansion [<a href="#entry-vikaasa" class="skt-link">vikāsa</a>] de réalisation spirituelle; on y ignore les interdits de viande, d'alcool et de sexe; cf. <a href="#entry-duutii" class="skt-link">dūtī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulazekhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulaśekhara</i> [<a href="#entry-zekhara" class="skt-link">śekhara</a>] <a href="#entry-kulazekhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Kulaśekhara Perumāl, 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ des saints <i>ālvār</i>; il régnait au <a href="#entry-Umalaya" class="skt-link">Malaya</a> au 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il est l'auteur du poème dévotionnel <a href="#entry-Umukundamaalaa" class="skt-link">Mukundamālā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuliina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुलीन</span> <i>kulīna</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a><a href="#entry--ana" class="skt-link">-ana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuliina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuliina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuliinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kulīnā</i> noble, de noble origine, de bonne famille — ifc. de la famille de — <a href="#entry-kuliina" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. brâhmane du Bengale de haute classe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. adepte de la voie de la main gauche [<a href="#entry-kulaacaara" class="skt-link">kulācāra</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuliiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुलीय</span> <i>kulīya</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-kuliiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuliiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuliiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kulīyā</i> appartenant à la famille de ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुलिन्</span> <i>kulin</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kulin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kulin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kulinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kulinī</i> de bonne famille — <a href="#entry-kulinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kulinī</i> natu. bot. <i>Impatiens balsamina</i>, impatience.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulinda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुलिन्द</span> <i>kulinda</i> <a href="#entry-kulinda" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. nom d'un pays de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a> central <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Kulinda, titre princier de ce pays <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kulindās</i> géo. ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuliza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुलिश</span> <i>kuliśa</i> <a href="#entry-kuliza" class="skt-link">m.</a> hache, hachette — <a href="#entry-kuliza" class="skt-link">n.</a> var. <i>kulīśa</i> myth. np. du foudre d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-vajra" class="skt-link">vajra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> par ext. diamant — ifc. lit. titre d'ouvrages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulluuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुल्लूक</span> <i>kullūka</i> <a href="#entry-kulluuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kullūka <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, commentateur de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a> du 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur de la <a href="#entry-Umanvarthamuktaavalii" class="skt-link">Manvarthamuktāvalī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulpha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुल्फ</span> <i>kulpha</i> var. <i>gulpha</i> <a href="#entry-kulpha" class="skt-link">m.</a> cheville.
  </div>
</div>
