<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulphadaghna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kulphadaghna</i> [<a href="#entry-daghna" class="skt-link">daghna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kulphadaghna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kulphadaghna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kulphadaghnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kulphadaghnā</i> <a href="#entry-kulphadaghnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kulphadaghnī</i> qui a (de l'eau) jusqu'aux chevilles.<br> <a href="#entry-kulpha" class="skt-link">kulpha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuluuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुलूत</span> <i>kulūta</i> <a href="#entry-kuluuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. géo. np. du pays et du peuple Kulūta.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुल्य</span> <i>kulya</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kulya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kulya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kulyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kulyā</i> concernant la famille ou le clan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> de bonne famille — <a href="#entry-kulya" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme honorable — <a href="#entry-kulya" class="skt-link">n.</a> demande amicale concernant la famille; congratulation; condoléances — <a href="#entry-kulyaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kulyā_2</i> coutume familiale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kulyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुल्या</span> <i>kulyā</i> <a href="#entry-kulyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> ruisseau; canal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुमार</span> <i>kumāra</i> <a href="#entry-kumaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> garçon, jeune homme; fils <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prince; page; cavalier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kumāra «Prince», épith. du (toujours jeune) dieu <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> — <a href="#entry-kumaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> or pur — <a href="#entry-kumaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumārī</i> adolescente, jeune fille, vierge — <a href="#entry-kumaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme efféminé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaarabha_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumārabhaṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaarabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du prince-poète de Ceylan Kumārabhaṭṭa; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukumaaradaasa" class="skt-link">Kumāradāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaarabhiimezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumārabhīmeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-bhiimezvara" class="skt-link">bhīmeśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaarabhiimezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du <a href="#entry-zivalifga" class="skt-link">śivaliṅga</a> Kumārabhīmeśvara, établi au <a href="#entry-pa_ncaraamak_setra" class="skt-link">pañcarāmakṣetra</a> <a href="#entry-Ukumaararaama" class="skt-link">Kumārarāma</a> par <a href="#entry-Ukumaara" class="skt-link">Kumāra</a>; il est en calcaire blanc, de plus de 4 m de hauteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaaradaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumāradāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaaradaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du prince Ceylanais Kumāradāsa (~517–~526), auteur du <a href="#entry-Ujaanakiihara_na" class="skt-link">Jānakīharaṇa</a>; il est aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Ukumaarabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Kumārabhaṭṭa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaaragiri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumāragiri</i> [<a href="#entry-giri" class="skt-link">giri</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaaragiri" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Kumaragiri Reddy de Kondavīdu (1386–1402); grand amateur d'art, il laissa le gouvernement à son beau-frère Katayavema, et s'occupa d'organiser des festivals comme <a href="#entry-vasantotsava" class="skt-link">vasantotsava</a>; on le surnomma Karpūra <a href="#entry-Uvasanta" class="skt-link">Vasanta</a> Rāyalu; on lui doit le traité de danse <a href="#entry-Uvasantaraajiiya" class="skt-link">Vasantarājīya</a>; sa danseuse de cour Lakumadevī était renommée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaarajiiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumārajīva</i> [<a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">jīva</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaarajiiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du lettré brâhmane Kumārajīva (4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), qui répandit le bouddhisme en Asie Centrale et en Chine; né à Kucha (sur la route de la soie au nord du Taklamakan) du brâhmane Kumārāyaṇa et de la princesse koutchéenne Jīvakā, il voyagea avec sa mère en Inde ef fut ordonné moine bouddhiste dans la tradition <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a>; rentré au pays, lettré célébre, il fut amené à l'empereur de Chine pour diriger une équipe de traduction des textes bouddhiques sanskrits en chinois; il fixa en chinois la terminologie <a href="#entry-abhidharma" class="skt-link">abhidharma</a> du <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumāraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> enfant, garçon, jeune homme — <a href="#entry-kumaarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumārikā</i> fille, jeune fille, vierge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. l'une des 10 régions [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] formant le <a href="#entry-jambudviipa" class="skt-link">jambudvīpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaaralaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुमारलात</span> <i>kumāralāta</i> <a href="#entry-kumaaralaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du grammairien bd. Kumāralāta; il vivait à la fin du 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle à la cour <a href="#entry-Uku_saa_na" class="skt-link">Kuṣāṇa</a> de <a href="#entry-Utak_sazilaa" class="skt-link">Takṣaśilā</a>; il fonda l'école bd. <a href="#entry-sautraantika" class="skt-link">sautrāntika</a>; il écrivit une grammaire sanskrite inspirée (ou à l'origine) du <a href="#entry-Ukaatantra" class="skt-link">Kātantra</a>; on l'identifie parfois à <a href="#entry-Uazvagho_sa" class="skt-link">Aśvaghoṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaarapaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumārapāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaarapaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. du roi <a href="#entry-Ucaulukya" class="skt-link">Caulukya</a> Kumārapāla du <a href="#entry-Ugurjara" class="skt-link">Gurjara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaarapuur_nimaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumārapūrṇimā</i> [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaarapuur_nimaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pleine lune du mois <a href="#entry-aazvina" class="skt-link">āśvina</a>; on y célèbre <a href="#entry-Ukaarttikeya" class="skt-link">Kārttikeya</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-azvattha" class="skt-link">aśvattha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaararaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumārarāma</i> [<a href="#entry-raama" class="skt-link">rāma</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaararaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Kumārarāma, grand lieu de pélerinage shivaïte à Somarlakota en pays <a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a>, à l'est du delta de la <a href="#entry-Ugodaavarii" class="skt-link">Godāvarī</a>; c'est un <a href="#entry-pa_ncaraamak_setra" class="skt-link">pañcarāmakṣetra</a>; le temple fut construit par le roi <a href="#entry-Ucaalukya" class="skt-link">Cālukya</a> <a href="#entry-Ubhiima_2" class="skt-link">Bhīma_2</a> à la fin du 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukumaarabhiimezvara" class="skt-link">Kumārabhīmeśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaarasambhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumārasambhava</i> [<a href="#entry-sambhava" class="skt-link">sambhava</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaarasambhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Kumārasambhava «Origine de <a href="#entry-Ukumaara" class="skt-link">Kumāra</a>», poème [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] en forme d'épopée lyrique de <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a> composé vers 400; il décrit les événements menant à la naissance de <a href="#entry-Ukumaara" class="skt-link">Kumāra</a>, qui doit naître pour tuer le démon <a href="#entry-Utaaraka" class="skt-link">Tāraka</a>; les dieux demandent à <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> de faire tomber <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> amoureux de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>, fille de <a href="#entry-Uhimavaan" class="skt-link">Himavān</a>, mais la colère de celui-ci pratiquant son ascèse réduisit en cendres celui-là; <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> parvient à ses fins en devenant elle-même ascète, et le poème s'achève par leur union; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvivara_na_1" class="skt-link">Vivaraṇa_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kumaara" class="skt-link">kumāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaaratantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumāratantra</i> [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaaratantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kumāratantra, manuel d'exorcisme pour guérir les enfants possédés par un démon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaaratva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumāratva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaaratva" class="skt-link">n.</a> jeunesse (d'un garçon).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaaravana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumāravana</i> [<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaaravana" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [BhP.] np. du bois Kumāravana «Bois de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>», près du Mont <a href="#entry-Ukailaasa" class="skt-link">Kailāsa</a>; c'est là que <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> et <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> étaient occupés maritalement, lorsqu'un groupe de sages menés par <a href="#entry-Uzunaka" class="skt-link">Śunaka</a> s'y rendit pour honorer <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; surpris dans ses ébats, celui-ci maudit la forêt de tourner en femme tout homme qui y pénétrerait; <a href="#entry-Usudyumna" class="skt-link">Sudyumna</a> vint un jour y chasser, et ainsi redevint la belle <a href="#entry-Ui_daa" class="skt-link">Iḍā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaaravyaakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumāravyākaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vyaakara_na" class="skt-link">vyākaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kumaaravyaakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kumāravyākaraṇa «Grammaire de <a href="#entry-Ukumaara" class="skt-link">Kumāra</a>», œuvre du grammarien bd. <a href="#entry-Uzarvavarmaa" class="skt-link">Śarvavarmā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukaatantra" class="skt-link">Kātantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumaarila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुमारिल</span> <i>kumārila</i> <a href="#entry-kumaarila" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kumārila <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, célèbre maître de <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>; il vécut en Inde du Sud vers l'an 700; on lui doit not. le <a href="#entry-Uzlokavaarttika" class="skt-link">Ślokavārttika</a>, le <a href="#entry-Utantravaarttika" class="skt-link">Tantravārttika</a>, la <a href="#entry-Ub_rha_t_tiikaa" class="skt-link">Bṛhaṭṭīkā</a>, la <a href="#entry-Umadhyama_tiikaa" class="skt-link">Madhyamaṭīkā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-bha_t_tamata" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭamata</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुम्भ</span> <i>kumbha</i> <a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> cruche, pot-à-eau; jarre, vase, urne; syn. <a href="#entry-kalaza" class="skt-link">kalaśa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>kumbharāśi</i>] astr. signe zodiacal [<a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>] du Verseau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bosse frontale de l'éléphant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. vase sacré contenant l'ambroisie [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>] provenant du barattage de la mer de lait [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kumbha «Cruche», fils de <a href="#entry-Ukumbhakar_na" class="skt-link">Kumbhakarṇa</a> — <a href="#entry-kumbhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumbhā</i> prostituée — <a href="#entry-kumbhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumbhī</i> petite cruche; petite jarre, petit pot en terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhaa_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhāṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-a_n_da" class="skt-link">aṇḍa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kumbhaa_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kumbhaa_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kumbhaa_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumbhāṇḍā</i> ventru, pansu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kumbhāṇḍās</i> myth. génies ventrus; syn. <a href="#entry-yak_saas" class="skt-link">yakṣās</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhadhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhadhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhadhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. [«qui porte la jarre»] signe du Verseau; syn. <a href="#entry-kumbharaazi" class="skt-link">kumbharāśi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुम्भक</span> <i>kumbhaka</i> [<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> ifc. pot de, mesure de — <a href="#entry-kumbhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kumbhaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] arrêt de la respiration, la bouche fermée et les narines bloquées par les doigts de la main droite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bosse frontale de l'éléphant — <a href="#entry-kumbhikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumbhikā</i> petite cruche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> potier — <a href="#entry-kumbhakaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumbhakārī</i> femme de potier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhakar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhakarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kar_na" class="skt-link">karṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhakar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de l'ogre-vampire [<a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>] Kumbhakarṇa «Oreille de cruche», fils de <a href="#entry-Uvizravaa" class="skt-link">Viśravā</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukezinii_2" class="skt-link">Keśinī_2</a>, frère de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>, de taille gigantesque et à l'appêtit insatiable; il voulut demander à <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> comme récompense de ses austérités la destruction des dieux [<a href="#entry-nirdevatva" class="skt-link">nirdevatva</a>]; effrayés de sa voracité, les dieux obtinrent que <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī</a> danse sur sa langue lorsqu'il fit sa requête; elle lui fit faire un lapsus et il demanda en fait le sommeil éternel [<i>nidrāvattva</i>]; il ne se réveille qu'un jour tous les 6 mois; <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> lui fit boire 2000 cruches d'alcool pour le réveiller et combattre l'armée de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>; il vainquit <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a>, mais <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> lui coupa la tête; <a href="#entry-Ukumbha" class="skt-link">Kumbha</a> et <a href="#entry-Unikumbha_1" class="skt-link">Nikumbha_1</a> sont ses fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhamela">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhamela</i> [<a href="#entry-mela" class="skt-link">mela</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhamela" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. grand pélerinage ayant lieu sur un cycle de 12 ans, période de Jupiter; il a lieu alternativement à Allahabad [<a href="#entry-prayaaga" class="skt-link">prayāga</a>], Hardwar [<a href="#entry-haridvaara" class="skt-link">haridvāra</a>], Ujjain [<a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>] et Nasik [<a href="#entry-Unaasika" class="skt-link">Nāsika</a>]; myth. ce sont les 4 endroits où tombèrent sur Terre une goutte du <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> qu'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> transportait dans les airs dans le vase sacré [<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbharaazi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbharāśi</i> [<a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbharaazi" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. signe du Verseau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुम्भी</span> <i>kumbhī</i> iic. f. de <a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhiika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhīka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhiika" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. garçon éromène.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhiinasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhīnasa</i> [<a href="#entry-nasa" class="skt-link">nasa</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhiinasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> grand serpent venimeux — <a href="#entry-kumbhiinasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumbhīnasī</i> myth. np. de Kumbhīnasī «aux narines en forme de cruche», épouse du <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> <a href="#entry-Uafgaarapar_na" class="skt-link">Aṅgāraparṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhiipaaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhīpāka</i> [<a href="#entry-paaka" class="skt-link">pāka</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhiipaaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> cuisson dans un pot, nourriture cuite dans un pot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhiipaakanaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhīpākanaraka</i> [<a href="#entry-naraka" class="skt-link">naraka</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhiipaakanaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du Kumbhīpākanaraka «Enfer cuisant», où sont brûlés les damnés au <a href="#entry-Upit_rloka" class="skt-link">Pitṛloka</a>; aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Upit_rtiirtha" class="skt-link">Pitṛtīrtha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kumbhiipaaka" class="skt-link">kumbhīpāka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhiira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumbhīra</i> [<a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhiira" class="skt-link">m.</a> crocodile.<br> <a href="#entry-kumbhii" class="skt-link">kumbhī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुम्भिल</span> <i>kumbhila</i> [<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] <a href="#entry-kumbhila" class="skt-link">m.</a> voleur, cambrioleur; plagiaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> avorton.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumbhin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुम्भिन्</span> <i>kumbhin</i> [<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kumbhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kumbhin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kumbhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumbhinī</i> qui porte une jarre; qui ressemble à un pot — <a href="#entry-kumbhin" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui a une protubérance crânienne»] éléphant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 8 — <a href="#entry-kumbhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumbhinī</i> la Terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumud">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुमुद्</span> <i>kumud</i> [<a href="#entry-kumuda" class="skt-link">kumuda</a>] <a href="#entry-kumud" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Nymphæa esculenta</i>, lotus blanc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumuda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुमुद</span> <i>kumuda</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-mudaa" class="skt-link">mudā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kumuda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kumuda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kumudaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumudā</i> [«qui provoque une telle joie»] réjouissant, agréable — <a href="#entry-kumuda" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Nymphæa esculenta</i>, lotus blanc (fleurissant au lever de la lune) — <a href="#entry-kumuda" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'éléphant céleste Kumuda «Joyeux», gardien [<a href="#entry-diggaja" class="skt-link">diggaja</a>] du Sud-Ouest.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumudavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumudavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kumudavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kumudavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kumudavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumudavatī</i> abondant en lotus blanc — <a href="#entry-kumudavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumudavatī</i> étang couvert de lotus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de la princesse Kumudavatī, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ukuza" class="skt-link">Kuśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumudika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumudika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kumudika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kumudika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kumudikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumudikī</i> abondant en lotus blanc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumudinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumudinī</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-kumudinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> étang couvert de lotus.<br> <a href="#entry-kumuda" class="skt-link">kumuda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kumudvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kumudvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kumudvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kumudvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kumudvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumudvatī</i> abondant en lotus blanc — <a href="#entry-kumudvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kumudvatī</i> étang couvert de lotus.<br> <a href="#entry-kumud" class="skt-link">kumud</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kun_rpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुनृप</span> <i>kunṛpa</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-n_rpa" class="skt-link">nṛpa</a>] <a href="#entry-kun_rpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> mauvais roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kunda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुन्द</span> <i>kunda</i> <a href="#entry-kunda" class="skt-link">m.</a> jasmin (blanc); il fleurit au printemps — <a href="#entry-kunda" class="skt-link">n.</a> fleur de jasmin, très parfumée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kundaavadaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kundāvadāta</i> [<a href="#entry-avadaata" class="skt-link">avadāta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kundaavadaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kundaavadaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kundaavadaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kundāvadātā</i> de la blancheur du jasmin (se dit not. des dents).<br> <a href="#entry-kunda" class="skt-link">kunda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kunta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुन्त</span> <i>kunta</i> <a href="#entry-kunta" class="skt-link">m.</a> flèche, javelot <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>contus</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuntaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuntaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kuntaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Kuntaka, poète du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> du 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur du <a href="#entry-Uvakroktijiivita" class="skt-link">Vakroktijīvita</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kunta" class="skt-link">kunta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuntala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुन्तल</span> <i>kuntala</i> <a href="#entry-kuntala" class="skt-link">m.</a> chevelure; mèche de cheveux — <a href="#entry-kuntala" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. pays ou prince des <a href="#entry-Ukuntalaas" class="skt-link">Kuntalās</a> (ancien nom du <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_ta" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭa</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. géo. np. du peuple des Kuntalās — <a href="#entry-kuntalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuntalā</i> ifc. chevelure de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kunth">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुन्थ्</span> <i>kunth</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kunthati)</i> blesser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> souffrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kunthu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुन्थु</span> <i>kunthu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kunth" class="skt-link">kunth</a>] <a href="#entry-kunthu" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-kunthunaatha" class="skt-link">kunthunātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kunthunaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kunthunātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-kunthunaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Kunthu ou Kunthunātha «Martyr», 17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a>; son corps est doré, son symbole est une chèvre.<br> <a href="#entry-kunthu" class="skt-link">kunthu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kunti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुन्ति</span> <i>kunti</i> <a href="#entry-kunti" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. homme d'un peuple du Nord-Ouest <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>kuntibhoja</i>] myth. np. du prince Kunti <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kuntayas</i> géo. le peuple <a href="#entry-kunti" class="skt-link">kunti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuntibhoja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuntibhoja</i> [<a href="#entry-bhoja" class="skt-link">bhoja</a>] <a href="#entry-kuntibhoja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Kuntibhoja «<a href="#entry-Ukunti" class="skt-link">Kunti</a> le bon vivant», prince <a href="#entry-kunti" class="skt-link">kunti</a> de la dynastie de <a href="#entry-Uyadu" class="skt-link">Yadu</a>; sa mère était la sœur du <a href="#entry-yaadava" class="skt-link">yādava</a> <a href="#entry-Uzuura" class="skt-link">Śūra</a>, qui lui donna à adopter son premier-né, sa fille <a href="#entry-Up_rthaa" class="skt-link">Pṛthā</a>, qui devint <a href="#entry-Ukuntii" class="skt-link">Kuntī</a>; il combattit dans la grande bataille du <a href="#entry-Ukuruk_setra" class="skt-link">Kurukṣetra</a> aux côtés des <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kunti" class="skt-link">kunti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuntii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुन्ती</span> <i>kuntī</i> [<a href="#entry-kunti" class="skt-link">kunti</a>] <a href="#entry-kuntii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Kuntī «(fille adoptive) de <a href="#entry-Ukunti" class="skt-link">Kunti</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Up_rthaa" class="skt-link">Pṛthā</a> fille de <a href="#entry-Uzuura" class="skt-link">Śūra</a>, sœur de <a href="#entry-Uvasudeva" class="skt-link">Vasudeva</a> (et ainsi tante de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>); elle reçut du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-Udurvaasaa" class="skt-link">Durvāsā</a> un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> ayant le pouvoir d'invoquer les dieux pour enfanter; elle l'essaya sur l'heure en invoquant <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>, dont naquit son aîné <a href="#entry-Ukar_na" class="skt-link">Karṇa</a>; plus tard elle devint la 1⟨sup⟩re⟨/sup⟩ épouse du roi <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_du" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍu</a>, qui ne pouvait avoir de rapports sexuels sous peine de mort; elle enfanta de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> par <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>, de <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a> par <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a>, et d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kup">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुप्</span> <i>kup</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(kupyati)</i> pft. <i>(cukopa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kupita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i><i>)</i> s'irriter contre qqn. ⟨g. dat.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être irrité, se mettre en colère — ca. <i>(copayati)</i> irriter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>cupio</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kupita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुपित</span> <i>kupita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kup" class="skt-link">kup</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kupita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kupita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kupitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kupitā</i> irrité, en colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kupuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुपुरुष</span> <i>kupuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-kupuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> mauvais homme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> homme méprisable; lâche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kura_n_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुरण्ट</span> <i>kuraṇṭa</i> var. <i>kuraṇḍa</i> <a href="#entry-kura_n_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Amaranthus caudatus</i>, amarante jaune; elle est considérée de mauvais augure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kura_n_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuraṇṭaka</i> var. <i>kuraṇḍaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kura_n_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. id.<br> <a href="#entry-kura_n_ta" class="skt-link">kuraṇṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kurabaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुरबक</span> <i>kurabaka</i> <a href="#entry-kurabaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Amaranthus cruentus</i>, amarante rouge; on dit qu'elle ne fleurit qu'après qu'une jeune fille ne l'ait regardée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kurafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुरङ्ग</span> <i>kuraṅga</i> <a href="#entry-kurafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kurafgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuraṅgī</i> antilope, daim.<br> <i>kuraṅgāyate</i> se comporter comme une antilope.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुरु</span> <i>kuru</i> <a href="#entry-kuru" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-paurava" class="skt-link">paurava</a> Kuru, roi de la lignée lunaire; il est fils de <a href="#entry-Usa_mvara_na" class="skt-link">Saṃvaraṇa</a> et de <a href="#entry-Utapatii" class="skt-link">Tapatī</a>, ancêtre de <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāṃtanu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. son royaume, le <a href="#entry-kuruvar_sa" class="skt-link">kuruvarṣa</a>, l'un des 10 sous-continents [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] formant le <a href="#entry-jambudviipa" class="skt-link">jambudvīpa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kuravas</i> myth. les Kuravas, peuple antique du Nord de l'Inde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kurujaafgala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kurujāṅgala</i> [<a href="#entry-jaafgala" class="skt-link">jāṅgala</a>] <a href="#entry-kurujaafgala" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du Kurujāṅgala «Pays de <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a>», dans la vallée du Gange; sa capitale était <a href="#entry-Uhastinaapura" class="skt-link">Hastināpura</a> — <a href="#entry-kurujaafgala" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>kurujāṅgalās</i> ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuruk_setra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kurukṣetra</i> [<a href="#entry-k_setra" class="skt-link">kṣetra</a>] <a href="#entry-kuruk_setra" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la vaste plaine du Kurukṣetra, entre les rivières <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_1" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Ud_r_sadvatii" class="skt-link">Dṛṣadvatī</a>, au nord-ouest de Delhi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] territoire des <a href="#entry-Ukuravas" class="skt-link">Kuravas</a>; lieu de la grande bataille entre les <a href="#entry-kauravaas" class="skt-link">kauravās</a> et les <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>.<br> <i>dharmakṣetre</i> <i>kurukṣetre</i> [BhG.] Sur la terre de <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a>, sur la terre du Destin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kurukullaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुरुकुल्ला</span> <i>kurukullā</i> <a href="#entry-kurukullaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. np. de la déesse tantr. Kurukullā.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kurunandana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kurunandana</i> [<a href="#entry-nandana" class="skt-link">nandana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kurunandana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kurunandana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kurunandanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kurunandanā</i> qui fait la joie des <a href="#entry-kauravaas" class="skt-link">kauravās</a> — <a href="#entry-kurunandana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. not. de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuruupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुरूप</span> <i>kurūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuruupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuruupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuruupaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kurūpā</i> mal formé, difforme; laid.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuruvar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuruvarṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-kuruvar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. pays des <a href="#entry-Ukuravas" class="skt-link">Kuravas</a>, l'un des 9 bassins versants [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] du monde.<br> <a href="#entry-kuru" class="skt-link">kuru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuruvinda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुरुविन्द</span> <i>kuruvinda</i> <a href="#entry-kuruvinda" class="skt-link">n.</a> cinabre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sorte de rubis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. corindon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kurvaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kurvāṇa</i> [ppr. md. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kurvaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kurvaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kurvaa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kurvāṇā</i> faisant — <a href="#entry-kurvaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> celui qui fait — <a href="#entry-kurvaa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kurvāṇā</i> celle qui fait.<br> <a href="#entry-kurvat" class="skt-link">kurvat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kurvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुर्वत्</span> <i>kurvat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kurvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kurvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kurvantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kurvantī</i> faisant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faisant le serviteur.<br> <i>aśabdaṃ</i> <i>kurvan</i> en ne faisant pas de bruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusiida">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुसीद</span> <i>kusīda</i> a. <a href="#entry-kusiida" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kusiida" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kusiidaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kusīdā</i> paresseux, inerte — <a href="#entry-kusiida" class="skt-link">n.</a> usure; prêt à intérêt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusiidin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kusīdin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kusiidin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kusiidin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kusiidinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kusīdinī</i> usurier.<br> <a href="#entry-kusiida" class="skt-link">kusīda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kustubha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुस्तुभ</span> <i>kustubha</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_2" class="skt-link">ku_2</a>-<a href="#entry-stambha" class="skt-link">stambha</a>] <a href="#entry-kustubha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kustubha, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «qui soutient la Terre».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kustumburu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुस्तुम्बुरु</span> <i>kustumburu</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-tumburu" class="skt-link">tumburu</a>] <a href="#entry-kustumburu" class="skt-link">m.</a> coriandre — <a href="#entry-kustumburu" class="skt-link">n.</a> graine de coriandre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusuma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुसुम</span> <i>kusuma</i> <a href="#entry-kusuma" class="skt-link">n.</a> fleur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fruit — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(kusumayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kusumita</i><i>)</i> couvrir de fleurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusumaayudha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kusumāyudha</i> [<a href="#entry-aayudha" class="skt-link">āyudha</a>] <a href="#entry-kusumaayudha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kusumāyudha, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> «armé de fleurs».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusumaa_njali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kusumāñjali</i> [<a href="#entry-a_njali" class="skt-link">añjali</a>] <a href="#entry-kusumaa_njali" class="skt-link">m.</a> offrande de fleurs dans le creux des mains jointes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusumabaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kusumabāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-baa_na" class="skt-link">bāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kusumabaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kusumabāṇa, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> «aux flèches de fleurs».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusumamaarga_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kusumamārgaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-maarga_na" class="skt-link">mārgaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kusumamaarga_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kusumamārgaṇa, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> «aux flèches de fleurs».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusumapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kusumapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-kusumapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de Kusumapura, épith. de <a href="#entry-Upaa_taliputra" class="skt-link">Pāṭaliputra</a> «Ville des fleurs».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusumasukumaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kusumasukumāra</i> [<a href="#entry-sukumaara" class="skt-link">sukumāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kusumasukumaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kusumasukumaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kusumasukumaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kusumasukumārā</i> tendre comme une fleur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusumazara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kusumaśara</i> [<a href="#entry-zara" class="skt-link">śara</a>] <a href="#entry-kusumazara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kusumaśara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> «aux flèches de fleurs».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusume_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kusumeṣu</i> [<a href="#entry-i_su" class="skt-link">iṣu</a>] <a href="#entry-kusume_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kusumaśara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> «aux flèches de fleurs».<br> <a href="#entry-kusuma" class="skt-link">kusuma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kusumita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुसुमित</span> <i>kusumita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kusuma" class="skt-link">kusuma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kusumita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kusumita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kusumitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kusumitā</i> en fleurs, couvert de fleurs, fleuri.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुतप</span> <i>kutapa</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-tapa" class="skt-link">tapa</a>] <a href="#entry-kutapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kutapa" class="skt-link">n.</a> couverture faite de poils de chèvre de montagne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुतस्</span> <i>kutas</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a><a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. d'où? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (au fig.) pourquoi? comment? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> par ⟨abl.⟩; introduit un exemple de ce qui précède <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à plus forte raison, bien loin que.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuthumin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुथुमिन्</span> <i>kuthumin</i> <a href="#entry-kuthumin" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du lettré védique Kuthumī ou Kuṭhumi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुत्र</span> <i>kutra</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a><a href="#entry--tra" class="skt-link">-tra</a>] adv. où? vers où?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutracid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kutracid</i> var. <i>kutracit</i> [<a href="#entry-cid_1" class="skt-link">cid_1</a>] adv. quelque part.<br> <a href="#entry-kutra" class="skt-link">kutra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuts">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुत्स्</span> <i>kuts</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(kutsayati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. md. <i>(kutsayate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kutsati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kutsita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>kutsya</i><i>)</i> mépriser; critiquer, blâmer; condamner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutsa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुत्स</span> <i>kutsa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kuts" class="skt-link">kuts</a>] <a href="#entry-kutsa" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Kutsa «Qui blâme», auteur d'hymnes du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a>; il est ami d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, qui le prit un jour sur son char; lorsque le démon <a href="#entry-Uzu_s_na" class="skt-link">Śuṣṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> le défendit et tua le démon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. géo. np. des Kutsās, peuple des descendants de Kutsa.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutsaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुत्सा</span> <i>kutsā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kuts" class="skt-link">kuts</a>] <a href="#entry-kutsaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mépris; blâme, critique.<br> <i>kutsayā</i> i. adv. avec mépris.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutsana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुत्सन</span> <i>kutsana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kuts" class="skt-link">kuts</a>] <a href="#entry-kutsana" class="skt-link">n.</a> mépris; blâme, reproche — <a href="#entry-kutsanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kutsanā</i> expression de mépris.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutsita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुत्सित</span> <i>kutsita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kuts" class="skt-link">kuts</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kutsita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kutsita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kutsitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kutsitā</i> méprisé; blâmé, flétri <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méprisable; vil.<br> <i>kutsite</i> avec une intention de mépris.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuttaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुत्ता</span> <i>kuttā</i> <a href="#entry-kuttaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. contrat de location; bail; viager.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुतुक</span> <i>kutuka</i> [<a href="#entry-kutas" class="skt-link">kutas</a>] <a href="#entry-kutuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> curiosité — ifc. désir pour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutuuhala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुतूहल</span> <i>kutūhala</i> [<a href="#entry-kutas" class="skt-link">kutas</a>-<a href="#entry-hala" class="skt-link">hala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kutuuhala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kutuuhala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kutuuhalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kutūhalā</i> étonnant, surprenant; intéressant, fascinant; amusant; excellent — <a href="#entry-kutuuhala" class="skt-link">n.</a> désir; curiosité, intérêt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutuuhalak_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kutūhalakṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kutuuhalak_rt" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kutuuhalak_rt" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kutuuhalak_rt" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui excite la curiosité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kutuuhalavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kutūhalavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kutuuhalavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kutuuhalavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kutuuhalavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kutūhalavatī</i> curieux, intéressé.<br> <a href="#entry-kutuuhala" class="skt-link">kutūhala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कू</span> <i>kū</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. md. <i>(kuvate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kūta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuu_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूट</span> <i>kūṭa</i> a. <a href="#entry-kuu_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuu_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuu_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kūṭaa</i> faux, menteur, trompeur — <a href="#entry-kuu_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuu_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> pointe, sommet, éminence; front, corne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tas; multitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arch. sanctuaire; cf. <a href="#entry-ekakuu_ta" class="skt-link">ekakūṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-dvikuu_ta" class="skt-link">dvikūṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-trikuu_ta" class="skt-link">trikūṭa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> piège, arme dissimulée; énigme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> illusion, fraude; fausseté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuu_taak_sara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūṭākṣara</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sara" class="skt-link">akṣara</a>] <a href="#entry-kuu_taak_sara" class="skt-link">n.</a> consonnes jointes dans une syllabe; ligature.<br> <a href="#entry-kuu_ta" class="skt-link">kūṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuu_takaaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūṭakāraka</i> [<a href="#entry-kaaraka" class="skt-link">kāraka</a>] <a href="#entry-kuu_takaaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> menteur; faux témoin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuu_talekha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūṭalekha</i> [<a href="#entry-lekha" class="skt-link">lekha</a>] <a href="#entry-kuu_talekha" class="skt-link">m.</a> document falsifié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuu_tastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūṭastha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuu_tastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuu_tastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuu_tasthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kūṭasthā</i> qui est au sommet; à la tête de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. inaltérable, inamovible; uniforme, constant; absolu, suprême — <a href="#entry-kuu_tastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. l'âme, le Soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuubara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूबर</span> <i>kūbara</i> <a href="#entry-kuubara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuubara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuubarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kūbarī</i> timon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूज्</span> <i>kūj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kūjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kūjita</i><i>)</i> crier, piailler, caqueter; fredonner; gazouiller, roucouler; chanter (oiseau) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grogner, gémir; murmurer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूज</span> <i>kūja</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kuuj" class="skt-link">kūj</a>] <a href="#entry-kuuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> gazouillis, roucoulement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gargouillis; murmure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuujana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūjana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-kuujana" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-kuuja" class="skt-link">kūja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuujita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूजित</span> <i>kūjita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kuuj" class="skt-link">kūj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuujita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuujita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuujitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kūjitā</i> rempli de murmures ou de gémissements — <a href="#entry-kuujita" class="skt-link">n.</a> cri d'oiseau; caquet, roucoulement, ramage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूल</span> <i>kūla</i> <a href="#entry-kuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> pente, berge, rive, rivage, bord.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuulafka_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūlaṃkaṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_sa" class="skt-link">kaṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuula_mka_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuula_mka_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuula_mka_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kūlaṃkaṣā</i> [«qui emporte ses rives»] impétueux (torrent).<br> <a href="#entry-kuula" class="skt-link">kūla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूप</span> <i>kūpa</i> <a href="#entry-kuupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> puits, citerne; fontaine.<br> <i>kūpe</i> <i>toyam</i> il y a de l'eau dans le puits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuupaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूपार</span> <i>kūpāra</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-akuupaara" class="skt-link">akūpāra</a>] <a href="#entry-kuupaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> océan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuupama_n_duuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūpamaṇḍūka</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_n_duuka" class="skt-link">maṇḍūka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuupama_n_duuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuupama_n_duuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuupama_n_duukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kūpamaṇḍūkā</i> [«grenouille de puits»] sédentaire, casanier; dont l'horizon est borné; syn. <a href="#entry-udapaanama_n_duuka" class="skt-link">udapānamaṇḍūka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kuupa" class="skt-link">kūpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuurca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूर्च</span> <i>kūrca</i> <a href="#entry-kuurca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuurca" class="skt-link">n.</a> botte, faisceau; gerbe ou poignée d'herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> brosse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> partie du corps humain, membre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> place entre les sourcils; barbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> geste pinçant le bout du medium avec l'extrémité du pouce <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fraude; hypocrisie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuurcaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūrcaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-kuurcaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> brosse, pinceau; crayon; brosse à dents.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuurcin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūrcin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuurcin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuurcin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuurcinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kūrcinī</i> barbu.<br> <a href="#entry-kuurca" class="skt-link">kūrca</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuurma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूर्म</span> <i>kūrma</i> <a href="#entry-kuurma" class="skt-link">m.</a> tortue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Kūrma «Tortue», 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, qui soutint l'axe de la baratte lors du barattage de la mer de lait [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uakuupaara" class="skt-link">Akūpāra</a>, <a href="#entry-Umandara" class="skt-link">Mandara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaasuki" class="skt-link">Vāsuki</a> — <a href="#entry-kuurmii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kūrmī</i> tortue femelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuurmapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kūrmapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-kuurmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Kūrmapurāṇa ou Kaurmapurāṇa, texte mythologique tantrique.<br> <a href="#entry-kuurma" class="skt-link">kūrma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuvala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुवल</span> <i>kuvala</i> <a href="#entry-kuvala" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Nymphæa nelumbo</i>, lotus bleu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuvalaazva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuvalāśva</i> [<a href="#entry-azva" class="skt-link">aśva</a>] <a href="#entry-kuvalaazva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi Kuvalāśva ou Kuvalayāśva «Au cheval bleu», fils de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhadazva" class="skt-link">Bṛhadaśva</a>; il tua l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Udhundhu" class="skt-link">Dhundhu</a> [<a href="#entry-Udhundhumaara" class="skt-link">Dhundhumāra</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-kuvala" class="skt-link">kuvala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuvalaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuvalaya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-kuvalaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Nymphæa nelumbo</i>, lotus bleu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuvalayaananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuvalayānanda</i> [<a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>] <a href="#entry-kuvalayaananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Kuvalayānanda «Joie du lotus bleu», traité de rhétorique d'Appayya <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuvera">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुवेर</span> <i>kuvera</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-vera" class="skt-link">vera</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuvera" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuvera" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuveraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuverā</i> difforme, monstrueux — <a href="#entry-kuvera" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kuvera «Difforme», forme d'origine du nom plus tardif <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuvid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुविद्</span> <i>kuvid</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-id" class="skt-link">id</a>] part. véd. (interrogation) si? est-ce que? où donc?.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuvitsa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuvitsa</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_2" class="skt-link">sa_2</a>] <a href="#entry-kuvitsa" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. quiconque; un inconnu.<br> <a href="#entry-kuvid" class="skt-link">kuvid</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुश</span> <i>kuśa</i> <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Desmostachya bipinnata</i>, pâturin; fourrage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. herbe sacrée utilisée dans les cérémonies religieuses dès l'âge védique, et dont on fait des cordes; cf. <a href="#entry-darbha" class="skt-link">darbha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Kuśa, fils du roi <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, jumeau de <a href="#entry-Ulava" class="skt-link">Lava</a>; à la mort de son père il devint roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a>; son épouse était la princesse <a href="#entry-Ukumudavatii" class="skt-link">Kumudavatī</a>; il fut tué en combattant l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Udurjaya" class="skt-link">Durjaya</a>; [Ragh.] son fils <a href="#entry-Uatithi_1" class="skt-link">Atithi_1</a> lui succéda <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-raajar_si" class="skt-link">rājarṣi</a> Kuśa_1, fils spirituel de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>; ses fils par <a href="#entry-Uvaidarbhii" class="skt-link">Vaidarbhī</a> sont <a href="#entry-Ukuzaamba" class="skt-link">Kuśāmba</a>, qui fonda la ville de <a href="#entry-Ukauzaambii" class="skt-link">Kauśāmbī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukuzanaabha" class="skt-link">Kuśanābha</a>, qui fonda la ville de <a href="#entry-Umahodayaa" class="skt-link">Mahodayā</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukanyaakubjaa" class="skt-link">Kanyākubjā</a> <a href="#entry-Uamuurtarajasa" class="skt-link">Amūrtarajasa</a> qui fonda la ville de <a href="#entry-Udharmaara_nya" class="skt-link">Dharmāraṇya</a>, et <a href="#entry-Uvasu_3" class="skt-link">Vasu_3</a> qui fonda la ville de <a href="#entry-Ugirivraja" class="skt-link">Girivraja</a>-<a href="#entry-Uraajag_rha" class="skt-link">Rājagṛha</a> — <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">n.</a> eau — <a href="#entry-kuzaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśā</i> aiguille de bois ou de métal, servant de marqueur de récitation — <a href="#entry-kuzii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśī</i> soc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzaagra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśāgra</i> [<a href="#entry-agra" class="skt-link">agra</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzaagra" class="skt-link">n.</a> pointe coupante de l'herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a> — a. <a href="#entry-kuzaagra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzaagra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzaagraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśāgrā</i> coupant; pénétrant; intelligent — <a href="#entry-kuzaagra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a> Kuśāgra «l'Intelligent», fils de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhadratha" class="skt-link">Bṛhadratha</a>, frère de <a href="#entry-Ujaraasandha" class="skt-link">Jarāsandha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzaagrabuddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśāgrabuddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-buddhi" class="skt-link">buddhi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuzaagrabuddhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzaagrabuddhi" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzaagrabuddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> à l'esprit acéré; intelligent, astucieux — <a href="#entry-kuzaagrabuddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> intelligence; astuce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzaagriiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśāgrīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuzaagriiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzaagriiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzaagriiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśāgrīyā</i> aiguisé comme l'herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a>; pénétrant.<br> <a href="#entry-kuzaagra" class="skt-link">kuśāgra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzaamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुशाम्ब</span> <i>kuśāmba</i> <a href="#entry-kuzaamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi Kuśāmba, fils de <a href="#entry-Ukuza_1" class="skt-link">Kuśa_1</a>; il fonda la ville de <a href="#entry-Ukauzaambii" class="skt-link">Kauśāmbī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-aasana" class="skt-link">āsana</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. petite natte d'herbe sacrée, utilisée pour la méditation.<br> <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzaavarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśāvarta</i> [<a href="#entry-aavarta" class="skt-link">āvarta</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzaavarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. d'un gué sur le Gange <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a> Kuśāvarta <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>kuśāvartās</i> ses descendants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzaazva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśāśva</i> [<a href="#entry-azva" class="skt-link">aśva</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzaazva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi de <a href="#entry-Uvizaalaa" class="skt-link">Viśālā</a> Kuśāśva, fils de <a href="#entry-Usahadeva_2" class="skt-link">Sahadeva_2</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Usomadatta_1" class="skt-link">Somadatta_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzadhvaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśadhvaja</i> [<a href="#entry-dhvaja" class="skt-link">dhvaja</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzadhvaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du sage Kuśadhvaja «Qui a l'herbe sacrée comme emblème», fils de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>; sa fille est la très belle <a href="#entry-Uvedavatii" class="skt-link">Vedavatī</a>, qu'il ne voulut pas marier à quiconque sauf <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; le <a href="#entry-daitya" class="skt-link">daitya</a> <a href="#entry-Uzambhu_1" class="skt-link">Śambhu_1</a>, furieux de ne pouvoir épouser celle-ci, le tua; son épouse se jeta sur son bûcher, et sa fille se réfugia comme ascète dans une forêt, où elle fut agressée par <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Kuśadhvaja_1 <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Uhrasvaromaa" class="skt-link">Hrasvaromā</a>; après la victoire de son frère aîné <a href="#entry-Ujanakaraaja" class="skt-link">Janakarāja</a> sur le roi <a href="#entry-Usudhanvaa_1" class="skt-link">Sudhanvā_1</a> qui avait attaqué <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a>, il obtint son trône et devint roi de <a href="#entry-Usafkaazyaa" class="skt-link">Saṃkāśyā</a>; il avait deux filles, <a href="#entry-Umaa_n_davii" class="skt-link">Māṇḍavī</a> qui épousa <a href="#entry-Ubharata_1" class="skt-link">Bharata_1</a>, et <a href="#entry-Uzrutakiirti_1" class="skt-link">Śrutakīrti_1</a> qui épousa <a href="#entry-Uzatrughna" class="skt-link">Śatrughna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzadviipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśadvīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzadviipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. l'un des 7 continents [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>], entouré d'une mer de <a href="#entry-gh_rta" class="skt-link">ghṛta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुशल</span> <i>kuśala</i> a. <a href="#entry-kuzala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśalā</i> bon, juste, approprié à, convenable pour ⟨g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sain, prospère, en bonne santé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> capable, habile, adroit; expert, instruit — <a href="#entry-kuzala" class="skt-link">n.</a> bien-être, prospérité, bonne santé.<br> <i>kuśalam</i> adr. bien à vous.<br> <i>kuśalam</i> <i>te</i> comment te portes-tu?<br> <i>kuśalam</i> <i>asti</i> <i>vā</i> var. <i>kuśalamvā</i> comment allez-vous?<br> <i>...</i> <i>api</i> <i>kuśalam</i> comment se porte ... ⟨g.⟩?<br> <i>kuśalam</i> <i>iva</i> ça va.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzalava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśalava</i> <a href="#entry-kuzalava" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>kuśalavau</i> myth. np. des Kuśalavau, fils jumeaux de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> <a href="#entry-Ukuza" class="skt-link">Kuśa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ulava" class="skt-link">Lava</a> «Fourrage et Froment»; il furent élevés par <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a>, qui leur apprit à réciter son <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rāmāyaṇa</a> en public; ils sont donc aussi <a href="#entry-Ukuziilavau" class="skt-link">Kuśīlavau</a> «les deux Bardes».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśalin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuzalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzalin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśalinī</i> qui est prospère, qui se porte bien.<br> <a href="#entry-kuzala" class="skt-link">kuśala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzanaabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśanābha</i> [<a href="#entry-naabhi" class="skt-link">nābhi</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzanaabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi Kuśanābha, fils de <a href="#entry-Ukuza_1" class="skt-link">Kuśa_1</a>; il fonda la ville de <a href="#entry-Ukanyaakubjaa" class="skt-link">Kanyākubjā</a> la prospère [<a href="#entry-Umahodayaa" class="skt-link">Mahodayā</a>]; il eut 100 filles très belles de la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] <a href="#entry-Ugh_rtaacii" class="skt-link">Ghṛtācī</a>; elles furent rendues bossues par <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a> pour n'avoir pas cédé à ses désirs; il les maria au roi <a href="#entry-Ubrahmadatta" class="skt-link">Brahmadatta</a>, et elles retrouvèrent leur beauté; il fit alors un sacrifice pour obtenir un fils pareil à <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, et <a href="#entry-Ugaadhi" class="skt-link">Gādhi</a> lui naquit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzasthala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśasthala</i> [<a href="#entry-sthala" class="skt-link">sthala</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzasthala" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de Kuśasthala «où l'on trouve l'herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a>», épith. de la ville de <a href="#entry-Ukaanyakubja" class="skt-link">Kānyakubja</a> — <a href="#entry-kuzasthalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśasthalī</i> myth. np. de Kuśasthalī, épith. de la ville de <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuze">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुशे</span> <i>kuśe</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a>] ind. dans l'herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzezaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśeśaya</i> [<a href="#entry-zaya" class="skt-link">śaya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuzezaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzezaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzezayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśeśayā</i> couché dans l'herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a> — <a href="#entry-kuzezaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lotus [«étendu sur l'eau»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzezayamaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśeśayamaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuzezayamaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzezayamaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzezayamayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśeśayamayī</i> fait de lotus.<br> <a href="#entry-kuzezaya" class="skt-link">kuśeśaya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-kuze" class="skt-link">kuśe</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुशि</span> <i>kuśi</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-kuzin" class="skt-link">kuśin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuziida">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुशीद</span> <i>kuśīda</i> <a href="#entry-kuziida" class="skt-link">n.</a> prêt à intérêts.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuziidika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśīdika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuziidika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuziidika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuziidikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśīdikī</i> usurier.<br> <a href="#entry-kuziida" class="skt-link">kuśīda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuziila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुशील</span> <i>kuśīla</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_1" class="skt-link">ku_1</a>-<a href="#entry-ziila" class="skt-link">śīla</a>] <a href="#entry-kuziila" class="skt-link">n.</a> mauvaise conduite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuziilava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśīlava</i> [<a href="#entry--va" class="skt-link">-va</a>] <a href="#entry-kuziilava" class="skt-link">m.</a> barde, hérault; mime, acteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de Kuśīlava, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a> «le Barde» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>kuśīlavau</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Kuśīlavau «les deux Bardes», épith. des deux fils jumeaux de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukuza" class="skt-link">Kuśa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ulava" class="skt-link">Lava</a> (jeu de mots sur <a href="#entry-Ukuzalavau" class="skt-link">Kuśalavau</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-kuziila" class="skt-link">kuśīla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुशिक</span> <i>kuśika</i> a. <a href="#entry-kuzika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśikā</i> affecté de strabisme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi <a href="#entry-Upaurava" class="skt-link">Paurava</a> Kuśika «Qui louche», fils de <a href="#entry-Ubalaakaazva" class="skt-link">Balākāśva</a>, descendant de <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a>; il fit pénitence pour obtenir un fils invincible, l'égal d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; touché par ses dévotions, <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> s'incarna en son fils <a href="#entry-Ugaadhi" class="skt-link">Gādhi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] «fils de <a href="#entry-Ukuza_1" class="skt-link">Kuśa_1</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukuzanaabha" class="skt-link">Kuśanābha</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Ugaadhi" class="skt-link">Gādhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzikaputra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśikaputra</i> [<a href="#entry-putra" class="skt-link">putra</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzikaputra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] «descendant de Kuśika», patr. de <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-kuzika" class="skt-link">kuśika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुशिन्</span> <i>kuśin</i> [<a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kuzin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kuzinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśinī</i> riche en herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a> — <a href="#entry-kuzin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Kuśī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukuziilavau" class="skt-link">Kuśīlavau</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kuzinagara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuśinagara</i> [<a href="#entry-nagara" class="skt-link">nagara</a>] <a href="#entry-kuzinagara" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Kuśinagara, capitale de la dynastie <a href="#entry-Umalla" class="skt-link">Malla</a>; mod. Kasia, à l'est du mod. Uttar Pradesh; <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> y mourut; c'est un grand lieu de pélerinage bd. — <a href="#entry-kuzinagarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuśinagarī</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-kuzi" class="skt-link">kuśi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_nc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुञ्च्</span> <i>kuñc</i> cf. <a href="#entry-kuc" class="skt-link">kuc</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_ncaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुञ्चक</span> <i>kuñcaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-kuc" class="skt-link">kuc</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_ncaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_ncaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_ncikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuñcikā</i> qui courbe — <a href="#entry-ku_ncikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuñcikā</i> clé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la Kuñcikā, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Uvaiyaakara_nasiddhaantama_njuu_saa" class="skt-link">Vaiyākaraṇasiddhāntamañjūṣā</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Udurbalaacaarya" class="skt-link">Durbalācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_ncana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
