    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुञ्चन</span> <i>kuñcana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kuc" class="skt-link">kuc</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_ncana" class="skt-link">n.</a> courbure, contraction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_nci">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुञ्चि</span> <i>kuñci</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kuc" class="skt-link">kuc</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_nci" class="skt-link">f.</a> mesure de volume de grain, égale à 8 poignées.<br> <i>aṣṭamuṣṭirbhavetkuñciḥ</i> <i>kuñcayo'ṣṭau</i> <i>tu</i> <i>puṣkalam</i> <i>puṣkalāni</i> <i>ca</i> <i>catvāri</i> <i>āḍhakaḥ</i> <i>parikīrtitaḥ</i> Il est statué que la <a href="#entry-ku_nci" class="skt-link">kuñci</a> doit faire 8 poignées, le <a href="#entry-pu_skala" class="skt-link">puṣkala</a> 8 <a href="#entry-ku_nci" class="skt-link">kuñci</a> de même, et que 4 <a href="#entry-pu_skala" class="skt-link">puṣkala</a> font un <a href="#entry-aa_dhaka" class="skt-link">āḍhaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_ncita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुञ्चित</span> <i>kuñcita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kuc" class="skt-link">kuc</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_ncita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_ncita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_ncitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kuñcitā</i> contracté, resserré; courbé, tordu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_ncitaafghri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuñcitāṅghri</i> [<a href="#entry-afghri" class="skt-link">aṅghri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ku_ncitaafghri" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_ncitaafghri" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ku_ncitaafghri" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a le pied courbé; se dit not. de <a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a>.<br> <i>kuñcitāṅghriṃ</i> <i>bhaje</i> J'adore (le Seigneur <a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a>) au pied courbé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_ncitaafghristava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kuñcitāṅghristava</i> [<a href="#entry-stava" class="skt-link">stava</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_ncitaafghristava" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Kuñcitāṅghristava «Hymne de gloire au pied courbé (de <a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a>)», poème dévotionnel d'Umāpati Śivācārya (vers 1300).<br> <a href="#entry-ku_ncitaafghri" class="skt-link">kuñcitāṅghri</a> <br> <a href="#entry-ku_ncita" class="skt-link">kuñcita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_nja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुञ्ज</span> <i>kuñja</i> <a href="#entry-ku_nja" class="skt-link">m.</a> buissons, taillis, hallier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ku_njara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुञ्जर</span> <i>kuñjara</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_nja" class="skt-link">kuñja</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-ku_njara" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant — ifc. trésor; cf. <a href="#entry-navaku_njara" class="skt-link">navakuñjara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्व</span> <i>kva</i> adv. où? en quel lieu? en quel état? à quel degré?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvaapi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kvāpi</i> [<a href="#entry-api" class="skt-link">api</a>] adv. n'importe où.<br> <a href="#entry-kva" class="skt-link">kva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvaath">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्वाथ्</span> <i>kvāth</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-kvath" class="skt-link">kvath</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(kvāthayati)</i> faire cuire; préparer une décoction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्वाथ</span> <i>kvātha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-kvath" class="skt-link">kvath</a>] <a href="#entry-kvaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> cuisson, ébullition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kvaca</i> [<a href="#entry-ca" class="skt-link">ca</a>] adv. (après négation) nulle part; jamais.<br> <i>yatra</i> <i>kvaca</i> n'importe où; n'importe quand.<br> <i>yatra</i> <i>kvacana</i> id.<br> <i>kvaca</i> <i>...</i> <i>kvaca</i> il faut choisir entre ... et.<br> <i>kva</i> <i>tapaḥ</i> <i>kvaca</i> <i>ruditam</i> On ne peut pas être ascète et pleurer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvacid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kvacid</i> [<a href="#entry-cid_1" class="skt-link">cid_1</a>] adv. quelque part; parfois.<br> <i>na</i> <i>kvacid</i> adv. nulle part; jamais.<br> <i>kvacid</i> <i>...</i> <i>kvacid</i> tantôt ... tantôt; ça et là, diversement.<br> <i>yad</i> <i>iha</i> <i>asti</i> <i>tad</i> <i>anyatra</i> <i>yan</i> <i>neha</i> <i>asti</i> <i>na</i> <i>tat</i> <i>kvacit</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Ce qui est ici se retrouve ailleurs, ce qui n'est pas ici ne se trouve nulle part.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvasu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्वसु</span> <i>kvasu</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] des ppft. comme <a href="#entry-vidvas" class="skt-link">vidvas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvasvid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>kvasvid</i> [<a href="#entry-svid_1" class="skt-link">svid_1</a>] adv. n'importe où.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvath">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्वथ्</span> <i>kvath</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(kvathati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>kvathita</i><i>)</i> cuire, bouillir — ca. <i>(kvāthayati)</i> faire cuire; préparer une décoction, une infusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvathita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्वथित</span> <i>kvathita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-kvath" class="skt-link">kvath</a>] a. <a href="#entry-kvathita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-kvathita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-kvathitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>kvathitā</i> cuit, bouilli; chaud (par cuisson) — <a href="#entry-kvathita" class="skt-link">n.</a> hydromel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-kvip">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्विप्</span> <i>kvip</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>]; forme des agents ifc. à partir de la racine.
  </div>
</div>
